*Qiming Shi,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Tianjun Pan,Shaokang Fu,Chengyu Wang,Siyue Li,Yaping Cheng,Di Weng,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MerchantBench是一个基于真实电商数据的365天订单级模拟基准，用于评估LLM智能体的长期连贯性。通过商品采购、定价、现金流管理和混合延迟反馈等任务，测试8种LLM在48次运行中的表现。结果显示，最佳LLM配置仅达到人类参与者平均净资产的27.3%，揭示了当前模型在长期自主决策中的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估主要集中在有明确终止条件的受限任务上，忽视了真实场景中跨长时间跨度保持目标一致行为（即"长期连贯性"）的能力评估。真实部署需要智能体在累积证据的基础上持续做出相互依赖的决策，而缺乏对这类能力的基准测试。

Method: 研究者构建了MerchantBench模拟环境：基于98,843条真实电商产品记录，模拟365天订单级运营。提供26种工具供智能体交互，涵盖产品采购、上架与定价控制、现金流管理和混合延迟反馈适应四大核心任务。环境设计上将即时可观察的上游供应商事件与延迟的下游订单结果相耦合，要求智能体追踪单个订单生命周期并重新审视早期决策。对8种LLM在两种智能体框架下进行48次运行评估，每次运行覆盖365个模拟日。

Result: 实验揭示了最新LLM与人类参与者之间的巨大差距。表现最佳的LLM配置仅获得人类参与者平均最终净资产的27.3%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在需要长期连贯性的自主决策任务中仍远远落后于人类，凸显了开发能够在持续环境中跨时间保持目标一致性、适应延迟反馈并管理相互依赖决策的智能体系统的迫切需求。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly evaluated as autonomous tool users, yet most benchmarks focus on bounded tasks with immediate success criteria. Real-world deployments often require Long-Term Coherence, the capacity to preserve purposeful behavior across extended horizons while adapting decisions to accumulated evidence. Evaluating this capacity requires a persistent environment in which actions constrain future choices, feedback arrives at heterogeneous delays, and incoherent behavior produces measurable cumulative effects. Seller-side e-commerce provides a suitable setting for this evaluation through recurrent and interdependent decisions over Product Sourcing, Listing and Pricing Control, Cash-Flow Management, and Mixed-Latency Feedback Adaptation. We introduce MerchantBench, a 365-day order-level simulation grounded in 98,843 real e-commerce product records and equipped with 26 tools for agent interaction. MerchantBench couples promptly observable Upstream Supplier Events with delayed Downstream Order Outcomes, requiring agents to follow individual order lifecycles and revisit earlier decisions. We evaluate eight LLMs under two agent frameworks in 48 runs, each spanning 365 simulated days. Our results reveal a substantial gap between even the latest LLMs and human participants, with the best LLM configuration attaining only 27.3\% of the mean final net assets achieved by human participants.

</details>


### [149] [Scaling Scientific Discovery Environments for Turn-Level Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28990)
*Yucheng Xu,Keyi Zhang,Yuyang Yu,Min Zhang,Shiyuan Meng,Pei Chu,Zhongying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciDisco 是一个可扩展的科学发现智能体训练框架，通过构建过程可验证的环境（SciThèque）、基于DAG的轨迹合成以及DiscoPO训练方法，使14B模型在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型智能体在科学发现中的长期规划能力受限于缺乏面向真实科学数据的过程监督环境。解决这一问题需要构建可验证中间分析步骤的训练框架。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心组件：(1) SciThèque——将假设、数据集、隐藏证据图和验证器编译为可检查分析进展的任务环境；(2) DAG-grounded trajectory synthesis——利用这些环境构造经过验证器筛选的多轮交互示范；(3) DiscoPO——以环境为训练信号源，对产生可验证分析证据的动作进行回合级信用分配。

Result: SciDisco-14B 在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准上达到了最先进（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入过程可验证环境与回合级信用分配机制，SciDisco 有效提升了科学发现智能体的长周期分析能力，为LLM驱动科学发现提供了可扩展的训练范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown promising capabilities in data-driven scientific discovery tasks, where an agent interacts with an execution environment and produces a statistical claim. Long-horizon scientific analysis remains constrained by the lack of process supervised environments over real-world scientific data. This paper introduces SciDisco, a scalable framework for training Scientific Discovery agents in process-verifiable environments. SciThèque compiles hypotheses, datasets, hidden evidence graphs, and verifiers into task environments where analytical progress can be checked during interaction. DAG-grounded trajectory synthesis uses these environments to construct verifier-filtered multi-turn demonstrations. DiscoPO then uses the environment as the source of training signal, assigning turn-level credit to actions that produce verifiable analytical evidence. Experiments show that SciDisco-14B reaches state-of-the-art on hypothesis-driven scientific data analysis benchmarks.

</details>


### [150] [MMShopBench: A Real-Log Benchmark for Multimodal, Multi-Turn Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29002)
*Zeying Hao,Hao Guo,Mengtao Xu,Yimin Hu,Yuheng Song,Zesheng Zhou,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MMShopBench 是首个基于真实购物日志的多模态多轮购物智能体基准，要求模型从用户图像和多轮对话中联合推理购买意图与强制需求，通过图像/文本搜索检索候选产品，并基于产品图像和结构化属性验证候选品是否满足所有需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有购物智能体评测基准主要依赖纯文本或合成请求，无法真实反映用户通过图像和多轮对话联合表达复杂购物需求的现实场景，缺乏基于真实数据的多模态多轮评测。

Method: 从清洗和人工标注的真实购物日志构建 MMShopBench，提供每个请求的购买意图和强制产品需求的 ground-truth 标注。设计证据驱动的多模态评测协议，评估智能体在联合理解用户图像与多轮对话、图像/文本检索、基于产品图像和结构化属性进行需求验证的能力。同时构建配套训练集用于开源模型微调，并搭建离线购物沙盒环境以支持可复现实验。

Result: 对代表性开源和闭源模型进行评测，微调后的开源模型与领先闭源模型之间的性能差距大幅缩小，验证了所构建训练数据的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于真实日志的多模态多轮评测能更准确反映购物智能体的实际能力，高质量的领域训练数据可有效提升开源模型表现，离线沙盒环境保障了实验的可复现性。

Abstract: Online shoppers increasingly turn to AI shopping assistants, using images and multi-turn dialogue to express and refine product needs that are difficult to articulate in text alone. However, existing benchmarks largely rely on text-only or synthetic requests, underrepresenting complex real-world shopping requirements jointly expressed through images and language. We introduce MMShopBench, the first real-log benchmark for multimodal, multi-turn shopping agents. Built from carefully cleaned and manually annotated shopping logs, MMShopBench provides ground-truth annotations of each request's purchase intent and mandatory product requirements. Agents must infer these requirements jointly from user images and multi-turn dialogue, retrieve candidate products through image and text search, and verify that each candidate satisfies all requirements using its product images and structured attributes. We evaluate representative open-source and proprietary models using an evidence-grounded multimodal protocol and construct a companion training set for fine-tuning an open-source model. To ensure reproducible experimentation, we build an offline shopping sandbox, where fine-tuning substantially narrows the performance gap between our open-source model and leading proprietary models, demonstrating the effectiveness of our training data.

</details>


### [151] [Evidence-Grounded Constraint Checking in Construction Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29058)
*Rashid Mushkani,Hugo Berard,Shin Koseki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于专业文档审查的证据驱动流水线，通过四状态规则引擎处理事实约束检查。在29个建筑项目的160项任务上评估PDF证据分配策略，发现将图像预算从页面概览重新分配给一页概览加三块重叠瓦片（Region-RAG）可使决策准确率提升10.6个百分点，但此效应在更广泛的测试中不持续，揭示了分辨率-广度权衡而非区域聚焦的普遍优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业文档审查是约束检查问题，决策依赖于文本、几何、页面及文档版本之间的关系。现有方法在证据分配（如将有限的图像预算用于页面概览还是区域聚焦）上缺乏系统性评估，需要确定基于区域的证据检索（Region-RAG）是否优于传统的页面级别方法。

Method: 提出一条证据驱动的流水线：首先对提取的事实进行规范化处理，然后使用确定性四状态规则引擎执行约束检查，保留源文本跨度（source spans），并对未解决案例进行升级处理。在PDF证据分配器的评估中，采用重复四系统测试和不相交双系统广度扩展：在重复测试中将四图像预算从检索到的页面概览重新分配为一页概览加三块重叠瓦片；在广度扩展中比较Region-RAG与页面级别方法。

Result: 在重复测试中，Region-RAG使项目族标准化决策准确率提升10.6个百分点（95%项目聚类bootstrap CI: 4.3至18.0；精确p=0.031）。但在更广泛的测试中，Region-RAG的准确率变化为-4.1个百分点（95% CI: -10.2至1.9；p=0.209），等图像灵敏度分析倾向于页面广度。精确发现集召回率仍然较低，假通过（false passes）常见，重复运行一致性校准不佳。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了分辨率与广度之间的权衡关系，而非区域聚焦证据的普遍优势，这促使未来研究应关注规则感知的证据路由和专家审查机制。

Abstract: Professional-document review is a constraint-checking problem in which decisions depend on relations among text, geometry, pages, and document revisions. We present an evidence-grounded pipeline that normalizes extracted facts, executes four-state rules deterministically, retains source spans, and escalates unresolved cases. We evaluate its PDF evidence allocator on 160 reference-based tasks from 29 construction projects using a repeated four-system test and a disjoint two-system breadth extension. In the repeated test, reallocating a four-image budget from retrieved page overviews to one overview and three overlapping tiles improves project-family standardized decision accuracy by 10.6 percentage points (95% project-cluster bootstrap CI: 4.3 to 18.0; exact p = 0.031). This effect does not persist in the broader block: Region-RAG changes accuracy by -4.1 points (95% CI: -10.2 to 1.9; exact p = 0.209), while an equal-image sensitivity favors page breadth. Exact finding-set recovery remains low, false passes remain common, and repeated-run agreement is poorly calibrated. The results identify a resolution-breadth trade-off rather than a universal advantage for region-focused evidence, motivating rule-aware evidence routing and expert review.

</details>


### [152] [On the Generalization of Steering Vectors for Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29062)
*Matthew Nguyen,Kyle Cox,Austin Meek,Iván Arcuschin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了思维链（CoT）的忠实性问题——模型在推理过程中未口头承认重要步骤（如受暗示性线索影响却不承认）。作者扩展了激活引导（activation steering）方法，在三种模型上测试其跨线索类型、跨数据集和跨向量构建方法的泛化能力。结果表明：引导仅在最大模型上可靠提升线索承认率；引导效果主要由评估环境决定，而非训练设置；四种向量构建方法效果相似；引导减少了隐藏的线索使用，而非增加线索使用本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理的忠实性对AI安全至关重要——若模型在CoT中遗漏了关键推理步骤（如受误导性线索影响却不承认），则监控推理过程将失去意义。现有激活引导方法虽能改善忠实性，但其泛化性（跨线索、跨数据集、跨构建方法）尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在带线索的问答设置中，对三种模型（Gemma-3 4B、Qwen-3.5 9B、Gemma-3 12B）使用激活引导来提升CoT忠实性。系统考察了：(1) 跨线索类型的泛化；(2) 跨数据集的泛化；(3) 四种不同引导向量构建方法的比较（含一种优化目标不涉及特定线索的方法）。还测试了引导是否改变了线索的实际使用率，以区分"提升口头承认"与"提升线索显著性"两种机制。

Result: 引导仅在Gemma-3 12B上可靠提升线索承认率；当引导有效时，效果在跨线索和跨数据集分析中具有广泛泛化性——效应量主要由评估环境而非训练环境决定；四种向量构建方法产生相似的效应量；引导并未增加线索使用率，而是减少了隐藏的线索使用（即使用线索但不承认的行为）。

Conclusion: 激活引导对CoT忠实性的改善在最大模型上有效且泛化性强，但其作用机制是抑制隐藏的线索使用（促进诚实披露），而非增加线索依赖本身。引导向量的构建方式对效果影响很小，这暗示该方法具有良好的鲁棒性和实用前景。

Abstract: Model capabilities have improved in large part due to scaling chain of thought. This has been a promising development for AI safety--where models verbalize their reasoning, it is possible to monitor it. However, in some cases, models do not verbalize important steps in their reasoning process. For example, models prompted with a cue suggesting the incorrect answer may fail to acknowledge that cue, even when it appears instrumental to their conclusion. When chain of thought (CoT) fails to disclose instrumental reasoning steps, we describe it as unfaithful. Prior work has shown that activation steering can be a useful method to improve faithfulness in CoT. We extend this line of work by studying how well steering for faithfulness generalizes across cue types, datasets, and methods of constructing the steering vector for three models (Gemma-3 4B, Qwen-3.5 9B, Gemma-3 12B) in a cued question-answering setting. While steering reliably increases cue acknowledgment for only the largest model (Gemma-3 12B), we find that when steering is effective, its effect generalizes broadly across cue types and datasets--in cross-cue and cross-dataset analyses, effect size is determined primarily by the evaluation setting, rather than the vector's train setting. How the vector is built also matters little--four construction methods, including one whose optimization target mentions no specific cue, yield similar effect sizes. Finally, we consider the possibility that steering promotes the salience of the cue and causes greater cue use, rather than targeting verbalization behaviors. However, we find no evidence for this--steering leaves the rate of cue use roughly unchanged while reducing hidden cue use, i.e., cue use that is not acknowledged.

</details>


### [153] [Harnessing the Wisdom of LLM Crowds through Complementarity-Driven Iterative Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29087)
*Yanbin Fang,Xuan Wei,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WILC框架将多个大语言模型组织成接力式的顺序协作流程，通过"互补性驱动的模型选择"机制（包含前瞻互补适配和后验互补增益双门控），动态选择后继模型来解决前序输出的瓶颈，在四个基准测试上以远低于GPT-5.2的成本达到同等性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一LLM存在模型特定的能力边界，而现有的多模型组合方法（如集成、路由）均采用固定策略，忽视了复杂问题求解中互补性是动态的、状态依赖的。作者希望借鉴"群体智慧"范式，将集体LLM智能重新概念化为顺序的、基于瓶颈的互补过程。

Method: 提出WILC框架，包含两大设计原则：(1) 迭代反思与精炼——建立状态保持的工作流，使模型能够诊断并精炼前序输出；(2) 互补性驱动的模型选择——通过双门控机制运作：前瞻互补适配度(PCF)识别最适合当前瓶颈的工作模型，后验互补增益(PCG)评估所选过渡是否确实改进了演化中的解。

Result: 在四个多样化基准测试上，WILC优于单一模型自精炼、集成方法和查询路由方法。在标准化定价假设下，WILC以约7倍低的每查询估计成本，匹配了GPT-5.2的平均基准性能，同时支持自托管部署以实现数据主权。

Conclusion: 本研究将群体智慧理论从静态聚合拓展到顺序化AI互补，为多AI协调提供了可迁移的设计原则，展示了通过战略性地协调异构LLM可以释放超越任何单一模型的集体智能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in enterprise settings, yet individual models remain bounded by model-specific capability limitations. These heterogeneous boundaries pose a deployment challenge, but also create an opportunity: strategically coordinating multiple LLMs may unlock collective intelligence exceeding any single model. Existing approaches fix how models are combined in advance, overlooking the dynamic, state-dependent role of complementarity in complex problem solving. Drawing on the wisdom-of-crowds paradigm, we reconceptualize collective LLM intelligence as relay-style complementarity: a sequential process in which each successor model is selected to address the specific bottleneck identified in its predecessor's output. To operationalize this, we propose WILC (Wisdom Integration of LLM Crowds), a framework grounded in two design principles. First, iterative reflection-and-refinement establishes a state-preserving workflow through which models diagnose and refine prior outputs. Second, complementarity-driven model selection governs transitions via a dual-gate mechanism: prospective complementarity fit (PCF) identifies the worker most suited to the current bottleneck, while posterior complementarity gain (PCG) evaluates whether the selected transition improves the evolving solution. Experiments across four diverse benchmarks show that WILC outperforms existing approaches, including single-model self-refinement, ensemble methods, and query-routing methods. Under standardized pricing assumptions, WILC matches the average benchmark performance of GPT-5.2 at roughly 7 times lower estimated per-query cost, while facilitating data sovereignty through self-hosted deployment. This study extends wisdom-of-crowds theory from static aggregation to sequential AI complementarity and provides transferable design principles for multi-AI coordination.

</details>


### [154] [CAGE: Certified Authorization under Typed-Return Uncertainty for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29190)
*Blaise Delattre,Cong Wang,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAGE 提出一种针对 LLM 工具调用代理的联合鲁棒性认证方法，通过精确枚举离散分支并结合连续扰动认证，解决了分类通道与数值通道分别认证后无法组合的问题，能在保持一定自主决策比例的同时消除点式门控的误放行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 代理的运行时权限门控仅对观察到的单一返回值进行授权判断，未考虑返回值与其来源绑定时可能发生的小错误（包括一次绑定错误和有限的数值漂移）。在声明的"合理正确绑定返回"邻域内，现有方法无法保证候选动作始终保持授权状态。

Method: CAGE 直接对该联合邻域进行认证：对离散分支（绑定错误）进行精确枚举，在每个分支内对连续扰动进行认证。文中给出两种实现：当策略本身可执行时，CAGE-Exact 直接认证策略；否则 CAGE-Lip 和 CAGE-RS 在显式的、可测量的保真度假设下认证一个学习得到的门控函数。

Result: 在合成数据、策略即代码、监管规则及真实交易等场景中，CAGE 消除了精确点式门控所允许的预算内误放行，同时保持了相当比例的自主决策能力。

Conclusion: 分类通道与数值通道的分别认证不具备组合性，必须通过 CAGE 这样的联合认证方法才能可靠地保证邻域内动作授权的安全性。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents act on typed tool returns, records pairing provenance and categorical fields with numerical values. Runtime permission gates generally authorize the observed return and action, leaving the decision unprotected against small errors in how the return was bound to its source. We ask whether a candidate action stays authorized over a declared neighborhood of plausible correctly bound returns: one admissible binding fault plus bounded numerical drift. We prove that certifying the categorical and numerical channels separately does not compose: perturbations that are safe on each channel alone can jointly turn the same action unsafe. CAGE certifies this joint neighborhood directly, enumerating the discrete branches exactly and certifying the continuous perturbation within each branch. Across synthetic, policy-as-code, regulatory, and real-transaction settings, CAGE removes the in-budget false allows that accurate pointwise gates admit, while keeping a useful fraction of decisions autonomous. When the policy is executable, CAGE-Exact certifies the policy itself; otherwise CAGE-Lip and CAGE-RS certify a learned gate under an explicit, measured fidelity assumption.

</details>


### [155] [MirrorCraft: Paired Evaluation under Hidden Rule Changes in Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29218)
*Jianxin Gao,Beini Hu,Runze Li,Wanli Peng,Ruohan Lei,Jinyuan Zhang,Linna Deng,Tianyi Yu,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MirrorCraft 是一个用于评估 LLM 智能体在 Minecraft 中应对隐藏规则变化的成对基准测试，通过配对普通世界与修改规则后的镜像世界，使用规则干预效应（RIE）量化智能体性能变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Minecraft 基准测试在固定游戏机制下评估 LLM 智能体，无法衡量智能体在已知配方、掉落物等规则发生改变时是否仍能持续推进任务。因此需要一种能评估智能体对隐藏规则变化适应能力的基准。

Method: 构建 MirrorCraft 基准，包含五个受控生物群系、六套规则修改方案、三个渐进式目标、两种模型家族、六种智能体配置，均使用统一 Mineflayer 接口。每个 Mirror 世界与其配对的 Vanilla 世界在 terrain、出生点、资源布局、目标、界面和行动预算上完全匹配，仅通过 datapack 修改选定服务器端规则。使用确定性进度里程碑与成功率评估任务推进，并引入规则干预效应（RIE）度量配对世界间的性能变化。

Result: 实验表明，隐藏规则变化在不同规则套件间产生显著差异化的影响。在无规则描述的各配置中，ReAct 取得最高的汇总 Mirror 得分。提供精确规则描述在所有三个目标上仅带来适度的平均进度和完成率提升。

Conclusion: MirrorCraft 将 Minecraft 评估扩展到固定机制之外，为研究智能体在规则不同于熟悉环境时如何利用游戏结果进行决策提供了受控实验平台。

Abstract: With the prosperity of the large language models (LLMs), it has become an interesting topic: how do LLM-based agents work in Minecraft? Unfortunately, most existing benchmarks evaluate them under fixed game mechanics. High performance in these settings does not show whether an agent can continue making progress when familiar recipes, drops, and other rules change. In this paper, we introduce MirrorCraft, a paired benchmark for evaluating agents under hidden rule changes in Minecraft. Each Mirror world is a copy of its paired Vanilla world, with selected server-side rules modified by the corresponding datapack. Terrain, spawn, resource placement, objective, interface, and action budget remain matched within every Vanilla-Mirror pair. MirrorCraft includes five controlled biomes, six rule suites, three progression objectives, two model families, and six agent configurations under a shared Mineflayer interface. We evaluate task progress with deterministic advancement milestones and success rate and use the Rule Intervention Effect (RIE) to measure the performance change between matched Vanilla and Mirror worlds. The experiments show that hidden rule changes have strongly different effects across suites. Among the configurations evaluated without rule descriptions, ReAct achieves the highest pooled Mirror score. Providing the exact rules yields modest gains in average progress and completion across all three objectives. MirrorCraft extends Minecraft evaluation beyond fixed mechanics and provides a controlled setting for studying how agents use gameplay outcomes when the rules of the current world differ from familiar ones.

</details>


### [156] [Don't Mix Rewards, Mix Policies: Policy Decomposition and Optimization for Multi-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29246)
*Ruiming Liang,Yi Zhong,Yizhen Yuan,Yinan Zheng,Tianyi Tan,Tianyue Wang,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Xianyuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRISM 提出一种基于策略空间分解与组合的多奖励强化学习框架，通过独立优化多个正向策略和一个全局负向策略来避免奖励间的冲突，并在推理时实现灵活的策略组合与偏好控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多奖励 RL 在 LLM 训练中面临严重的"对齐税"问题——不同优化目标之间相互权衡甚至冲突，导致训练不稳定且效率低下。现有方法在奖励层面进行组合，难以从根本上解决目标冲突。

Method: PRISM 将多奖励优化问题从"奖励组合"转向"策略空间分解与组合"：分别训练一组独立的"正向策略"（每个对应一个奖励维度）和一个"全局负向策略"（捕捉所有不期望的行为），训练时各策略独立优化以消除冲突，推理时通过灵活组合这些策略实现可控的行为输出。

Result: 在科学推理、工具使用推理以及有用性-安全性对齐等三个任务上，PRISM 一致地优于现有多奖励 RL 基线方法，且额外提供了推理时的偏好控制能力。

Conclusion: PRISM 验证了策略空间分解优于奖励空间组合的思路，为解决 LLM 多目标对齐中的冲突问题提供了有效方案，同时赋予了推理阶段动态调节行为偏好的灵活性。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) are expected not just to answer correctly, but to adapt their behavior to different human values and use cases. As a result, multi-reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become an increasingly important problem for LLMs, where each reward captures a different aspect of desired behavior. However, optimizing with multiple rewards suffers from a more severe alignment tax issue, where different optimization objectives can trade off or even conflict with each other, leading to unstable and inefficient post-training. In this work, we propose PRISM, a new multi-reward RL framework built upon the idea of policy-space decomposition and composition. Instead of compositing different rewards, PRISM optimizes a set of standalone positive policies and a global negative policy. This alleviates the potential conflict during multi-reward policy optimization, while enabling controllability during inference by flexible policy composition. Experiments on scientific reasoning, tool-use reasoning, and helpfulness-safety alignment show that PRISM consistently outperforms existing multi-reward RL baselines, with extra controllability for inference-time preference control.

</details>


### [157] [Tool Specifications Matter: Uncovering and Mitigating Safety Risks in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29254)
*Minghui Pan,Jiayuxuan Yang,Yuanyuan Yuan,Yu Jiang,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现以 schema 格式编写的工具规格说明是 AI 智能体安全性下降的主要原因，它削弱了模型内部的拒绝信号；作者提出 SafeKeep 推理时保护机制，通过将安全判断与工具执行解耦，显著提升了智能体对有害请求的拒绝率并降低了攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为智能体部署时安全性显著下降，但导致这种退化的根源尚不清楚。本文旨在定位智能体安全退化的关键来源，并据此设计有效的保护措施。

Method: 首先通过白盒表征分析，识别出 schema 格式的工具规格说明是安全退化的主要原因——它削弱了模型内部的拒绝信号。基于此提出 SafeKeep：一种推理时保护方法，将安全判断与工具执行解耦，使用扁平化文本格式的工具规格说明进行安全审查，同时保留原始 schema 格式用于实际执行。

Result: 在两个代表性基准测试和四个 LLM（含白盒与黑盒模型）上：有害请求的平均拒绝率从 23.8% 提升至 70.6%；观察级提示注入下的平均攻击成功率从 25.6% 降至 2.5%；同时优于现有保护方法且保持了任务处理能力。

Conclusion: Schema 格式的工具规格是智能体安全性退化的关键来源，SafeKeep 通过解耦安全判断与工具执行，以轻量级推理时干预的方式有效解决了这一问题，在安全性和可用性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: AI agents extend large language models (LLMs) with external tools, enabling them to perform complex tasks and translate model outputs into consequential real-world actions. Yet LLMs often become substantially less safe when deployed as agents, and the source of this degradation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we identify schema-formatted tool specifications as a primary source of agent safety degradation and show, through white-box representation analysis, that they weaken the model's internal refusal signals and contribute to unsafe tool execution. Building on this finding, we propose SafeKeep, an inference-time safeguard that decouples safety judgment from tool execution: it assesses requests using flattened textual tool specifications while retaining the original schema-formatted specifications for execution. Across two representative benchmarks and four LLMs, including both white-box and black-box models, SafeKeep increases the average refusal rate for harmful requests from 23.8% to 70.6% and reduces the average attack success rate under observation-level prompt injection from 25.6% to 2.5%. It also outperforms existing safeguards and preserves task-handling capability. We release the code and data at https://github.com/snowcatsmoking/SafeKeep .

</details>


### [158] [MAGA: Multi-Platform Self-Fusion of GUI Agents via Structured Action Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29320)
*Hang Yan,Zhangxuan GU,Beitong Zhou,Jiaxuan Chen,Runze Li,Yusong Hu,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGA 提出一种面向跨环境 GUI agent 的蒸馏方法，通过结构化动作感知来重分配训练信号：抑制无效动作的蒸馏信号，聚焦于错误动作的学习，并引入训练专用的 hint 机制优化教师监督，在 8B 规模上超越最强基线 2.0%，达到接近教师模型的平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI agent 通常面向单一环境（移动端/Web/桌面），将多个领域专家模型整合为统一的跨环境策略存在挑战：权重合并（weight merging）在专家间存在分歧时会破坏可执行动作，而在策略蒸馏（OPD）虽避免了教师监督冲突，却对所有响应 token 一视同仁，忽略了只有动作 token 才是 agent 与环境交互的唯一接口。

Method: 提出 MAGA（Motion-Aware Gradient Allocation），核心思想是根据结构化动作的正确性重分配训练信号：抑制对正确动作的不必要蒸馏信号，将学习聚焦于错误动作；同时引入一种仅在训练阶段使用的 hint 机制，在不改变学生模型输入的前提下优化领域专家教师提供的监督信号。

Result: 在两个模型规模上，MAGA 均取得最高的平均成功率：在 8B 规模上比最强基线高出 2.0%，并且几乎达到与教师模型相同的平均性能。

Conclusion: MAGA 通过动作感知的梯度分配策略和训练 hint 机制，有效解决了跨环境 GUI agent 蒸馏中信号分配不合理的问题，实现了统一策略对多个领域专家模型的高效继承。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents based on large language models are increasingly deployed across mobile, web, and desktop environments. However, existing agents are typically domain-specific, limiting the deployment and user experience. This motivates the consolidation of specialized models into a single cross-environment policy. Weight merging directly merges domain-specific experts but can corrupt executable actions under expert disagreement, while on-policy distillation (OPD) avoids conflicting teacher supervision yet still treats all response tokens equally during distillation, ignoring that action tokens are the only interface between the environment and the agent. To address this, We introduce MAGA that re-allocates training signal according to the structured action. Based on the correctness of the generated action, it suppresses unnecessary or invalid distillation signals and focuses learning on erroneous actions. Besides, a training-only hint optimizes the supervision signal provided by domain-specific teachers without changing the student input. Across two model scales, MAGA achieves the highest mean success rate, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.0% at 8B and achieves almost the same average performance with teachers.

</details>


### [159] [Beyond Component Testing: Validating Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29405)
*Fabio Orazio Mirto,Luca D'Agati,Giuseppe Tricomi,Stefano Silvestri,Francesco Longo,Antonio Puliafito,Giovanni Merlino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了257篇论文，提出五维度分类法（行为、安全、时序、监管、多智能体）来刻画智能体系统的验证问题。研究发现行为评估相对成熟，而时序有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可读性和开放式多智能体保障仍发展不足，最终提出以生命周期为导向的研究议程，核心主张是：可信部署依赖于在上下文中验证轨迹，而非仅评估孤立组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统通过规划、工具使用、记忆、交互和适应的多步轨迹运行，其行为随时间展开且受环境变化影响，这使得传统的组件测试和单次输入-输出评估方法不再充分。当前缺乏对智能体系统验证问题的系统性刻画，亟需整合分散于各领域的研究，识别覆盖缺口并指引未来方向。

Method: 作者综合了257篇论文，涵盖智能体评估、软件保障、信息物理系统、运行时监控和监管指南等多个领域。在此基础上构建了一个五维度分类法（行为有效性、安全性、时序有效性、监管合规性、多智能体协调），并以此映射现有方法、揭示反复出现的覆盖缺口。通过医疗、工业运营和智能出行三个安全关键领域的案例研究，展示分类法在真实场景中的适用性，并以文献记录的故障模式为支撑。

Result: 分析表明：行为评估相对成熟；时序有效性验证、运行时证据维护、监管可读性以及开放式多智能体系统保障四个维度严重发展不足。三个跨领域案例验证了五维度分类法在安全关键场景中的解释力，揭示各维度在实际故障中相互交织。

Conclusion: 论文提出以生命周期为导向的研究议程，聚焦四个方向：有界自主性规范、对抗性轨迹生成、运行时监控以及可审计证据结构。核心结论是：智能体AI的可信部署取决于在上下文中验证完整轨迹，而非孤立地评估单个组件。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems act through multi-step trajectories that combine planning, tool use, memory, interaction, and adaptation. This behavior stretches validation practice beyond component testing and one-shot input--output evaluation, because acceptable system behavior now depends on how decisions unfold over time and under changing environmental conditions. This survey synthesizes 257 papers spanning agent evaluation, software assurance, cyber-physical systems, runtime monitoring, and regulatory guidance in order to characterize the validation problem for agentic systems. The review is organized around a five-dimension taxonomy covering behavioral, safety, temporal, regulatory, and multi-agent concerns, and uses that taxonomy to map current approaches and expose recurrent coverage gaps. The analysis shows that behavioral evaluation is comparatively mature, while temporal validity, runtime evidence maintenance, regulatory legibility, and open-ended multi-agent systems assurance remain under-developed. Three cross-domain case studies (medical care, industrial operations, smart-mobility systems) provide operational illustrations of how the five taxonomy dimensions recur in safety-critical settings, grounded in the failure patterns documented in the reviewed literature. The paper concludes with a lifecycle-oriented research agenda centered on bounded-autonomy specifications, adversarial trajectory generation, runtime monitoring, and audit-ready evidence structures. The central claim is that trustworthy deployment of agentic AI depends on validating trajectories in context rather than assessing isolated components alone.

</details>


### [160] [ModelEquivBench: Certifying Multi-Relational Evaluation of LLM-Generated Optimization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29431)
*Penglin Zhu,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ModelEquivBench 是一个多关系、可认证的评估系统，用于细粒度评估 LLM 生成的优化模型与真实模型之间在 7 个语义层级 (E0–E6) 上的等价性，并提供可独立核查的证据。对三个主流模型快照的评估揭示了粗粒度指标无法捕捉的差异化失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法将 LLM 生成的优化模型简化为"等价/不等价"二元判断或执行成功率，这些标签既不可独立核查，也无法忠实反映两个公式在多种不同语义层面上的关系，导致评估过于粗糙且缺乏诊断力。

Method: 提出 ModelEquivBench，定义 E0 到 E6 七个语义层级：E0（模型构建与精确摄入）、E1（经验证的表示对齐）、E2/E3（同空间与投影可行集关系）、E4（目标序等价）、E5（最优值相等）、E6（最优解集等价）。每个判定附带关系合适的、可独立复验的证据：E0–E1 提供可重放轨迹或显式映射，正向 E2–E6 结论提供精确有理数证书，负面结论提供显式见证。不完整映射、不支持的模型结构及资源限制产生 UNKNOWN 或 N/A，前提条件不满足则标记为 ABSENT。实验采用无修复协议，在 173 个基础问题的冻结队列上评估 GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Qwen3.5-397B-A17B 三个模型快照。

Result: 三个模型分别有 49、35、25 个单元包含可执行但至少在一个支持关系上被认证为负面的候选模型；同时分别出现 25、8、18 次结构性拒绝，这些拒绝发生在 E2 已验证映射下可行集相等的模型对上。三个模型在语义层级剖面的不同阶段失效，无法被归纳为单一准确率分数。

Conclusion: ModelEquivBench 揭示了 LLM 生成优化模型的多维度语义差异，证明粗粒度的二元等价判断或执行成功率无法有效区分模型能力。不同模型在语义剖面的不同层级上暴露出不同的失效模式，因此需要多关系评估体系来进行有意义的比较和诊断。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate optimization models from natural language, but existing evaluation often reduces a generated model and its ground truth to a single equivalent/not-equivalent verdict or an execution-success rate--labels that are neither independently checkable nor faithful to the multiple distinct senses in which two formulations can agree. We present ModelEquivBench, a certifying, multi-relational evaluation system that reports a per-pair semantic profile E0--E6: model construction and exact ingestion (E0), verified representation alignment (E1), same-space and projected feasible-set relations (E2, E3), objective-order equivalence (E4), optimal-value equality (E5), and optimizer-set equivalence (E6). Each decided entry carries relation-appropriate, independently re-checkable evidence: replayable traces or explicit maps for E0--E1, exact-rational certificates for positive E2--E6 conclusions, and explicit witnesses for supported negatives. Incomplete mapping search, unsupported structure, and resource limits produce typed UNKNOWN or N/A outcomes rather than guesses, while unmet prerequisites are reported as ABSENT. Using ModelEquivBench to evaluate three model snapshots--GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Qwen3.5-397B-A17B--on the same frozen cohort of 173 base problems (346 cells per model) under a no-repair protocol, the resulting profiles expose distinctions that coarse baselines do not represent: 49, 35, and 25 cells contain executable candidates that are nevertheless certified negative on at least one supported relation, and 25, 8, and 18 structural rejections occur on pairs for which E2 certifies mapped feasible-set equality under a verified map. The three model snapshots fail at different stages of the profile and therefore cannot be meaningfully reduced to a single accuracy score.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Retrieval: Analytic Memory for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29440)
*Zhoujin Tian,Yao Tian,Hao Zhang,Cheng Chen,Yakun Li,Lei Zhang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaMM框架，在传统检索记忆之外引入"分析记忆"概念，通过从多模态交互中自动提取属性-值观测、发现重复字段结构并物化，支持过滤、聚合、排序和时间比较等分析操作，在MemEye和MemGallery基准上分别提升11.3%和7.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期多模态记忆系统（如检索记忆）仅能按相关性返回信息，缺乏对累积观测进行计算分析的能力（如过滤、聚合、排序、时序比较），无法满足用户对结构化分析的需求。

Method: 提出AdaMM框架：1）从对话、图像和上下文元数据中提取带溯源信息的属性-值对；2）自动发现重复出现的字段结构并物化为可查询结构；3）推理时由记忆感知规划器将查询分解为检索操作和分析操作，并路由至对应工具执行。

Result: 在MemEye和MemGallery两个长期多模态记忆基准上，AdaMM分别实现了最高11.3%和7.3%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 分析记忆作为检索记忆的互补抽象，能有效增强多模态记忆系统的结构化计算能力，AdaMM框架验证了该范式的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Long-term multimodal memory must support not only retrieving relevant information but also computing over observations accumulated across interactions. Existing systems largely emphasize \emph{retrieval memory}, organizing interaction histories through summaries and indexes to return query-relevant information at multiple granularities, from high-level abstractions to underlying records. In this paper, we formulate \emph{analytic memory} as a complementary abstraction that organizes recurring multimodal observations into queryable structures supporting filtering, aggregation, ranking, and temporal comparison. We present AdaMM, a framework that jointly supports retrieval and analytic memory. Rather than relying on application-defined schemas, AdaMM extracts provenance-linked attribute-value observations from dialogue, images, and contextual metadata, discovers recurring field structures, and materializes them for analytical access. At inference time, a memory-aware planner decomposes queries into retrieval and analytic operations and routes each operation to the appropriate tools. Experiments on two long-term multimodal memory benchmarks, MemEye and MemGallery, show that AdaMM improves performance by up to 11.3\% and 7.3\%, respectively.

</details>


### [162] [Self-Play Meets Skill Evolution: Self-Evolving Search Agents that Pose, Solve, and Remember](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29468)
*Zenghuang Fu,Zhaoyang Li,Qiuyuan Ai,Haoyu Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Ante Wang,Guannan He,Changwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SESA 提出了一种自演进的技能增强智能体，通过挑战者-求解器双向循环机制，使技能记忆与任务生成协同进化，从而持续改进策略学习和训练分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自博弈（self-play）智能体缺乏持久状态来显式塑造未来训练；外部技能记忆虽能保留程序性经验，但通常从固定任务分布中学习，无法随训练动态演化。

Method: SESA 构建了一个双向循环系统：挑战者（challenger）生成问题，参数独立的求解器（solver）检索技能作答；有价值的失败案例被蒸馏为可复用技能并写回外部记忆库；更新后的记忆改变求解器行为与成功率，进而改变挑战者的奖励和未来问题分布，形成技能记忆与任务生成的协同演化。技能同时通过在线策略训练进入模型参数，实现无记忆部署。

Result: 在 7 个开放域和多跳问答基准上，SESA 平均准确率超过 SSP 1.2–3.2 个百分点，并超越 SkillRL 基线 0.9 个百分点。Qwen3 模型上，SESA-Off 保留 1.8–2.2 个百分点的提升，最终技能库额外带来 0.5–1.0 个百分点增益。

Conclusion: 演化式技能记忆不仅是推理时插件，更能改变策略学习和未来训练分布，同时保留可选外部记忆的价值。

Abstract: Self-play agents can generate training problems without questions from target benchmarks, but their curricula lack persistent state: failures affect gradients yet do not explicitly shape future practice. External skill memories preserve procedural experience but are typically learned from fixed task distributions. We introduce \textbf{SESA} (Self-Evolving Skill-Augmented Agent), which makes procedural memory an evolving state of tool-augmented search self-play. A challenger poses problems, while a separately parameterized solver alone retrieves skills. Informative failures are distilled into reusable skills and written back to memory. The updated memory changes solver behavior and success, which changes the challenger's reward and the distribution of future problems; the resulting frontier produces new failures that rewrite memory. This bidirectional loop makes task generation and skill memory co-evolve. Because retrieved skills shape on-policy training trajectories, their benefits can enter the model parameters as well as remain in the external bank, enabling memory-free deployment and optional inference-time retrieval. Across seven open-domain and multi-hop question-answering benchmarks, SESA improves average accuracy over SSP by 1.2--3.2 points across multiple backbones and surpasses the skill-augmented SkillRL baseline by 0.9 points under a unified evaluation protocol. On Qwen3 models, SESA-Off retains 1.8--2.2 points of improvement over SSP, while the final skill bank adds a further 0.5--1.0 points. These results show that evolving skill memory is not merely an inference-time plug-in: it changes policy learning and the future training distribution while retaining value as optional external memory. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zenghuang-Fu/SESA-Self-Evolving-Search-Agents.

</details>


### [163] [AMTFV: Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification for LLM Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29549)
*Rui Zou,Yutao Zhu,Mengqi Wei,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AMTFV 提出了一种通过数学工具流（MTF）将验证建模与具体执行解耦的智能体验证框架，利用数学工具箱实现精确计算，在多个模型和数据集上显著提升了答案验证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM答案验证方法存在两难：自然语言反思无法可靠支持精确计算，而直接生成验证程序又过早地将数学建模与底层实现耦合。需要一种既能精确计算又能保持建模灵活性的验证方法。

Method: 提出AMTFV框架，核心是"中断-执行-恢复"接口——数学工具流（MTF）。验证智能体构建验证工作流，将需要精确执行的数学对象和计算意图编码为MTF请求，发送给数学工具箱智能体；后者解析请求、生成可执行调用并分派到后端进行精确计算，工具输出再支撑答案裁决、答案修正和工作流修正。

Result: 在5个具有挑战性的数学推理数据集上，使用DeepSeek、GPT、Gemini共7种模型配置进行评估。AMTFV整体优于代表性基线；在单一模型配置下，相较最强基线平均准确率提升最高达8.3个百分点，在中高验证复杂度样本上增益更大。

Conclusion: AMTFV通过解耦验证建模与精确执行，有效提升了LLM数学答案验证的可靠性，尤其在复杂验证场景下优势显著。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated strong mathematical problem-solving capabilities, yet reliably verifying their candidate answers remains challenging. Existing representative methods mainly revise outputs through natural-language reflection or assist verification by directly generating verification programs; the former may not reliably support exact computation, whereas the latter prematurely couples mathematical modeling with low-level implementation. We propose AMTFV (Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification). By introducing Mathematical Tool Flow (MTF) as an interrupt--execute--resume interface, AMTFV decouples verification modeling from concrete execution and supports exact computation through a mathematical toolbox. Specifically, the verification agent first constructs a verification workflow, encodes the mathematical objects and computational intent requiring reliable execution in an MTF request, and sends it to the mathematical toolbox agent. The latter parses the request, generates executable calls, and dispatches them to the backend for exact computation. Tool outputs then support candidate-answer adjudication, answer revision, and verification-workflow revision. We evaluate AMTFV on five challenging mathematical reasoning datasets with seven model configurations from DeepSeek, GPT, and Gemini. Experimental results show that AMTFV outperforms the representative baselines evaluated in this study overall; under an individual model configuration, it improves average accuracy over the strongest baseline by up to 8.3 percentage points, with larger gains on samples of medium and high verification complexity.

</details>


### [164] [COntExt: Towards Context-Aware Ontology Extension from Operational Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29553)
*Hussain Hussain,Stefan Schöberl,Angelika Schneider,Verena Geist*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COntExt框架，利用结构化操作指标定义中的领域知识自动扩展本体，将扩展问题分解为父类预测、关系类型预测和数据属性赋值三个子任务，在四个网络安全本体上验证表明，指标上下文能有效改善建议质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织日益使用结构化、机器可读的格式定义操作指标，这些指标定义中隐式编码了大量领域知识（如概念、属性、关系），往往超出形式化本体所捕获的范围。然而，将操作指标目录与本体知识建立关联目前仍依赖人工、临时且劳动密集的方式，缺乏自动化支持。

Method: 提出COntExt（Context-aware Ontology Extension）框架：将本体扩展问题形式化为三个子任务——（1）父类预测：为指标引用的概念在现有本体中找到合适的父类；（2）关系类型预测：确定概念间的关系类型；（3）数据属性赋值：为概念分配合适的数据属性。利用指标定义中的上下文信息辅助上述预测，并与仅使用本体上下文的基线方法进行对比评估。

Result: 在四个网络安全本体上的实验表明，指标派生的上下文在关系类型预测和数据属性赋值两个子任务上均优于仅使用本体上下文的基线方法，验证了操作指标目录作为本体扩展信息源的有效性。

Conclusion: 操作指标目录是一种实用但未被充分利用的本体扩展来源；COntExt框架能够显著降低组织维护本体的成本，实现从人工工程到自动化扩展的转变。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly define operational metrics in structured, machine-readable formats to monitor systems, processes, and compliance. These metric definitions implicitly encode domain knowledge, such as referencing concepts, properties, and relationships, that often extends what is captured in formal ontologies. Yet the connection between operational metric catalogues and ontological knowledge remains manual, ad-hoc, and labor-intensive. We present COntExt, a framework for context-aware ontology extension that takes structured metric definitions as input and suggests how referenced concepts and properties should be integrated into an existing ontology, utilizing the context of these metrics. The framework defines the extension problem as three sub-tasks: parent class prediction, relation type prediction, and data property assignment. Across four cybersecurity ontologies, we evaluate different algorithms for each task. Our results show that metric-derived context improves the suggestions over ontology-context baselines for relation type prediction and data property assignment. Our work demonstrates that operational metric catalogues are a practical and underexploited source for ontology extension. This work enables organizations to maintain their ontologies at a significantly lower cost than manual engineering.

</details>


### [165] [LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning from Preference Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29559)
*Manith Adikari,Bei Peng,Samuele Vinanzi,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEMUR 提出了一个结合多目标强化学习与偏好反馈的新框架，使智能体能够在没有预定义奖励函数的情况下，通过多人的偏好反馈交互式地学习最优多目标策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策任务常涉及多个竞争目标（如性能与效率），难以指定真实的奖励函数。MORL 假设每个目标都有良好定义的奖励函数，而 PbRL 虽能从人类反馈中学习奖励，却主要限于单目标场景。两者之间存在空白，需要一种无需预定义奖励函数的多目标 RL 方法。

Method: LEMUR 框架联合学习策略和多个目标特定的奖励模型，从多个人类反馈的偏好中学习。智能体在训练过程中通过与人类交互获取偏好信号，同时优化多个目标的奖励模型和策略，从而在学习过程中有效平衡竞争目标。

Result: 在多目标基准任务上的实验表明，LEMUR 相比基线方法表现出更优的性能，验证了其在无预定义奖励函数条件下解决多目标决策任务的有效性。

Conclusion: LEMUR 为在无预定义奖励函数条件下解决多目标决策任务提供了一条有前景的路径，成功桥接了 MORL 与 PbRL 之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems are typically trained using a single, well-specified scalar reward function. However, real-world decision-making tasks often involve multiple, competing objectives, such as performance versus efficiency, where ground-truth reward functions are difficult to specify or inaccessible. While Multi-Objective RL (MORL) addresses such trade-offs by modeling rewards as vectors, existing approaches typically assume access to a well-specified reward function for each objective, inheriting the same challenges faced by single-objective RL. Meanwhile, Preference-based RL (PbRL) has shown great potential in solving complex tasks without access to a pre-defined reward function through reward learning from human feedback, yet has largely been studied in single-objective settings. In this work, we bridge this gap with LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Preference feedback, a novel framework where an agent interactively learns from the preferences of multiple humans to learn optimal multi-objective policies. Our approach jointly learns policies and multiple objective-specific reward models from human feedback, enabling agents to effectively balance competing objectives during learning. We evaluate LEMUR on a variety of benchmark multi-objective tasks, and empirical results demonstrate its superior performance over baseline methods. Our method presents a promising direction for solving multi-objective decision-making tasks without pre-defined reward functions.

</details>


### [166] [DungeonBench: A Benchmark for Rules-Rich Tactical Reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons Combat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29577)
*Ismayil Ismayilov,Atakan Kara,Kaan Oktay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DungeonBench 是一个基于 D&D 战斗规则的战术推理基准，提供完整的战术观测、决策空间和可执行选项，覆盖遭遇战和跨遭遇日两个赛道，评估前沿语言模型在局部战术决策与长程资源调度上的综合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏与模拟器基准大多未能充分测试"规则密集型战术推理"——即当几何、时机、资源、目标及规则交互同时起作用时的决策能力。简化的战斗模拟器往往抽象掉关键机制，导致评估的生态效度不足。因此需要构建一个能覆盖广泛规则内容、保留完整战术复杂性的新基准。

Method: 基于 D&D 2014 SRD 构建模拟器，生成包含全面战术观测、待决策节点及可执行选项（移动、攻击、法术、反应、目标、准备、稀缺资源等）的决策流。设两个赛道：Encounter 赛道评估单场遭遇战中的局部战术决策；Day 赛道将多场遭遇战通过持久生命值、法术位、消耗品、准备及短休时机串联，考察资源跨期调度。支持启发式控制器、语言模型策略、学习型选项排序器及掩码动作强化学习智能体四类评估模式。

Result: 在前沿语言模型策略上的评估表明，完整的战术观测并未使基准饱和：前沿模型在直接遭遇战中常有胜绩，但在跨遭遇日的关联场景下暴露出资源预算、短休时机选择和规则感知战术纪律方面的显著不足。

Conclusion: DungeonBench 成功暴露了当前语言模型在长程战术推理和资源管理上的短板，表明规则密集型、多时间尺度决策是未来智能评估的重要方向。该基准为战术推理研究提供了统一、可扩展的评估平台。

Abstract: Games and simulators make valuable benchmarks by turning decisions into measurable outcomes, but many current suites under-test rules-rich tactical reasoning: the ability to choose well when geometry, timing, resources, objectives, and rule interactions all matter at once. We introduce DungeonBench, a benchmark for tactical reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons combat, built to cover the vast majority of combat-relevant 2014 System Reference Document content whose effects can be resolved by the simulator while retaining mechanics that simplified combat simulators often abstract away. At each step, DungeonBench exposes a complete tactical observation, a pending decision, and an indexed list of executable options spanning movement, attacks, spells, reactions, objectives, preparation, and scarce resources. The task is to value legal choices whose consequences depend on action economy, creature traits, battlefield geometry, timing windows, and future encounters. DungeonBench has two tracks: Encounter, which evaluates local tactical play in single fights, and Day, which links encounters through persistent hit points, spell slots, consumables, preparation, and short-rest timing, forcing policies to trade off immediate tactical advantage against future survivability. The same engine-generated decision stream supports heuristic controllers, language-model policies, learned option rankers, and masked-action reinforcement-learning agents. We evaluate frontier language-model policies on this shared decision stream. Results show that full tactical observations do not saturate the benchmark: frontier policies often win direct encounters, but linked encounter days expose failures in resource budgeting, rest timing, and rule-aware tactical discipline.

</details>


### [167] [AgentHPOBench: A Benchmark For Evaluating LLM Agents as Sequential Hyperparameter Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29626)
*Tianyu Huai,Tingshuo Fan,Xinchi Chen,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Shuang Chen,Jie Zhou,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AgentHPOBench，一个面向科学智能体的序贯超参数优化基准，包含 30 个可执行 ML 任务，评估智能体基于实验证据进行迭代决策的能力。12 个主流智能体的测试表明，当前智能体虽具备跨领域实验优化能力，但在持续迭代精炼、复杂日志诊断和稳定收敛至参考性能方面仍存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多聚焦于静态代码生成、论文复现或最终答案正确性，缺乏对智能体能否"解读实验证据并据此指导后续超参数决策"这一核心能力的直接评估。LLM 正从代码补全系统向自主科学智能体演进，迫切需要填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建 AgentHPOBench 基准，涵盖 7 个研究类别的 30 个可执行 ML 任务。每个任务以经验证的基线运行为起点，智能体需进行多轮序贯干预——每步观察累积的配置、指标与日志，再提出下一个有效配置。在统一协议下评估 12 个广泛使用的智能体及传统 HPO 基线方法。

Result: 当前智能体在跨领域实验优化方面展现出可量化的能力，但在以下方面存在明显不足：(1) 持续迭代精炼能力有限；(2) 复杂日志诊断困难；(3) 难以稳定收敛至已报告的参考性能水平。

Conclusion: AgentHPOBench 揭示了当前 LLM 智能体在科学实验自主决策中的关键短板，为未来面向科学发现场景的智能体设计与评估提供了重要的基准工具和方向指引。

Abstract: As LLMs evolve from code completion systems into autonomous scientific agents, evaluating their ability to conduct experiments has become increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically focus on static code generation, paper replication, or final answer correctness, but do not directly assess whether agents can interpret experimental evidence and use it to guide subsequent hyperparameter decisions. To address this gap, we introduce AgentHPOBench, a sequential benchmark comprising 30 executable machine learning tasks across seven research categories. Each task begins with a validated baseline run, after which an agent performs several sequential interventions. At each step, the agent observes the accumulated configurations, metrics, and logs before proposing the next valid configuration. We evaluate 12 widely used agents and conventional HPO baselines under a unified protocol. The results show that current agents exhibit measurable experimental optimization ability across domains, but still face clear limitations in sustained iterative refinement, complex log diagnosis, and consistent progress toward reported reference performance.

</details>


### [168] [Development of FDD-ON: an Ontology for VAV HVAC System Fault Detection and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29657)
*Yimin Chen,Brian Fricke,Bo Shen,Jamie Lian,Mingkan Zhang,James Lo,Yun Zhang,Shi Ye,Jiajing Huang,Han Hu,Chujie Lu,Rui Tang,George Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FDD-ON，一个模块化、可扩展的本体，用于形式化表示VAV HVAC系统的故障检测与诊断语义，通过定义故障、症状、影响及其因果关系，为跨异构数据源的FDD应用提供机器可解释的语义基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前HVAC系统FDD领域面临数据可解释性和互操作性不足的问题，异构数据源、多样设备类型和不同诊断输出形成了碎片化的信息孤岛，阻碍了数字孪生FDD框架和AI驱动维护决策等应用的落地。亟需结构化的领域知识来桥接这些异构元素。

Method: 提出FDD-ON（FDD本体），采用模块化可扩展设计，形式化表示VAV HVAC系统的组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及相关属性。本体整合了受控词汇表支持的故障与症状语义，构建了涵盖广泛运行异常的故障、症状及影响库，并通过显式的"致因-故障-症状-影响"关系链实现知识建模。利用公开数据集进行评估，并通过FDD开发应用进行演示验证。

Result: 利用公开可用的VAV HVAC系统数据集评估表明，FDD-ON能够为跨多种应用场景构建可扩展、透明且可互操作的FDD解决方案提供基础语义框架。

Conclusion: FDD-ON为推进HVAC系统故障检测与诊断的规模化、透明化和互操作化提供了有效的语义基础设施，有望支撑数字孪生FDD和AI维护决策等高级应用的发展。

Abstract: Fault detection and diagnosis (FDD) technology is essential for improving HVAC system reliability, energy efficiency, and maintenance effectiveness. However, effective deployment of FDD solutions in buildings requires structured domain knowledge that can bridge heterogeneous data sources, diverse equipment types, and varied diagnostic outputs. Limited data interpretability and interoperability within the FDD domain have led to fragmented information silos, hindering the implementation of FDD and related applications, such as the digital twin-enabled FDD frameworks and artificial intelligence (AI)-driven maintenance decision-making systems. This paper presents an FDD Ontology (FDD-ON), a modular and extensible ontology to formally represent variable air volume (VAV) HVAC system components, fault types, symptom statuses, fault impacts and associated attributes. FDD-ON integrates HVAC system FDD semantics to provide comprehensive representations of fault and symptom attributes, supported by the well-defined controlled vocabulary. Additionally, FDD-ON offers comprehensive fault, symptom, and impact libraries to capture a broad spectrum of operational abnormalities and their consequences in VAV HVAC systems. Through explicit contributing cause-fault-symptom-impact relations, FDD-ON serves as a machine-interpretable basis for querying diagnostic knowledge, mapping heterogeneous FDD outputs, and developing interoperable FDD-related applications. FDD-ON is evaluated using publicly available VAV HVAC system datasets and demonstrated through FDD development applications. Results indicate that FDD-ON provides a foundational semantic framework for advancing scalable, transparent, and interoperable FDD solutions across various applications.

</details>


### [169] [ExtractBench: A Benchmark for Schema-Guided Enterprise Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29677)
*Boyang Zhang,Adrian Lyjak,Eli Stewart,Zhaoqi Li,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExtractBench是一个面向schema引导式信息提取的评测基准，首次同时评估值准确率、记录完整性、证据溯源和成本，包含4869页企业文档，覆盖8个业务领域。结果显示LlamaExtract Agentic Plus在全部三项指标上排名第一，准确率媲美编程Agent但成本显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业工作流越来越依赖Agent执行schema引导式提取任务，但现有评测缺乏同时对提取准确性、完整性、证据溯源和成本的综合评估。为此，需要构建一个能覆盖多领域、多文档类型，并具备可扩展标注流程的基准数据集。

Method: 构建ExtractBench基准，包含370份企业文档（4869页）、8个业务领域、67种文档类型，并标注挑战场景标签。可扩展的标注流程采用三重策略：真实文档使用多系统独立一致性校验，合成列表使用已知值注入，表单类文档辅以人工验证。评测指标包括：顺序无关的值F1（值准确率）、词级和页级F1（证据溯源），同时记录推理成本。

Result: 商业视觉语言模型（VLM）在短文档上表现良好，但在长文档上经常截断记录列表；编程Agent准确率更高但成本也显著更高。LlamaExtract Agentic Plus在三项指标上均排名第一，准确率与编程Agent相当，但成本仅为后者的零头。

Conclusion: ExtractBench填补了schema引导式提取任务综合评估的空白。LlamaExtract Agentic Plus在准确率-成本权衡上表现最优，证明了在保持高精度的同时大幅降低成本是可行的。数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Enterprise workflows increasingly rely on agents for \emph{schema-guided extraction}: given a document and a user-defined schema, the agent faithfully follows the schema to produce the correct output with source evidence as grounding metadata. We present ExtractBench, a benchmark for schema-guided extraction and, to our knowledge, the first to score value accuracy, record completeness at scale, grounding, and measured cost together. The evaluation system contains 4,869 pages across 370 enterprise documents, 8 business domains, and 67 document types, with clear tags differentiating their challenge scenarios. The scalable schema and ground-truth curation pipeline combines independent-system agreement for real documents, known values for synthetic lists, and human verification for forms. We report order-insensitive value F1 for value accuracy, plus two grounding metrics for source traceability: word- and page-level F1. Commercial VLMs perform well on short documents but often truncate record lists on long ones, while coding agents retain higher accuracy at much higher cost. LlamaExtract Agentic Plus ranks first on all three metrics, with accuracy comparable to coding agents at a fraction of the cost. Dataset and evaluation code are available on \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ExtractBench}{HuggingFace} and \href{https://github.com/run-llama/ExtractBench}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [170] [Evidence-Ledger Adjudication for Claim-Evidence Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26512)
*Gengyu Chen,Yongjie Yu,Weiling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种"证据账本裁定"工作流，将每条声明与证据包配对并判定支持关系，自动将不支持、矛盾或混合证据的声明退回作者修改。在三个数据集的2,335行盲测基准上，该方法在关系准确率和宏F1上显著优于非智能体基线，并能有效路由问题声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体起草声明的速度快于作者验证其证据支持的速度，导致写作中缺乏可追溯的证据审核机制。需要一个系统化的声明-证据追溯工作流来辅助AI写作的可审计性。

Method: 提出证据账本裁定（evidence-ledger adjudication）工作流：将每条声明与证据包配对，分配支持关系（支持/矛盾/缺失/混合），将不支持、矛盾或混合证据的声明路由回作者。使用AVeriTeC、CLIMATE-FEVER和SciFact三个数据集构建2,335行盲测基准，金标准关系和源证据标签在预测时隐藏，仅在评分时加入比较。

Result: 智能体证据账本条件达到0.676的关系准确率和0.601的宏F1，而最佳非智能体基线仅为0.383准确率和0.303宏F1。成功路由了1435条金标准标注为矛盾、缺失证据或混合证据声明中的1270条，同时路由了900条支持声明中的295条。

Conclusion: 证据账本裁定可将异构证据包转化为AI辅助写作中可审计的追溯层，为AI写作的事实性验证提供有效方案。

Abstract: AI agents can draft claims faster than authors can check whether the cited or retrieved evidence supports them. We study evidence-ledger adjudication: a claim-evidence traceability workflow that pairs each claim with an evidence packet, assigns a support relation, and routes unsupported, contradicted, or mixed-evidence claims back to the author. The empirical core is a 2,335-row blind benchmark built from independent external labels in AVeriTeC, CLIMATE-FEVER, and SciFact. Gold relations and source evidence labels are hidden during prediction and joined only for scoring. On this benchmark, the agent evidence-ledger condition achieves 0.676 relation accuracy and 0.601 macro-F1, compared with 0.383 accuracy and 0.303 macro-F1 for the best non-agent baseline. It also routes 1270/1435 claims whose gold labels indicate contradiction, missing evidence, or mixed evidence, while routing 295/900 supported claims. These results show that evidence-ledger adjudication can turn heterogeneous evidence packets into an auditable traceability layer for AI-assisted writing.

</details>


### [171] [SVR: Self-Verifying Refinement via Joint Verdict-Confidence Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28457)
*Hongyu Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SVR 是一种无需外部验证器的多轮强化学习框架，通过自验证机制对测试时计算进行自适应分配——模型在每轮生成答案的同时输出正确性判断和置信度，仅在判定正确且置信度超过阈值时停止，从而以更少的推理轮次实现更高的数学推理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩展测试时计算的方法存在两个痛点：（1）固定预算策略在简单样本上浪费计算资源；（2）基于验证器的精炼方法依赖外部反馈信号。亟需一种让模型自主决定何时停止精炼的内部控制机制。

Method: 提出 SVR 框架：在固定轮次轨迹上使用 GRPO 训练，奖励函数同时优化三个目标——答案正确性、校准感知的自验证质量、以及"正确即停"的决策行为。每轮模型输出答案、离散正确性判定和置信度分数；仅在判定为正确且置信度超阈值时保留答案，否则继续精炼。自适应停止策略仅在推理阶段激活，真实标签仅用于构造训练奖励，不暴露给策略。在 Qwen3.5-2B 上于七个数学推理基准集上评估。

Result: SVR 在 7 个数学推理基准上达到 0.563 的宏观平均准确率，平均仅需 2.99 轮推理。在完整系统对比中，SVR 优于标准 GRPO、多个强多轮基线以及固定预算的 oracle 引导分数反馈参考方法，且所需轮次远少于固定十轮推理。

Conclusion: SVR 证明，学得的自验证信号可以有效充当内部控制机制，实现答案保留和自适应测试时计算分配，为无需外部 oracle 的高效推理拓展提供了新路径。

Abstract: Scaling test-time computation can improve language-model reasoning, but uniform budgets waste computation on easy inputs, while verifier-guided refinement relies on external feedback. We introduce Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR), an oracle-free multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that learns to use self-verification as a compute-control policy. At each turn, the model produces a solution together with a discrete correctness verdict and a confidence score; it retains the current answer only when the verdict is Correct and confidence exceeds a threshold, and otherwise continues refinement using its own self-verification. Ground-truth correctness is used only to construct training rewards and is never exposed to the policy through refinement prompts or required at inference. SVR is trained with GRPO on fixed-horizon trajectories using rewards that promote solution correctness, calibration-aware self-verification, and stop-ready correct states; adaptive stopping is activated only at inference. On seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3.5-2B, SVR achieves a macro-average accuracy of 0.563 with only 2.99 inference turns on average. In the evaluated complete-system comparison, it exceeds standard GRPO, strong multi-turn baselines, and a fixed-budget oracle-guided score-feedback reference while requiring substantially fewer turns than fixed ten-turn inference. These results demonstrate that learned self-verification can serve as an effective internal control signal for answer retention and adaptive test-time compute allocation.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Multiplicative Errors-in-Variables Models for Hyperspectral Unmixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28743)
*Vivek Singh,Peter Hoff*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对高光谱图像解混问题，提出一种层次贝叶斯乘性误差变量模型，将噪声建模为与信号成比例的对数正态分布，从而生成随信号幅度缩放的置信区间，在覆盖率和重建误差方面均优于传统加性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性混合模型假设加性同方差误差，忽略了光学测量中噪声强度与信号幅度成正比这一物理特性，导致置信区间覆盖质量差——低丰度时过度覆盖、高丰度时覆盖不足。

Method: 提出层次贝叶斯乘性误差变量（EIV）模型：采用多元对数正态分布刻画信号依赖噪声，自然捕捉波段间相关性；将端元光谱视为随机变量并引入类别特异性方差缩放；通过后验分布实现全层次模型的推断。

Result: 理论证明乘性模型下丰度向量的MLE具有恒定渐近多元变异系数，故良好校准的置信区间应与信号幅度成比例缩放。多个真实与模拟数据集实验表明，所提模型的置信区间在不同丰度水平下覆盖率优于加性模型，区间宽度随信号幅度缩放，同时重建误差相当或更优。

Conclusion: 乘性误差模型更贴合高光谱成像的物理过程，能有效克服加性模型在置信区间校准上的系统性缺陷，为高光谱解混提供了更可靠的统计推断框架。

Abstract: Estimating endmember spectra and their corresponding abundances from hyperspectral images is a fundamental inverse problem in remote sensing. The standard linear mixing model relies on additive homoscedastic errors, which misspecifies the mean-variance relationship inherent to optical measurements for which the noise intensity might be proportional to the signal magnitude. This leads to confidence intervals with poor coverage. To address these limitations, we propose a hierarchical Bayesian multiplicative Errors-in-Variables (EIV) model. Motivated by the physics of light propagation, our formulation captures signal-dependent noise through a multivariate log-normal specification that naturally accommodates dependence across spectral bands. The EIV framework treats endmember signatures as random and allows for class-specific variance scaling, yielding a flexible and physically grounded data-generating process. We establish that the MLE of the abundance vector under the multiplicative model has a constant asymptotic multivariate coefficient of variation, implying that well-calibrated confidence intervals should scale proportionally with signal magnitude. The additive model, by contrast, produces intervals of constant width regardless of signal magnitude, leading to overcoverage at low abundance and undercoverage at high abundance when the true noise is multiplicative. Practical implementation and inference for the full hierarchical model is obtained from posterior distributions over the model parameters. Experiments on several real and simulated datasets demonstrate that confidence intervals derived from the proposed model achieve improved coverage across abundance levels compared to those from a standard additive model, with widths that scale proportionally with signal magnitude, while achieving comparable or superior signal reconstruction error.

</details>


### [173] [Bits per Spike as a Betting Game: An Interpretable Unit for Held-Out Log-Likelihood in Neural Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28779)
*Alex H. Williams*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出用博弈论统计学的视角重新解释 held-out log-likelihood 指标，将其转化为"达到显著性所需时间"（time to significance, τ），以秒为单位而非比特/脉冲，使模型比较更加直观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经脉冲统计模型中常用的 held-out log-likelihood（以 bits/spike 为单位）在直觉上难以理解——例如难以判断 0.34 bits/spike 的改进是否重要。作者希望提供一个更直观的可解释度量。

Method: 将拟合模型 Q 视为一个赌徒，在基线模型 B 设定的价格下对每次观测下注。最优 Kelly 策略下，财富增长过程为似然比 q/b 的累积，期望对数似然比 L 即财富的指数增长率。财富过程在原假设下是非负鞅，利用 Ville 不等式可构造 anytime-valid 检验：当财富超过 1/α 时拒绝基线。由此定义 τ = −Δ·log(α)/L，即平均需要多少秒记录才能拒绝基线。

Result: τ 与 bits/spike 排序完全一致，但以秒为单位更直观。在小鼠前丘脑头方向细胞的实验中，强调谐细胞约 120 ms 即可达到显著性，而中等调谐细胞需约 11 s。

Conclusion: τ 并非新统计量，而是现有指标的新单位（秒），更便于直觉理解模型间的差异实质。

Abstract: Held-out log-likelihood is the standard currency for comparing statistical models of neural spike trains, and is often reported as bits per spike relative to a homogeneous Poisson baseline. The units of this metric are difficult to reason about: it is rarely obvious whether an improvement of, say, $0.34$ bits per spike is a large effect or a negligible one. This note develops an interpretation of held-out log-likelihood borrowed from game-theoretic statistics. A fitted model $Q$ is treated as a player who bets on each upcoming observation at prices set by a baseline model $B$. Under the optimal (Kelly) betting strategy the player's contract function is exactly the likelihood ratio $q/b$, and the expected log-likelihood ratio $L$ is the exponential growth rate of the player's wealth. Because the wealth process is a nonnegative martingale under the null hypothesis that $B$ generated the data, Ville's inequality turns it into an anytime-valid test: the baseline may be rejected at level $α$ as soon as wealth exceeds $1/α$. This yields a simple summary statistic, the time to significance $τΔ= -Δ\log(α) / L$, which is the amount of held-out recording needed on average to reject the baseline at level $α$. Since $τ$ is a strictly decreasing function of $L$, it ranks models identically to bits per spike; it is not a new statistic but a more interpretable unit for an existing one, expressed in seconds of recording rather than in bits. We illustrate the construction on head-direction cells recorded in mouse anterior thalamus, where a generalized linear model reaches significance against a homogeneous Poisson baseline in roughly $120$ ms of held-out data for a strongly tuned cell and roughly $11$ s for a moderately tuned cell.

</details>


### [174] [How should we select test-negative controls? A causal perspective in the era of multiplex respiratory testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28792)
*Christopher B. Boyer,Kendrick Qijun Li,Xu Shi,Marc Lipsitch,Eric J. Tchetgen Tchetgen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文分析了测试阴性设计(TND)中"哪些测试阴性疾病构成有效对照"这一核心问题。基于因果识别的最新成果，作者提出了两种对照选择的理解框架（抽样观与偏倚校正观），并利用多重PCR检测信息开发了对照选择的分类体系和三项实用原则，通过模拟研究验证了所提方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试阴性设计中，对照组的合理选择一直是被低估的关键环节。多重PCR检测技术的普及使得研究者能够识别测试阴性患者中的特定非目标病原体，但这同时揭示了对照结局的异质性——不同对照结局可能满足或违反因果假设。目前缺乏系统框架来指导如何在多重检测信息下选择和筛选对照，这可能导致疫苗有效性估计出现严重偏倚。

Method: (1) 理论基础整合：综合TND因果识别的最新结果，提出两种对照选择视角——抽样观（对照代表源人群）和偏倚校正观（对照作为负对照结局，在等混杂条件下校正偏倚）。(2) 分类框架：建立基于多重检测的对照分类体系，区分"暴露代理"对照（与感染的决定因素共享未测量变量）和"检测代理"对照（与就医行为的决定因素共享未测量变量）。(3) 三项对照选择原则：疫苗无关性、避免与其他干预措施纠缠、检测过程可比性。(4) 模拟验证：在9种场景下，比较标准汇总估计量与预先指定的病原体筛选估计量的表现。

Result: 模拟研究表明，当违反假设的情况集中在某个单一对照病原体时，标准汇总TND估计量可能产生显著偏倚；而采用预先指定的病原体筛选估计量则能保持无偏。研究还形式化了多重检测带来的细微问题（包括共检出和全阴性病例），并明确了标准汇总估计量何时仍然有效、何时需要替代估计量。

Conclusion: 多重PCR检测既带来了对照异质性的挑战，也提供了更好地表征和筛选对照的机遇。研究者应根据疫苗无关性、避免干预纠缠和检测过程可比性三项原则，结合所提出的分类框架，审慎选择TND中的对照，以确保疫苗有效性估计的因果有效性。

Abstract: The test-negative design (TND) is widely used to estimate vaccine effectiveness (VE) for respiratory pathogens by comparing vaccination odds among test-positive cases versus test-negative controls. A central yet underexplored design element is which test-negative illnesses constitute valid controls. With rapid multiplex PCR panels, investigators can now identify specific non-focal pathogens among test-negative patients, allowing for better characterization of ``test-negative illness'', but also revealing a mixture of control outcomes that may each satisfy or violate causal assumptions. We synthesize recent causal identification results for the TND and show that they imply two distinct interpretations of control selection: 1) a sampling view in which controls represent the source population and 2) a bias-correction view in which controls function as negative control outcomes under equi-confounding. Building on these interpretations, we develop a framework for multiplex-informed control selection. We propose a taxonomy that distinguishes controls that serve primarily as exposure proxies (sharing unmeasured determinants of infection) from those that serve as testing proxies (sharing unmeasured determinants of care-seeking), derive implications for pathogen-specific and pooled estimators, and suggest three practical principles for control selection: vaccine irrelevance, avoidance of entanglement with other interventions, and testing-process comparability. We also formalize nuances introduced by multiplex panels, including co-detections and pan-negative episodes, and outline when standard pooled estimators remain valid versus when alternative estimators are needed. In simulations across 9 scenarios, we demonstrate violations concentrated in a single control pathogen can substantially bias pooled TND estimates, whereas a pre-specified pathogen screening estimator remained unbiased.

</details>


### [175] [Bayesian Mediation Analysis for Individualized Treatment Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28804)
*Emmanuel M. Rockwell,Patrick J. Smith,Michael R. Kosorok,Nikki L. B. Freeman*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果中介分析框架，用于将个体化治疗规则（ITR）的价值对比分解为直接效应和间接效应，从而解释ITR的获益是如何通过不同路径产生的。作者定义了自然直接和间接规则效应，给出了识别条件（规则级中介g-公式），并采用贝叶斯因果中介森林进行估计，通过模拟和TRIUMPH试验数据验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ITR的价值（value）仅能评估平均临床获益，但无法解释该获益是通过何种机制或路径实现的。研究者需要一个能够将ITR与参考规则之间的价值差异分解为直接路径和经由特定中介变量的间接路径的分析框架，以提供ITR的机制性解释。

Method: 作者提出基于规则特异性嵌套潜在结果的因果中介框架，定义自然直接规则效应和自然间接规则效应；给出通过规则级中介g-公式进行识别的条件；在估计层面，采用贝叶斯因果中介森林（Bayesian causal mediation forests）进行后验推断，获得价值对比及其路径特异性成分的估计与 credible interval。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在具有不同直接和中介贡献程度的场景下，随着样本量增大，所提估计量的偏差和均方根误差均减小，且 credible interval 覆盖率接近名义水平。在TRIUMPH试验数据中，该方法将生活方式干预规则的认知获益分解为通过神经血管、心肺和行为等候选中介变量的路径特异性效应。

Conclusion: 该框架将最优ITR学习与中介机制解释相结合，为ITR的机制性评估提供了一种自然的方法论补充，使研究者不仅能评估ITR是否有效，还能理解其如何产生效果。

Abstract: The value of an individualized treatment rule (ITR), defined as the expected outcome under treatment assignment according to the rule, is useful for assessing average clinical benefit but does not explain how the benefit of a rule is generated. We propose a causal mediation framework for decomposing the value contrast between a prespecified candidate ITR and a clinically meaningful reference rule into direct and indirect components. Using rule-specific nested potential outcomes, we define natural direct and indirect rule effects that quantify the extent to which the improvement in value arises through pathways operating directly on the outcome versus through a specified mediator. We give identification conditions under which these components are identified by a rule-level mediation g-formula. For estimation, we adapt Bayesian causal mediation forests to obtain posterior inference for the value contrast and its path-specific components. Our simulations demonstrate that the proposed estimator achieved near-nominal credible interval coverage with decreasing bias and root mean squared error as sample size increased in settings with varying direct and mediated contributions. We further illustrate the method using data from the TRIUMPH trial, decomposing the cognitive benefit of a lifestyle intervention rule through candidate neurovascular, cardiorespiratory, and behavioral mediators. The proposed framework complements optimal ITR learning with explanation using mediation, providing a natural approach for mechanistic evaluation of ITRs.

</details>


### [176] [Upper Confidence Bounds for the Prediction Error of Kernel Ridge Regression via Gaussian Refitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28846)
*Yijin Ni,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为核岭回归提出了一种基于高斯重拟合的模型拟合评估方法，利用Anderson不等式构造可计算的尾部置信界。该界在仅需对称噪声、无需矩假设的条件下，能以极小极大速率收缩，且在实际数据驱动包络下实现紧致覆盖，大幅优于传统交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交叉验证方法给出的置信界受噪声波动限制，收敛速度仅为$O(n^{-1/2})$，无法匹配预测误差更快的收敛速率。已有的wild重拟合方法虽可消除该噪声层级，但基于Rademacher符号的方法在核岭回归中退化且依赖不可观测量。因此需要一种适用于核岭回归、仅需弱假设且可计算的有效置信界方法。

Method: 提出高斯重拟合(Gaussian refit)方法：对核岭回归，利用Anderson不等式证明拟合移动量关于噪声大小单调，从而构造可计算的尾部置信界。该方法仅假设噪声对称分布，无需矩条件，通过次序统计量实现任意置信水平的校准。理论上采用最坏情况包络分析收缩速率，实践上使用数据驱动的包络进行覆盖评估。

Result: 理论上，最坏情况包络下置信界以极小极大速率$O_P(n^{-2s/(2s+1)})$收缩，正确匹配预测误差。实证上，数据驱动包络在真实95%误差分位数两倍范围内保持完全覆盖；而交叉验证超出该分位数最多达51倍，在无限方差噪声下达数百倍。方法还成功扩展到非线性约束估计和真实空间数据。

Conclusion: 所提高斯重拟合方法在核岭回归的模型拟合评估中，以极弱假设（仅对称噪声）实现了理论上最优的收缩速率和实践中显著优于交叉验证的紧致覆盖，且具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Assessing a single model fit requires a computable upper confidence bound for the gap between the fit and the unknown truth, as mean estimates ignore realization variance. Standard cross-validation margins are bottlenecked at order $n^{-1/2}$ by noise fluctuations, even when the true error shrinks faster. While wild refitting cancels this noise level, existing Rademacher sign methods degenerate for kernel ridge regression and rely on unobservable quantities.
  We propose a Gaussian refit for kernel ridge regression. By Anderson's inequality, the fit movement is monotone in the noise sizes, yielding a computable tail bound. Assuming only symmetric noise, the bound requires no moment assumptions and is calibrated at any confidence level via order statistics. Theoretically, using a worst-case envelope, the bound contracts at the minimax rate $O_P(n^{-2s/(2s+1)})$, correctly matching the prediction error. Empirically, using a practical data-driven envelope, the bound maintains full coverage within twice the true $95\%$ error quantile. By contrast, cross-validation exceeds this quantile by factors up to $51$, and by hundreds under infinite-variance noise. The procedure extends empirically to nonlinear constrained estimators and real spatial data.

</details>


### [177] [The Debiased Score Test: Hunt-and-test for Semiparametric Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28861)
*Aditya Dhawan,F. Richard Guo,Rajen D. Shah*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于参数得分检验思想的半参数回归模型拟合优度检验框架。通过将回归函数视为风险最小化器，检验其是否属于某个线性函数类。该方法采用"搜寻-检验"策略：在一部分数据上用机器学习识别经验得分中的有前景方向，在另一部分数据上检验得分是否在该方向上消失，并通过加权最小二乘投影进行去偏校正。方法在温和条件下控制第I类错误，且当搜寻方向与真实得分相关时具有检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参数得分检验依赖对数似然的导数，其期望在原假设下为零。作者旨在将这一思想推广到回归函数为非参数或半参数设定的场景，以检验回归函数是否属于某个给定的线性函数类，从而为广义可加模型、部分线性模型等常见半参数回归模型提供统一的拟合优度检验框架，并用于检测观察性研究中的效应修饰因子。

Method: 提出"搜寻-检验"（hunt-and-test）策略：将数据随机分成两部分。第一部分（hunt阶段）在拟合原假设模型后，利用机器学习方法在经验得分中识别一个有前景的方向；第二部分（test阶段）检验得分是否在该方向上消失。为处理原假设模型估计误差，采用基于加权最小二乘投影的去偏校正。最终构建一个在温和条件下控制第I类错误、且当搜寻方向与真实得分相关时具有检验功效的检验统计量。

Result: 理论方面，在相对温和的条件下建立了第I类错误控制，并证明当搜寻方向与真实得分相关时检验具有功效。模拟实验和实际数据应用展示了良好的性能，包括在HIV临床试验中成功识别效应修饰因子，以及在保险索赔数据中评估可加模型的适用性。方法已在R包dScoreTest中实现。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将参数得分检验推广到半参数回归模型的拟合优度检验，构建了一个灵活且理论上严谨的检验方法。通过数据分割与机器学习相结合的"搜寻-检验"策略，以及去偏校正技术，实现了对多种半参数模型的有效检验，在效应修饰因子检测等实际应用中表现优异。

Abstract: The parametric score test assesses a hypothesis through derivatives of the log-likelihood, whose expectation vanishes under the null. When the parameter of interest is a regression function identified as a risk minimiser, we extend this idea to test whether it belongs to a given linear function class. This yields goodness-of-fit tests for common semiparametric regression models, including generalised additive and partially linear models. Suitably formulated, the framework also detects effect modifiers in observational studies. We propose a hunt-and-test strategy that splits the data into two: on one part, after fitting the null model, machine learning is used to identify a promising direction in the empirical scores; on the other, we test whether the score vanishes in that direction. To account for error in estimating the null model, we apply a debiasing correction based on a weighted least squares projection. We establish Type I error control under relatively mild conditions and show the test has power whenever the hunted direction is correlated with the true score. Simulations and real-data examples demonstrate favourable performance, including identifying effect modifiers in an HIV clinical trial and assessing an additive model for insurance claims. The methodology is implemented in the R package dScoreTest.

</details>


### [178] [Active Learning for Data-Efficient Calibration of Stochastic Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28874)
*Özge Sürer*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对昂贵随机仿真的主动学习校准框架，通过自适应平衡探索（新输入点）与重复（已有输入点的重复运行），以最少仿真次数高效学习未知参数的后验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机仿真模型的校准面临双重挑战：既要探索新的输入位置以学习输入-输出关系，又需在已有输入点进行重复运行以应对输出噪声。现有方法未能有效平衡探索与重复的权衡，导致在昂贵仿真场景下数据效率低下。

Method: 提出基于不确定性的主动学习框架，以学习仿真参数后验密度为目标。推导出两种形式的采集函数——分别对应探索和重复；在此基础上设计了序贯设计策略，在每一阶段选择探索或重复，以最大程度降低参数后验密度估计的不确定性。

Result: 在合成基准测试和真实流行病学模型上的实验表明，该方法在显著减少所需仿真次数的同时，提升了参数后验分布的学习效果。

Conclusion: 该自适应探索-重复平衡策略在昂贵随机仿真校准中具有显著优势，能够以更少的仿真运行获得更准确的后验推断。

Abstract: Simulation-based calibration aims to infer unknown parameters of complex simulation models by aligning model outputs with real-world observations. When simulation runs are computationally expensive, statistical emulators trained on simulation data are used to efficiently approximate the model. An intelligent, adaptive selection of simulation inputs for building the emulator can substantially improve the efficiency of the calibration process. This task is particularly challenging for stochastic simulations with noisy outputs, since both selecting new input locations (exploration) and allocating repeated runs at existing inputs (replication) are essential for efficiently learning the input-output relationship. In this paper, we introduce an active learning framework that adaptively balances exploration and replication for data-efficient calibration. Our uncertainty-aware acquisition criterion targets learning the posterior density of the unknown simulation parameters, and we derive two corresponding forms of the acquisition function for exploration and replication. Building on these, we propose a strategy that, at each stage of the sequential design, chooses between exploration and replication to most effectively reduce the uncertainty in the estimate of the posterior density of the simulation parameters. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and a real epidemiological model demonstrate that our approach significantly improves learning of the posterior distribution of the simulation parameters while reducing the number of required simulations, making it well-suited for expensive stochastic simulation settings.

</details>


### [179] [Log-F-penalized Conditional Logistic Regression for Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28899)
*Ying Yu,Jiying Wen,Jinko Graham,Brad McNeney*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于独立 log-F 先验的惩罚条件 logistic 回归方法，用于解决稀疏匹配病例对照研究中条件最大似然估计的偏倚问题。该方法允许研究者通过可解释的先验假设来校准收缩程度，可通过数据增强在标准软件中实现，在模拟研究中表现出与 Firth 方法相当的置信区间覆盖率，且均方误差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准条件最大似然估计在小型或稀疏匹配病例对照研究中存在远离零的偏倚。Firth 惩罚似然方法虽被广泛使用且频率性质良好，但无法对单个回归系数的收缩程度提供精细控制。因此需要一种既能纠正偏倚、又能让分析者灵活校准收缩程度的方法。

Method: 作者提出用独立的 log-F 分布对条件似然进行惩罚，构建点估计和区间估计。log-F 惩罚方法允许通过可解释的效应量先验假设来校准收缩程度。该方法可通过数据增强（data augmentation）在标准条件 logistic 回归软件中实现，并提供了实际收缩量校准的指导。

Result: 通过两个实例（DES暴露与阴道癌风险、2型糖尿病遗传关联研究）和模拟研究进行验证。模拟中，log-F 惩罚估计量的置信区间覆盖率与 Firth 方法相当，均方误差更低，且第I类错误率和检验效能相似。

Conclusion: log-F 惩罚条件 logistic 回归适用于稀疏匹配和分层研究中的推断，在保持良好频率性质的同时提供了更灵活的收缩控制，优于传统的 Firth 方法。

Abstract: We investigate penalized likelihood methods for estimation and inference in conditional logistic regression. The standard conditional maximum likelihood estimator is known to be biased away from zero in small or sparse matched case-control studies. A widely used remedy is Firth's penalized likelihood approach, which has good frequentist operating characteristics but provides limited control over the degree of shrinkage applied to individual regression coefficients. We develop point and interval estimators by penalizing the conditional likelihood with independent log-$F$ distributions. The log-\(F\)-penalized approach allows analysts to calibrate shrinkage using interpretable prior assumptions about plausible effect sizes. We also provide practical guidance for calibrating the amount of shrinkage and show that the method can be implemented through data augmentation using standard conditional logistic regression software. We illustrate the methods using data from (i) a study of maternal exposure to diethylstilbestrol and the risk of vaginal cancer in daughters, and (ii) a genetic association study of type 2 diabetes. We then compare the log-$F$-penalized approach with Firth's penalized likelihood method in a simulation study. In simulations, the log-$F$-penalized estimators had confidence-interval coverage comparable to that of Firth's method and lower mean squared error, with similar type~1 error rates and power. These results support the use of log-$F$-penalized conditional logistic regression for inference in sparse matched and stratified studies.

</details>


### [180] [Comparing Two Survival Functions at a Fixed Timepoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28929)
*Michael P. Fay,Allyson Mateja,Megan C. Grieco*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了右删失下两个生存曲线在固定时间点比较的置信区间方法。作者将Greenwood方差调整整合到两样本delta方法中，并扩展了基于beta乘积置信过程(BPCP)的melding方法，引入mid-p版本以求获得更接近名义水平的覆盖率。结果表明，只有melding on BPCP能在所有场景下保证覆盖率，mid-p版本通常最接近名义覆盖率，相关R函数已整合到bpcp包中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在右删失生存数据的固定时间点比较中，基于Kaplan-Meier估计和Greenwood方差的标准渐近方法在小样本、重删失或KM估计值为0或1时，置信区间会出现崩溃或覆盖率不足的问题。现有方法虽有针对单样本的Greenwood方差调整，但尚未整合到两样本delta方法中。此外，melding on BPCP虽能保证覆盖率但过于保守，需要改进以获得更接近名义水平的覆盖率。

Method: 1) 将单样本Greenwood方差调整整合到两样本delta方法中，并对KM估计为0或1的情况提供修正；2) 扩展melding on BPCP方法，引入mid-p版本；3) 通过数值方法和模拟研究评估各方法的覆盖率表现。

Result: 仅melding on BPCP能在所有研究场景下保证名义覆盖率；mid-p版本的BPCP melding在大多数情况下覆盖率最接近名义水平；无删失时，melding on BPCP的p值与Fisher精确检验等价，但可能非常保守。

Conclusion: 对于右删失下两样本固定时间点的生存曲线比较，melding on BPCP是唯一能保证覆盖率的方法，其mid-p版本在实践中提供了更接近名义水平的覆盖率，推荐使用bpcp R包中的相关函数进行分析。

Abstract: For comparing two survival curves at a fixed timepoint with right censoring, a standard method uses asymptotic methods on two Kaplan-Meier estimators with Greenwood variance estimators and transformations using the delta method. Confidence intervals on associated estimands can either breakdown or undercover due to Kaplan-Meier estimates of zero or one, small sample sizes, or heavy censoring. Although others have proposed adjustments to the Greenwood variance for a single sample, these adjustments have not been incorporated into the two-sample application of the delta method and we do that here as well as provide modifications when Kaplan-Meier estimates are zero or one. An alternative non-asymptotic existing method called melding on the beta product confidence procedure (BPCP) appears to have at least nominal coverage regardless of the sample size. Without censoring the problem reduces to comparing two independent binomials and melding on the BPCP gives p-values equivalent to Fisher's exact test p-values and further gives compatible confidence intervals for treatment comparison estimands; however, the melding can be very conservative. In this paper, we expand the melding on the BPCP intervals to include mid-p versions, which are designed to achieve coverage closer to nominal without guaranteeing coverage for all cases. We study these existing methods and our minor modifications of them primarily by numerical methods or simulation. Results suggest only the melding on the BPCP can guarantee coverage in all studied scenarios, and the mid-p version most often has closest to nominal coverage. We provide R functions in the bpcp R package.

</details>


### [181] [Distance Profile Embedding for Independence and Conditional Independence Testing of Random Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28981)
*Wenxi Tan,Bing Li,Lingzhou Xue*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出距离轮廓嵌入（DPE），将一般度量空间中的随机对象映射到平方可积函数的Hilbert空间，并基于此构建统一的边缘与条件独立性检验框架。该方法首次支持对象值条件变量，具有严谨的渐近理论，并通过闭式渐近零分布计算解析p值，避免了置换检验的计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
