Motivation: 非欧随机对象的独立性或条件独立性检验是统计推断的基础问题，但现有方法在几何灵活性与理论可处理性之间存在难以调和的权衡。尤其是条件独立性检验中，现有方法无法处理对象值的条件变量（通常局限于欧氏或Hilbert空间），且基于置换检验的方法计算负担沉重。

Method: 作者提出距离轮廓嵌入（DPE），将一般度量空间中的随机对象映射到平方可积函数的Hilbert空间中，并证明该映射是单射且保留完整分布信息，无需等距Hilbert嵌入或一一对应条件。基于DPE，构建统一的边缘独立性与条件独立性检验框架，并推导出闭式渐近零分布用于计算解析p值。

Result: 理论层面，该方法享有对检验水平和功效的严谨渐近理论；首次在文献中实现对对象值条件变量的条件独立性检验。数值层面，通过模拟实验和两个真实应用（肠道微生物组构成与全球人类死亡率分布）验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DPE框架成功弥合了几何灵活性与理论严谨性之间的鸿沟，为非欧随机对象的独立性检验提供了统一、高效且理论完备的解决方案，尤其突破了条件变量必须为欧氏或Hilbert空间的限制。

Abstract: Testing independence or conditional independence is fundamental to statistical inference, yet existing methods for non-Euclidean random objects often face a difficult trade-off between geometric flexibility and theoretical tractability. We introduce the Distance Profile Embedding (DPE), a novel representation that maps random objects from general metric spaces into a Hilbert space of square-integrable functions. We prove that this mapping is injective and preserves full distributional information without requiring isometric Hilbert embeddings or one-to-one correspondence conditions. Leveraging the DPE, we develop a unified framework for marginal and conditional independence testing of random objects that enjoys a rigorous asymptotic theory for both size and power. Notably, our framework is the first in the literature to accommodate object-valued conditioning variables when testing conditional independence, overcoming the Euclidean or Hilbertian constraints of existing methodologies. We facilitate the calculation of analytic $p$-values using closed-form asymptotic null distributions, which avoids the computational burden of permutation tests common in existing metric-based methods. The numerical properties of our methods are demonstrated through both simulations and two real-world applications involving gut microbiome compositions and global human mortality distributions, respectively.

</details>


### [182] [Frequentist-calibrated Bayesian group sequential design with dynamic borrowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29108)
*Francesco Mariani,Shirin Golchi,Stefania Gubbiotti,Fulvio De Santis*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯群序贯设计方法，通过后验几率与一致最优势检验之间的显式对应关系实现动态借用历史信息，在满足频率论I类错误控制的同时保留统计功效增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯方法在临床试验中应用日益广泛，但监管机构仍要求评估频率论操作特性（I类错误和功效）。传统上，当从外部来源借用信息时，严格控制频率论I类错误率会抵消借用效果，导致功效无法提升。因此需要一种既能满足监管要求、又能有效利用历史信息的设计方法。

Method: 提出贝叶斯群序贯设计（Bayesian group sequential design），核心是利用后验几率（posterior odds）与频率论一致最优势（UMP）检验之间的显式对应关系。在每次期中分析时提供两个证据阈值：一个精确复现频率论UMP决策；另一个允许研究者适当纳入历史信息。该方法实现动态借用（dynamic borrowing）。

Result: 数值研究表明该方法在控制I类错误的同时能够获得功效增益。将该框架应用于一项III期结核病预防试验设计，成功整合了成人和儿童的历史试验数据。

Conclusion: 该方法通过双重阈值策略，在满足监管机构频率论要求与充分利用历史信息之间取得平衡，为临床试验设计提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Bayesian analysis is increasingly used in clinical trials. However, assessment of the design with respect to the frequentist operating characteristics, such as type I error and power, remains a regulatory requirement in many cases. It is well established that, when information is borrowed from external sources to the trial, imposing strict frequentist type I error rate control is equivalent to offsetting the borrowing, which results in no power gains. We propose a Bayesian group sequential design with dynamic borrowing that exploits an explicit correspondence between Bayesian decision criteria based on posterior odds and frequentist uniformly most powerful (UMP) tests. At each interim analysis, two evidential thresholds are made available: the one that exactly retrieves the frequentist UMP decision; the other, that allows the investigator to incorporate historical information when appropriate. We assess the performance of the proposed approach in numerical studies, and apply the framework to the design of a phase III tuberculosis prevention trial, incorporating historical adult and pediatric trial data.

</details>


### [183] [Local spectral clustering for heterogeneous clustering structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29147)
*Yuanxing Chen,Qingzhao Zhang,Yuhong Yang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种频率学派（frequentist）的局部聚类框架，将高维数据中异质性特征的聚类问题转化为"聚类之聚类"（clustering-of-clusterings）问题，通过特征分组与局部谱聚类联合识别不同特征子集所对应的异质样本划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典聚类方法假设所有信息性特征支持单一的样本划分，但现代高维数据中不同特征子集可能编码不同的相似性概念、诱导异质样本分区，同时部分特征可能不含任何聚类信息。这一假设过于严格，亟需能够同时识别特征分组及其对应聚类结构的方法。

Method: 在异质次高斯混合模型下，作者构建特征特定的高斯核相似矩阵，提出基于聚类矩阵优化准则的局部谱聚类方法。核心思路是：用标签不变的聚类矩阵表示各样本划分，根据特征间共享的聚类结构对其进行分组，从而将局部聚类重新表述为特征分组问题（聚类之聚类）。该方法无需显式似然或贝叶斯后验计算。

Result: 广泛的模拟实验和实际应用表明，该方法在异质性特征分布场景下表现出色，能够有效容纳非信息性特征，且相比现有方法具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的局部聚类框架突破了传统方法"单一全局划分"的限制，为高维异质性数据提供了一种灵活、可扩展的聚类解决方案，在理论和实践中均展现出良好性能。

Abstract: Classical clustering methods typically assume that all informative features support a single latent partition of the observations. This assumption can be overly restrictive for modern high-dimensional data, where different subsets of features may encode distinct notions of similarity and induce heterogeneous sample partitions, while some features may contain no meaningful clustering information. We develop a frequentist framework for local clustering that simultaneously identifies feature groups and estimates the sample clustering structure associated with each group. Our approach represents each sample partition by a label-invariant clustering matrix and groups features according to their shared clustering structures, thereby reformulating local clustering as a feature-grouping, or clustering-of-clusterings, problem. Under a heterogeneous sub-Gaussian mixture model, we construct feature-specific Gaussian-kernel similarity matrices and propose a local spectral clustering procedure based on a clustering-matrix optimization criterion. The proposed method avoids explicit likelihood specification and Bayesian posterior computation, accommodates heterogeneous feature distributions, and permits the presence of non-informative features. Extensive simulations and applications further demonstrate the practical utility and superiority of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [184] [Mixed Frequency Stochastic Frontier Model: with application to the linkage of weather extremes and firm efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29189)
*Erniel B. Barrios,Nur Syazwani Mazlan,Lim Foo Weng,Paolo Victor T. Redondo,Gian Karlo M. Torreno,Lee How Chinh*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将高频效率决定因素纳入低频随机前沿模型的新策略，通过逻辑函数设定无效率方程并使用非参数函数引入高频变量，采用回代算法进行混合估计，规避了MLE的收敛和分布假设问题。该方法应用于菲律宾电力合作社数据，刻画企业效率与极端天气事件的关联，为气候变化背景下微观可持续性评估提供新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机前沿模型面临两大挑战：一是生产率指标通常以低频（如年度）编制，而无效率的驱动因素可能以更高频率变化，需要有效整合不同频率数据；二是模型常遭遇正偏度问题，迫使研究者采用更复杂的无效率项分布假设。此外，MLE估计容易陷入收敛困难且对分布假设敏感。

Method: 1）为无效率方程设定逻辑函数形式，避免复杂分布假设，同时解决正偏度问题；2）以非参数函数形式将高频决定因素纳入无效率方程；3）采用混合方法结合回代算法（backfitting algorithm）进行模型估计，替代传统MLE。

Result: 该方法成功应用于菲律宾电力合作社数据，有效刻画了企业效率与极端天气事件之间的关联，验证了模型在实证中的可行性和解释力。

Conclusion: 该策略为将高频驱动因素引入低频随机前沿分析提供了方法论创新，克服了MLE的估计难题和分布假设脆弱性，并展示了其在气候变化背景下微观可持续性评估中的应用潜力。

Abstract: A strategy for incorporating higher-frequency determinants of inefficiency into the stochastic frontier models with production function indicators compiled at lower frequencies is proposed. Furthermore, the problem with positive skewness in stochastic frontier models is resolved by postulating a logistic function for the inefficiency equation and refraining from assuming a more complicated distribution of the inefficiency term. High frequency determinants are incorporated into the inefficiency equation in a nonparametric function. The model is estimated with hybrid methods in a backfitting algorithm instead of maximum likelihood estimation (MLE), which typically struggles with convergence issues and is vulnerable to distributional assumptions. The model is then used to characterize the linkage between firm efficiency and extreme weather events using data from electric cooperatives in the Philippines. This provides an approach to assess micro level sustainability issues from extreme weather arising events from climate change.

</details>


### [185] [Bayesian fusion forests for heterogeneous treatment effects on survival from randomised and real-world data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29295)
*Tijn Jacobs,Stéphanie L. van der Pas,Wessel N. van Wieringen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯融合森林框架，通过结合RCT和真实世界数据，在放宽无混杂假设的条件下，利用加速失效时间模型和贝叶斯树先验估计生存结局的异质性处理效应，特别支持右删失和区间删失数据的融合分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于单一RCT的生存分析效率有限，而融合真实世界数据面临混杂偏倚和来源间异质性的挑战。现有方法多依赖严格的无混杂假设，且难以处理右删失和区间删失的生存数据。需要一种能同时解决混杂、异质性和删失问题的灵活非参数融合框架。

Method: 构建加速失效时间(AFT)模型，将生存时间分解为四个组件：共享基线预后（跨源借力）、源特异性偏差（捕获异质性）、处理效应和混杂函数（吸收RWD中的混杂偏倚）。各组件均赋予贝叶斯树集成先验（BART），误差分布采用层次狄利克雷过程混合模型进行非参数建模。假设处理效应可在两个数据源间传输，以此替代RWD的无混杂假设。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在不同混杂强度和来源间异质性水平下，融合分析相比仅使用RCT的分析均取得效率增益。在ACTG 175试验与MACS队列的实证中，融合分析识别出联合抗逆转录病毒治疗对几乎所有患者均有益，而单独试验分析则无法得出确证结论。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯融合森林框架通过允许处理效应跨源传输而非强求无混杂假设，成功实现了RCT与RWD在生存分析中的有效融合，显著提升了估计效率，在处理删失数据方面具有独特优势。

Abstract: We develop the Bayesian fusion forest, a nonparametric framework to estimate heterogeneous treatment effects on survival outcomes by combining a randomised controlled trial and real-world data. The framework relaxes the unconfoundedness assumption on the real-world data by assuming instead that the treatment effect transports across the two sources. Our method opens up right- and interval-censored outcomes to data fusion. We model the survival time with an accelerated failure time decomposition into a shared baseline prognosis, a source-specific deviation, a treatment effect, and a confounding function. The confounding function absorbs the confounding bias in the real-world data. Each component receives a Bayesian tree ensemble prior. The shared baseline prognosis borrows strength across sources, while the deviation captures between-source heterogeneity. A hierarchical Dirichlet process mixture models the error distribution nonparametrically. A simulation study shows efficiency gains over a trial-only analysis across varying levels of confounding and between-source heterogeneity. We combine the ACTG 175 trial with the Multicenter AIDS Cohort Study to estimate the effect of combination antiretroviral therapy for HIV. The fusion identifies a benefit for nearly every patient whereas the trial alone is inconclusive.

</details>


### [186] [Hierarchical Clustering of Networks via Hierarchical Distance Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29296)
*Li Chen,Nathaniel Josephs,Eric D. Kolaczyk,Lizhen Lin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了层次距离矩阵（Hierarchical Distance Matrix）框架，用于刻画网络群体的潜在层次组织，并设计了基于双样本检验的网络层次聚类算法（NHC-TST），该算法自顶向下地通过谱聚类递归分裂网络群体，利用图双样本检验作为停止规则，无需预设聚类数或树深度，理论和模拟均证明其能精确恢复层次结构，并在全球移民数据中发现了传统扁平静态聚类无法揭示的多分辨率时间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络群体聚类中恢复潜在层次组织是一个基础但尚未被充分探索的问题。现有的群体级聚类方法大多假设扁平静态结构，无法捕捉网络群体中递归嵌套的层次关系，且往往需要预设聚类数目。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出一个能够同时进行聚类并自适应推断层次树结构的统一框架。

Method: （1）形式化定义层次距离矩阵：一类编码递归嵌套距离分离的群体级距离矩阵，可容纳不平衡树深度。（2）提出NHC-TST算法：自顶向下的递归分裂过程，每层使用谱聚类进行二分，并采用基于图的双样本检验（graph-based two-sample test）作为停止规则，自适应确定树的分支结构与深度。（3）理论分析：建立群体层次结构的精确恢复条件，并证明经验过程的统计一致性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，NHC-TST在多种设定下均能高精度地恢复聚类成员身份与层次关系。在全球移民数据集的应用中，该方法揭示了具有可解释性的多分辨率时间结构，而传统的扁平聚类方法无法发现这些层次化的模式。

Conclusion: NHC-TST为网络群体的层次聚类提供了一个完全数据驱动、理论有保障的解决方案，能够在不预设聚类数和树深度的情况下同时恢复聚类结构与层次关系，在模拟和实际数据中均展现出优越的发现能力。

Abstract: Clustering populations of networks while recovering their latent hierarchical organization is a fundamental yet largely unexplored problem in network analysis.
  To formalize this, we introduce the Hierarchical Distance Matrix, a specific class of population-level distance matrices that encodes latent hierarchical organization through recursively nested distance separation, accommodating unbalanced tree depths.
  Building on this framework, we propose a fully data-driven top-down procedure: network hierarchical clustering based on two-sample testing (NHC-TST). The algorithm recursively splits networks via spectral clustering and uses a graph-based two-sample stopping rule. The procedure adaptively determines the branching structure without requiring prior knowledge of the number of clusters or tree depth.
  Theoretically, we establish exact recovery of the population-level hierarchical structure and statistical consistency in the empirical procedure.
  Simulation studies demonstrate highly accurate recovery of both cluster memberships and hierarchical relationships across a wide range of settings. Applied to a global migration dataset, NHC-TST uncovers interpretable multi-resolution temporal structures that are not revealed by conventional flat clustering approaches.

</details>


### [187] [Predicting Bot Vulnerability from Posting Trajectories: Censored Functional Regression under Informative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29415)
*Jake Koerner,Ana-Maria Staicu,Caitrin Murphy,Eric Laber,Mihai Nicola,William Rand,Zakaria Babutsidze*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出SoCIFR框架，用于在删失且信息性设计下进行标量对函数回归，扩展至匹配病例对照设计及多函数预测变量，并通过仿真和社交媒体机器人交互预测应用验证方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代纵向数据和数字数据应用中，函数型数据常面临删失和信息性抽样设计问题，但现有函数型数据文献对该问题研究不足，亟需发展新方法来处理此类复杂数据结构。

Method: 提出SoCIFR（Scalar-on Censored Informative-design Functional Regression）框架：首先解决删失信息性设计下的估计与预测问题；扩展至匹配病例对照设计；进一步推广到多函数预测变量情形，可同时处理删失与未删失轨迹、信息性与非信息性抽样设计。

Result: 仿真研究表明所提方法在多种数据生成场景下表现良好；在社交媒体行为轨迹的动机应用中，成功预测用户在未来不同时间窗口内对自动化（机器人）交互的易感性。

Conclusion: SoCIFR框架有效填补了函数型回归在删失信息性设计下的方法学空白，具有良好的扩展性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we propose a novel framework for Scalar-on Censored Informative-design Functional Regression or SoCIFR. This setting is increasingly common in modern longitudinal and digital data applications but remains underdeveloped in functional data literature. We first discuss estimation and prediction in SoCIFR and extend the methodology to accommodate a matched case-control design. The proposed methodology is further generalized to handle multiple functional predictors, allowing for both censored and uncensored trajectories, observed under informative or non-informative sampling designs. Through simulation studies, we assess the performance of the proposed methods under various data-generating scenarios. We apply the methods to the motivating application for predicting user susceptibility to automated ("bot") interactions at increasing future time horizons based on social media behavioral trajectories observed over fixed time windows.

</details>


### [188] [Longitudinal Adaptive Experimental Design for Learning Multiple Target Estimands with Semiparametric Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29421)
*Wenxin Zhang,Mark van der Laan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种纵向自适应实验的半参数效率框架，通过后向递归策略推导oracle设计，并开发ADL-LTMLE估计器，实现从依赖数据中进行半参数有效推断，模拟显示相比非自适应设计方差大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应设计研究主要集中在单阶段治疗场景，而纵向研究中涉及多阶段、时变治疗的自适应实验设计仍缺乏系统探索。针对特定阶段效应优化的设计可能严重损害其他阶段效应的估计效率，存在设计权衡问题。

Method: 构建一般半参数效率框架：(1)提出效率导向的设计准则，可处理单一或联合多目标估计；(2)证明前期最优随机化依赖于后期分配，采用后向递归推导oracle设计；(3)提出纵向自适应设计，利用累积数据序贯学习和逼近oracle设计；(4)开发ADL-LTMLE（基于自适应设计似然的纵向目标最大似然估计器），实现非参数假设下的渐近正态与半参数有效估计。

Result: 应用于治疗起始时间效应（比较某阶段开始治疗与延迟到后续阶段开始治疗），发现针对特定阶段优化会损害其他阶段估计效率；模拟研究表明，所提设计和估计方法相对于非自适应设计实现了显著的方差降低，且性能接近oracle设计。

Conclusion: 该框架为纵向自适应实验提供了统一的设计与估计方法，能有效解决多阶段治疗场景下的效率优化与设计权衡问题，ADL-LTMLE在依赖数据中实现了稳健的半参数有效推断。

Abstract: Adaptive designs are increasingly used in clinical trials and digital experiments to improve estimation efficiency by updating treatment randomization probabilities as data accumulate. While most existing work focuses on settings with a single-stage treatment, adaptive designs for longitudinal studies with multi-stage, time-varying treatments remain relatively underexplored. In this work, we develop a general semiparametric efficiency framework for designing longitudinal adaptive experiments to optimize the estimation efficiency of a broad class of target estimands of interest. An efficiency-oriented design criterion is proposed to accommodate both single-estimand targets and joint optimization across multiple estimands. We demonstrate that optimal randomization at earlier stages depends on later-stage allocations, yielding a backward-recursive strategy for deriving the oracle design, and propose a longitudinal adaptive design to sequentially learn and target the oracle design using accumulating data. We further develop an adaptive-design-likelihood-based longitudinal targeted maximum likelihood estimator (ADL-LTMLE) for asymptotically normal and semiparametric efficient estimation of statistical estimands from dependent data collected from adaptive experiments, without relying on parametric model assumptions. Applying the framework to time-to-treatment-initiation effects that compare initiating treatment at a given stage with delaying initiation until a subsequent stage, we show that designs optimized for a particular stage-specific effect can substantially compromise estimation efficiency for effects defined at other stages, highlighting the design trade-offs addressed by our framework. Simulation studies show the proposed design and estimation approaches achieve substantial variance reductions relative to non-adaptive designs, with performance close to that of the oracle design.

</details>


### [189] [Multiple type I error concepts for clinical trials with overlapping populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29528)
*Remi Luschei,Werner Brannath*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对个性化医学临床试验中多重重叠患者群体的多重检验问题，提出两种改进的人群错误率（PWER）变体——PWER-P和PWER-U，分别对每个目标群体或所有群体并集施加个体错误控制，以解决原始PWER中特定群体错误概率受其他群体存在性影响的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化医学中的临床试验常涉及多个重叠的患者亚群。原始PWER虽然比FWER更宽松、能提升统计效能，但其核心缺陷在于：某一特定群体的错误概率高度依赖于分析中是否包含其他群体。这在实际应用中可能导致对某些群体的I类错误控制不可靠，因此需要改进PWER以在保持效能优势的同时实现更稳健的个体群体错误控制。

Method: 作者提出两种PWER修正方案：（1）PWER-P（PWER over the populations），对所有目标群体强制执行个体错误率控制；（2）PWER-U（PWER over population unions），对所有可能的目标群体并集强制执行错误率控制。论文系统研究了这两种新错误率的数学性质，并通过与原始PWER和传统FWER在I类错误控制和统计效能方面的对比分析进行评估。

Result: PWER-P和PWER-U均能在多重重叠群体场景下提供比FWER更优的统计效能，同时克服了原始PWER中群体水平错误控制不稳定的问题。两种方法为不同应用场景提供了灵活的误差控制策略：PWER-P适用于需保护每个个体群体的情形，PWER-U适用于需保护群体并集的情形。

Conclusion: PWER-P和PWER-U是PWER框架的有效改进，成功在多重比较的严格性（FWER）与宽松性（PWER）之间取得平衡，在保障群体层面个体化错误控制的同时保持较高的统计效能，对个性化医学临床试验设计具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: The population-wise error rate (PWER) was introduced as a more liberal alternative to the family-wise error rate (FWER) for clinical trials with multiple, overlapping patient populations. These trials are particularly relevant in personalized medicine, which aims to find therapies tailored to specific patient subgroups. By controlling an average multiple type I error probability over all population strata, the PWER can substantially improve statistical power. However, one disadvantage of this concept is that the error probability for a given population can strongly depend on the presence or absence of other populations included in the analysis. To address this issue, we propose two modifications of the PWER that enforce individual error control either for all target populations, or for all possible unions of target populations. We call these approaches the PWER over the populations (PWER-P) and the PWER over population unions (PWER-U). We investigate the properties of these new error rates and compare them with the PWER and FWER in terms of type I error control and power.

</details>


### [190] [Marginal generalized raking with parametric working models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29629)
*Brian D Williamson,Runjia Zou,Thomas Lumley,Bryan E Shepherd,Pamela A Shaw*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出"边际广义 raking"方法，将基于影响函数的广义 raking 理论从回归系数估计推广至边际估计量，并通过模拟和HIV队列数据验证其优于简单的条件估计边际化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义 raking 最初用于调查统计中整合辅助信息，近期被用于缺失数据（包括两阶段研究设计）下的参数模型回归系数估计。然而，现有理论主要针对条件回归参数，缺乏对边际估计量的系统处理。简单的做法是将条件GR估计量直接边际化，但这种"朴素"方法可能效率低下。因此需要将GR理论直接推广至边际估计量。

Method: 作者基于影响函数理论，将广义 raking 框架推广至边际估计量（marginal estimands），提出"边际广义 raking"方法。该方法直接针对感兴趣的边际参数构造GR估计量，而非先估计条件回归参数再进行边际化。通过完全合成模拟和HIV感染者的观察性队列数据应用，将所提方法与朴素边际化方法进行对比。

Result: 在合成模拟和HIV队列数据的实证应用中，边际广义 raking 方法均优于先将条件GR回归参数估计量边际化的朴素方法，表现出更好的统计性能。

Conclusion: 边际广义 raking 成功将GR的影响函数理论扩展至边际估计量，为缺失数据场景下的边际参数估计提供了更优的统计方法，在两阶段研究等设计中有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Generalized raking (GR) was originally developed in the survey statistics literature to incorporate auxiliary information in estimation. Recently, it has been used in the biostatistical and epidemiological literature to estimate regression coefficients in parametric models in cases with missing data, including missing data by design (e.g., two-phase studies). In the regression parameter context, the optimal GR estimator has been shown to be equivalent to the optimal augmented inverse probability weighted estimator. In this paper, we generalize the influence function-based theory for GR to marginal estimands; we call our approach \textit{marginal generalized raking}. We compare our approach to a naive procedure that marginalizes a conditional GR estimator of regression parameters in both fully-synthetic simulations and in an application using data from an observational cohort of persons living with HIV.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Accelerated Random-Sweep Gibbs Sampling for Gaussian Graphical Models via Dual Normal Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28706)
*Borna Khodabandeh,Mehdi Molkaraie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了薄薄膜先验下高斯图模型中随机扫描Gibbs采样的收敛性质，证明通过对偶变换（傅里叶变换）可显著加速收敛。对于齐次k-正则图，给出了原始域和对偶域的精确收敛率，并证明对偶域的收敛率具有普适性且与图拓扑无关，实际收敛速度由图的代数连通度决定。此外，建立了原始模型与对偶模型协方差结构之间的显式代数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯图模型中的Gibbs采样在含有环的图上收敛缓慢，尤其是在薄薄膜先验等强相关先验下。作者旨在探索通过对偶变换加速MCMC收敛的理论基础，揭示收敛率与图结构之间的内在联系。

Method: 将原始正态因子图模型通过傅里叶变换作用于局部因子，得到对偶模型；在齐次k-正则图上解析推导原始域和对偶域的精确收敛率；利用代数图论（特别是代数连通度）分析对偶域中的有效收敛加速机制；建立原始与对偶模型协方差矩阵之间的代数关系以实现边际统计量的直接转换。

Result: 对于所有包含环的齐次模型，对偶域的收敛率具有普适性，与底层图拓扑无关；对偶域的有效收敛率由图的代数连通度支配，且不增加每次扫描的计算复杂度；原始与对偶模型的协方差结构存在显式代数关系，允许从对偶模型直接恢复原始模型的边际统计量；数值实验在多个图族上验证了理论结果，展示了收敛率的显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过对偶变换（傅里叶变换）可以将高斯图模型的Gibbs采样映射到加速收敛的对偶域，该加速效应对含环的齐次图模型具有普适性，且代数连通度是关键控制参数。这一框架在不增加计算代价的前提下，为提升概率图模型推断效率提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study the convergence properties of the random-sweep Gibbs sampler for Gaussian graphical models with a thin-membrane prior. We demonstrate that the convergence rate of the Gibbs sampler is significantly accelerated in the dual model, which is obtained by applying the Fourier transform to the local factors of the normal factor graph representing the original model. In both domains, we derive the exact convergence rates for homogeneous $k$-regular graphs. We prove that, for all homogeneous models whose graphical representations contain cycles, the convergence rate in the dual domain is universal and independent of the underlying graph topology. Moreover, we show that the effective convergence rate in the dual domain is governed by the algebraic connectivity of the graph, providing an additional acceleration without increasing the computational complexity per sweep. We further establish an explicit algebraic relation between the covariance structures of the primal and dual models, enabling marginal statistics of the primal model to be recovered directly from those of the dual model. Finally, numerical experiments on several graph families confirm our theoretical results and demonstrate substantial improvements in the convergence rates in various settings.

</details>


### [192] [Conditioning Tree-Based Diffusions and Flows for Probabilistic Tabular Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28864)
*Silas Koemen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: DiffGBM 提出两个改进方向：一是基于高斯路径流匹配的 $p(y|x)$ 训练器，直接学习速度场并进行确定性 ODE 采样；二是将评分侧的配方（残差化、EDM 式预处理、log-sigma 时间采样等）暴露为可联合调优的轴，在 LightGBM 上搜索。在 11 个表格基准上，优化后的配置全面超越已发布的基线，流匹配方案采样速度提升 5.2 倍且校准最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于树的扩散模型直接继承了神经扩散模型的设计默认值（加噪路径、参数化方式、训练分布、特征和采样器），但这些默认值实际决定了梯度提升集成所解决的监督回归问题的条件设定。作者认为这些默认值是性能的约束瓶颈，需要将其显式化并加以优化。

Method: 方法分两个轴：(1) 高斯路径流匹配训练器——直接学习速度场 $v_\theta$，通过代数方式恢复评分函数，支持少步确定性 ODE 采样。(2) 评分弹性（score-flex）空间——将残差化、EDM 式预处理、log-sigma 时间采样、噪声水平特征、损失加权和直方图分辨率等组件在共享的 LightGBM 表面上作为可联合调优的轴暴露出来，而非固定捆绑。在 11 个表格基准上，采用 fold-0 调优、folds-1–5 评估，匹配 40 次试验预算和采样器进行实验。

Result: 在全部 11 个数据集上，选出的配置均击败已发布基线（配对 Wilcoxon 检验 11/0，$p < 10^{-3}$），最佳聚合 CRPS 技能为 0.725（基线 0.699）。流匹配是最便宜的采样器（比已发布基线快 5.2 倍），且是校准最优的 DiffGBM 行。评分弹性行通过随机采样器获得更高精度但速度最慢。随机（$\varepsilon > 0$）流采样器并未帕累托支配确定性边界。非扩散基线在个别数据集上仍能胜出。

Conclusion: DiffGBM 通过显式解耦并优化树基扩散模型的设计轴，表明评分弹性空间以更慢的随机采样换取精度提升，而流匹配方案以更快的确定性采样和更好的校准性能形成互补。两者的结合展示了树基扩散模型中设计选择的系统性优化空间。

Abstract: Tree-based diffusion models fit flexible conditional predictive distributions for tabular regression without a neural density estimator, but they inherit their design defaults---noising path, parameterization, training distribution, features, sampler---from the neural setting. We show these defaults are the binding constraint: what a gradient-boosted ensemble actually solves is a supervised regression problem whose conditioning they determine. We present DiffGBM, which makes them explicit along two axes. First, a Gaussian-path flow-matching trainer for $p(y \mid x)$ that learns a velocity field directly and recovers the score algebraically, admitting few-step deterministic ODE sampling. Second, we expose the score-side recipe---residualization, EDM-style preconditioning, log-sigma time sampling, noise-level features, loss weighting, and histogram resolution---as jointly tunable axes over a shared LightGBM surface rather than one frozen bundle. This \emph{score-flex} space represents the published recipe as a special case; across eleven tabular benchmarks under fold-0 tuning, folds-1--5 evaluation, and a matched 40-trial budget and sampler, the selected configurations beat that baseline on \emph{every} dataset (paired Wilcoxon $11/0$, $p<10^{-3}$), with the best aggregate CRPS skill (0.725 vs.\ 0.699) of any row. The two rows are complementary: score-flex buys accuracy with a stochastic sampler and is the slowest row, while flow matching is the cheapest sampler ($5.2\times$ faster than the published baseline) and the best-calibrated DiffGBM row. Tuned non-diffusion baselines still win individual datasets, and stochastic ($\varepsilon>0$) flow samplers do not Pareto-dominate the deterministic corner.

</details>


### [193] [Structured Neural Chaos: An Adaptive Surrogate Modeling Framework for Functional Uncertainty Quantification and Global Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28903)
*Isabel Corona Guevara,Yeping Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化神经混沌展开（sNC），将PCE的正交可解释结构与神经网络表达能力相结合，以可分离低秩近似构建功能性ANOVA分解，用于高维随机输入和函数响应场景下的方差基全局灵敏度分析，且能从展开系数中直接、低成本地提取灵敏度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维随机输入与函数响应场景下，传统PCE面临维度灾难且计算负担大，而现有替代模型难以同时兼顾可扩展性、可解释性，以及跨时空域的灵敏度估计需求。

Method: 提出结构化神经混沌展开（sNC）：模仿截断的 functional ANOVA 分解，每个交互分量采用可分离低秩近似，基函数和系数均由神经网络参数化；采用序贯构造方式，自适应识别各ANOVA子空间内的主导模态并确定表示的有效复杂度。

Result: sNC展开保留了结构化分解的可解释性，同时借助神经网络获得表达力；展开系数可直接提取统计量和灵敏度信息，提取成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: sNC为函数响应下的方差基GSA提供了一种兼具可解释性、可扩展性和计算效率的替代建模框架，有效缓解了PCE的维度灾难问题。

Abstract: Variance-based global sensitivity analysis (GSA) plays a key role in uncertainty quantification by identifying the contributions of uncertain inputs to the variability of the model response. The repeated model evaluations required for these tasks are often prohibitively expensive; surrogate models provide an efficient alternative by constructing inexpensive approximations of the underlying system response. Constructing surrogate models that combine scalability and interpretability for systems with high-dimensional stochastic inputs and functional responses remains challenging, particularly when sensitivity estimates are required across spatial or temporal domains. Polynomial chaos expansion (PCE) provides an effective framework for uncertainty propagation and sensitivity analysis due to its orthogonal structure and direct relationship with variance-based sensitivity measures. However, PCE suffers from the curse of dimensionality, whose computational burden is amplified for problems with functional responses. In this work, we introduce the Structured Neural Chaos (sNC) expansion as a surrogate modeling framework for variance-based GSA, inspired by the interpretability and orthogonal structure of PCE. The proposed framework retains the interpretability of structured decompositions while leveraging the expressive power of neural networks. The sNC expansion mirrors a truncated functional ANOVA decomposition, where each interaction component admits a separable low-rank approximation whose basis functions and coefficients are parameterized by neural networks. The expansion is constructed sequentially, adaptively identifying the dominant modes within each ANOVA subspace and determining the effective complexity of the representation. The resulting structure enables the extraction of statistical and sensitivity quantities directly from the coefficients of the sNC expansion at negligible cost.

</details>


### [194] [Persistent Convolution: A Topological Framework for AI Alignment Testing and Semantic Space Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29008)
*Tyler Ashoff,Jordan Rodu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑学的多模态对齐测试方法，利用人工整理的知识结构来检验不透明模型的嵌入空间与语义结构的一致性，旨在提升模型部署、选择与比较的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代不透明AI模型以牺牲可解释性为代价追求性能，使得测试变得困难。模型开发者需要一种利用人工策划的知识结构来检验对齐性的模型比较技术。此外，即使相对简单任务的输入空间规模也很大，这要求在标准结果推理之外增加对齐性检查。

Method: 开发并演示了一种基于拓扑学的多模态对齐测试方法。该方法对模型的嵌入空间进行形式统计检验，以表征语义结构、概念分离和知识图谱对齐，并将该方法与可能性理论及从数据到部署的统一决策理论框架建立直观联系。

Result: 该方法能够对嵌入空间中的语义结构、概念分离和知识图谱对齐提供稳健的表征，为不透明模型的可解释测试提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: 基于拓扑学的多模态对齐测试使不透明模型的部署、选择和比较更加可解释，为模型对齐检验提供了超越标准结果推理的增强手段，并构建了从数据到部署的统一决策理论框架。

Abstract: Modern opaque AI models prize performance over interpretability, which makes testing difficult. However, formal statistical tests conducted on a model's embedding space can provide robust characterizations of semantic structure, concept separation, and knowledge graph alignment. Model developers would benefit from a model comparison technique that leverages human-curated knowledge structures to test alignment. The scale of the input space for even relatively simple tasks motivates the need for alignment checks that augment standard outcome reasoning. This work develops and demonstrates a topology-based multi-modal alignment test to make deployment, selection, and comparison of opaque models more interpretable. These methods also offer an intuitive connection to possibility theory and a unified decision theoretic framework from data to deployment.

</details>


### [195] [Simple-regret rates and minimax optimality of fixed-prior expected improvement in Matérn and squared-exponential RKHSs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29245)
*Emmanuel Vazquez,Sébastien Petit*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文分析了期望改进（EI）策略在RKHS中确定性函数最小化问题上的表现，通过创新范数和序列分离半径建立了有限预算下的简单遗憾界，证明了EI策略对Matérn核达到极小极大速率最优，对平方指数核达到指数级最优（常数因子内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究确定性目标函数在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）上的全局优化问题，旨在为期望改进（EI）策略提供有限预算下的理论遗憾界，填补该策略在无噪声确定性设置下缺乏严格有限样本理论分析的空白。

Method: 将候选点处的归一化后验标准差与典范特征空间中的创新范数等同起来；定义序列分离半径以界定沿任意查询序列的排序创新范数；利用Gram行列式和Kolmogorov宽度估计不同维度子空间的分离半径；结合一步遗憾不等式导出有限预算下的简单遗憾界。

Result: 经过N次初始后查询，各向同性Matérn核（光滑度ν>0）的简单遗憾为O(N^{-ν/d})；各向同性平方指数核在近似EI最大化下为O(exp[-c₁ min{N, N^{1/d} log(eN)}])，在精确EI最大化下为O(exp[-c₂ N^{1/d} log(eN)])。所有界在RKHS球上一致成立。当𝒳内部非空且B>0时，精确EI策略对Matérn核达到极小极大速率最优，对平方指数核在指数常数因子内达到极小极大速率最优。

Conclusion: EI策略在无噪声确定性RKHS优化中具有坚实的理论基础：对Matérn核达到极小极大最优收敛速率，对平方指数核亦接近最优。这一分析框架将EI的贝叶斯启发式策略与频率主义者的遗憾界联系起来，为高斯过程优化提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study the expected improvement (EI) policy for minimizing a deterministic objective function $f$ on a nonempty compact set $\mathcal X \subset\mathbb R^d$. We assume that $f$ belongs to the RKHS $\mathcal H_k$ of a continuous positive-semidefinite kernel $k$ on $\mathcal X$. Function values are observed exactly, and EI is computed from a fixed zero-mean Gaussian-process model with covariance $σ^2k$. After an initial design, the policy queries a point whose EI is at least a fixed positive fraction of its maximum.
  We identify the normalized posterior standard deviation at a candidate point $x$ with the norm of the corresponding innovation in the canonical feature space, namely the component of $k(x,\cdot)$ orthogonal to the span of the preceding evaluation representers. Sequential separation radii bound the ranked innovation norms along arbitrary query sequences. We estimate these radii using Gram determinants and Kolmogorov widths for subspaces of different dimensions, then combine the estimates with a one-step regret inequality to obtain finite-budget bounds for simple regret.
  After $N$ post-initial queries, simple regret is $O(N^{-ν/d})$ for isotropic Matérn kernels of smoothness $ν>0$. For the isotropic squared-exponential kernel, simple regret is $O(\exp[-c_1\min\{N, N^{1/d}\log(eN)\}])$ for some $c_1>0$. With exact EI maximization, it is $O(\exp[-c_2N^{1/d} \log(eN)])$ for some $c_2>0$. For every fixed $B\geq0$, these bounds are uniform over the RKHS ball of radius $B$.
  If $\mathcal X$ has nonempty interior and $B>0$, then, among deterministic methods whose final recommendation may be any point of $\mathcal X$, the exact EI policy is minimax-rate optimal over the RKHS ball of radius $B$ for Matérn kernels and minimax-rate optimal up to constants in the exponent for squared-exponential kernels.

</details>


### [196] [The Greedy Advantage in Finite-Horizon Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29375)
*Kai Zhou,Michael Lingzhi Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文针对有限时域的多臂伯努利老虎机问题，提出了一类正则化贪心算法，首次推导出有限时域遗憾上界，揭示了遗憾可分解为暂态探索成本与随正则化强度指数衰减的次优收敛项，并据此给出正则化参数的校准规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际应用中的序贯实验是在有限且外部给定的时间范围内运行的，而现有文献中的多臂老虎机算法多聚焦于渐近遗憾保证，缺乏针对有限时域的精确性能刻画。这一鸿沟促使作者针对有限时域设置开发更具实用性的算法。

Method: 作者针对多臂伯努利老虎机提出一类正则化贪心算法，首次推导出有限时域遗憾包络（regret envelope），将遗憾分解为暂态探索成本与随正则化强度指数衰减的次优收敛项；基于该理论表征，设计了正则化参数的原则性校准规则，并将经典贪心策略作为极限情形纳入分析框架，获得更紧的遗憾保证。

Result: 在大规模数值实验中，经过校准的正则化贪心策略一致地匹敌或超越当前最先进算法，验证了其在有限时域场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 正则化贪心策略为有限时域老虎机问题提供了一种高效且具有理论保障的解决方案，其参数可通过原则性校准规则确定，具有很强的实用价值。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly rely on sequential experimentation to improve decision-making. While the multi-armed bandit literature has developed algorithms with strong asymptotic regret guarantees, many practical applications operate over finite and externally imposed horizons. Motivated by the finite-horizon setting, we develop a class of regularized greedy algorithms for multi-armed Bernoulli bandits. We derive the first finite-horizon regret envelopes for regularized greedy bandits, showing that finite-horizon regret decomposes into transient exploration costs and a suboptimal convergence term that decays exponentially with the regularization strength. This characterization yields principled calibration rules for the regularization parameters and, as a limiting case, sharper regret guarantees for the classical greedy policy. Across extensive numerical experiments, calibrated regularized greedy policies consistently match or outperform state-of-the-art algorithms. These results suggest that regularized greedy policies can provide an effective approach for finite-horizon bandit problems.

</details>


### [197] [Analytical and Bootstrap Confidence Intervals of Double Machine Learning: Simulation studies and an application to rural-urban difference in obesity prevalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29456)
*Haozheng Xu,Siyuan Ma,Qingyan Xiang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过全面的模拟研究，系统评估了双机器学习（DML）中不同机器学习算法选择对置信区间覆盖概率的影响，发现分析置信区间和Bootstrap置信区间的覆盖性能存在显著差异，且令人意外的是，随着样本量增大，两者的覆盖概率反而下降。真实数据分析进一步印证了学习者选择对模型性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DML方法在实践中要求研究者从众多机器学习算法中选择扰动参数模型，但这一选择对DML方差估计和推断可靠性的影响尚未得到充分刻画。研究者缺乏系统性的指导来理解不同学习器选择如何影响DML置信区间的覆盖性能。

Method: 作者设计了全面的蒙特卡洛模拟研究：设置多种数据生成机制（不同非线性程度、混杂结构等），比较(1)DML理论分析置信区间与(2)Bootstrap置信区间；纳入五类学习器（OLS、LASSO、随机森林、LightGBM、神经网络）；以偏差、置信区间宽度和覆盖概率作为评估指标。此外，利用美国县级城乡差异与肥胖率数据进行了真实数据验证分析。

Result: 模拟结果表明：分析置信区间与Bootstrap置信区间在不同学习器和数据场景下覆盖性能差异显著，学习器选择对DML推断可靠性至关重要；反直觉地发现，在许多场景下，样本量增大反而导致两种置信区间的覆盖概率下降。真实数据分析发现模型性能仍因学习器选择而异，且更强的乡村性对县级肥胖率有统计显著的正向效应。

Conclusion: DML推断的可靠性高度依赖于扰动参数学习器的选择，研究者不能简单依赖默认算法；大样本下覆盖概率下降的现象提示DML渐近理论在有限样本中的局限性；建议实际应用中应谨慎选择学习器并进行充分的敏感性分析。

Abstract: Double Machine Learning (DML) is a popular approach for treatment effect estimation in various settings, which allows a wide range of flexible machine learning methods to be used for nuisance parameter estimation while preserving valid inference. In practice, however, applied researchers must choose among many machine learning algorithms for nuisance models, and the impact of this choice on the variance estimation of DML is not well characterized. We conduct a comprehensive simulation study to compare the coverage probability of DML confidence intervals across different machine learning algorithms. In this study, we compare (1) analytical confidence intervals derived by DML theory versus (2) bootstrap confidence interval. We use a set of learners including ordinary least squares, LASSO, Random Forest, LightGBM, and Neural Networks under different data generation settings. We evaluate the performance across difference settings by bias, confidence interval width, and most importantly, coverage probability. Our results show substantial variability in coverage performance across analytical and bootstrap confidence intervals, highlighting that learner choice plays a critical role in reliable DML inference. Surprisingly, we find that in many settings, when sample size increases, the coverage probability of both DML analytical and bootstrap confidence interval decreases. We further investigate coverage probabilities using a real dataset on rural urban differences among U.S. counties. The real data analysis discovers that (1) the model performance still varies by the learner choices and (2) greater rurality has a statistically significant increasing effect on county level obesity prevalence.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Topology-Aware Data Movement for Disaggregated GPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28633)
*Sanjeev Rao Ganjihal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分离式LLM推理中KV缓存跨池传输问题，揭示了统一RDMA方案的局限性——GPU间带宽因物理拓扑差异可达72倍。提出拓扑感知传输编排器，融合流水线逐层传输、NVLink域感知的MoE放置优化，以及CXL 3.0共享溢出层三项机制，预计可将传输延迟降低3-18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分离式LLM推理系统（DistServe、Splitwise、Mooncake）在prefill和decode分池部署时，均采用统一RDMA传输KV缓存，完全忽略了GPU间互联拓扑的异构性：NVLink域内带宽900 GB/s，跨节点InfiniBand仅50 GB/s，跨数据中心TCP仅12.5 GB/s，差距高达72倍。70B模型的单请求KV缓存达2.6 GB，生产规模下聚合带宽超100 GB/s，统一RDMA方案造成严重的带宽浪费和延迟瓶颈。

Method: 提出拓扑感知传输编排器，包含三项协同机制：(1) 流水线逐层传输——将KV缓存按层分片，在prefill计算尚未完成时即开始传输已生成层，将60-85%的传输延迟隐藏在计算之后；(2) NVLink域感知的MoE放置——针对混合专家模型，联合优化专家路由调度与KV缓存局部性，减少跨域传输；(3) CXL 3.0内存扩展器作为共享溢出层——提供6倍于本地内存的容量，延迟比NVMe低86倍。系统启动时自动发现互联拓扑层级，为每次传输选择最优传输协议。

Result: 由于完整评估需要含异构互联的多节点集群和尚未在GPU云中可用的CXL 3.0硬件，超出学术资源范围，论文给出了分析性带宽模型、组件实现，以及三种架构下的投影分析。结果显示，相比统一RDMA方案，传输延迟降低3至18倍。

Conclusion: 分离式LLM推理的网络传输必须考虑物理拓扑异构性。拓扑感知的KV缓存传输编排，结合流水线隐藏延迟、拓扑感知放置和CXL内存扩展，可显著突破现有统一RDMA方案的性能瓶颈。该方法为未来大规模分离式推理系统的网络层设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM inference creates a datacenter networking problem that no existing system solves correctly. When prefill and decode run on separate GPU pools, the KV cache must be transferred between them. For a 70B model this is 2.6 GB per request, exceeding 100 GB/s aggregate at production scale. Yet DistServe, Splitwise, and Mooncake all use uniform RDMA, ignoring that bandwidth between two GPUs varies by 72x depending on their physical relationship: 900 GB/s via NVLink within a domain, 50 GB/s via InfiniBand across nodes, 12.5 GB/s via TCP across data centers. We design a topology-aware transfer orchestrator that discovers interconnect hierarchy at startup and selects optimal transport per transfer. Three mechanisms work together: (1) pipelined layer-by-layer transfer that overlaps transmission with ongoing prefill, hiding 60 to 85 percent of latency behind computation; (2) NVLink domain-aware placement for Mixture-of-Experts models that co-optimizes expert dispatch with KV cache locality; and (3) CXL 3.0 memory expanders as a shared overflow tier providing 6x capacity at 86x lower latency than NVMe. Full evaluation requires multi-node clusters with heterogeneous interconnects and CXL 3.0 hardware that is beyond academic resources and not yet available in GPU clouds. We present analytical bandwidth models, component implementations, and projected analysis across three architectures showing 3 to 18x transfer latency reduction over uniform RDMA.

</details>


### [199] [Sensitivity Analysis of GRU, LSTM and Transformer Encoder in Classification of Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28665)
*Bidhya Shrestha,Christos Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种序列分类模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer）基于车辆遥测数据识别L2级自动驾驶系统（Openpilot、Autopilot、Super Cruise及人工驾驶）的性能，并提出了一种模块化鲁棒性评估框架。三种模型在干净数据上均表现优异（宏F1 0.90-0.93），但鲁棒性测试揭示了关键失效模式：事件级损坏影响较小，而时间抖动导致宏F1急剧下降至0.44-0.50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来软件定义车辆可能同时运行多种自动驾驶系统（原生或后装），因此需要独立监控系统来验证当前激活的ADS，以服务于安全监控、法规合规、保险评估和异常检测。然而，遥测数据在真实环境中面临多种退化（噪声、抖动、丢包等），现有分类方法在退化数据上的鲁棒性尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 首先，使用车辆遥测数据训练三种序列分类模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer编码器）识别四种驾驶模式。其次，提出模块化鲁棒性评估框架，定义五类损坏（加性高斯白噪声带累积漂移、跨通道相关噪声、时间抖动、突发丢失/延迟转换/虚假翻转等事件级损坏），每类五个严重等级（L1-L5），以宏F1作为评估指标。

Result: 干净数据上，Transformer编码器宏F1达0.93，GRU为0.92，LSTM为0.90；威胁匹配训练后宏F1为0.904-0.916（干净数据性能损失有限）。鲁棒性测试：事件级损坏在L5级别宏F1仍≥0.87，但时间抖动使所有模型宏F1崩溃至0.44-0.50。

Conclusion: 序列分类模型可有效基于遥测数据识别ADS，但时间抖动是当前模型的首要鲁棒性瓶颈，需针对性改进。所提出的模块化鲁棒性评估框架可为ADS识别系统的可靠性验证提供标准化工具。

Abstract: Automated driving systems (ADSs) are becoming ubiquitous. Future Software Defined Vehicles (SDVs) may be able to run multiple ADSs, both native and aftermarket such as Comma.ai's Openpilot. Monitoring systems to independently verify which automated driving system is active are important for safety monitoring, regulatory compliance, insurance assessment, and anomaly detection. In this paper, we first evaluate the effectiveness of three sequence-based classification models: Gated Recurrent Units (GRU), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and a Transformer encoder model for identifying Level 2 automated driving systems using vehicle telematics data alone: Comma Openpilot, Tesla Autopilot, and Cadillac Super Cruise, along with manual driving. All three models achieve strong clean-data performance with macro F1-scores of 0.92 (GRU), 0.90 (LSTM), and 0.93 (Transformer encoder model) when trained on clean data; threat-matched training yields 0.904-0.916 macro F1 with only a modest clean-data penalty. Second, we introduce a modular robustness evaluation framework that simulates realistic telematics degradation through five corruption families at five severity levels (L1-L5). Continuous channels are perturbed using additive white Gaussian noise with cumulative drift, correlated cross-channel noise, and temporal jitter. Binary event signals are subjected to burst loss, delayed transitions, spurious toggles and cross-feature inconsistencies inspired by communication errors. Robustness is measured using macro-F1, which gives equal weight to each class and is suitable for imbalanced multiclass evaluation. Our evaluation reveals a sharp failure-mode split: event-level corruptions reduce macro-F1 only slightly (greater than equal to 0.87 at L5), while temporal jitter collapses macro-F1 to 0.44-0.50 across GRU, LSTM, and Transformer encoder model.

</details>


### [200] [Guarantees on Dynamical System Distinguishability for LLM Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28667)
*Mohamed Akrout,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为基于动力学系统（DS）的LLM响应分类方法提供了严格的理论基础，将其形式化为两个随机线性DS之间的二元假设检验，揭示了分类精度随序列长度指数衰减的规律，并刻画了跨嵌入模型的迁移条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于动力学系统的LLM分类方法虽在实证上取得成功，但缺乏理论理解：为什么该方法有效、分类性能如何随token序列长度变化、以及何时能跨嵌入模型迁移。本文旨在填补这些理论空白。

Method: 将分类任务形式化为两个随机线性动力学系统之间的二元假设检验。通过分析两个DS稳态边缘分布间的全变差距离，建立分类精度下限；推导误分类概率随序列长度L指数衰减的界，衰减速率由动力学可区分量δ²（刻画两DS间的谱距离）决定；引入嵌入模型间的近似交织条件，并基于交织映射的最小奇异值建立可迁移可区分性的下界。

Result: （1）即使两个DS的动力学差异显著，其稳态边缘分布的全变差距离可任意小，为忽略token动力学的分类器设定了根本性的精度下限；（2）基于DS的分类误分类概率随序列长度L指数衰减，衰减率由δ²控制；（3）跨嵌入泛化能力可通过近似交织条件刻画，迁移可区分性下界由交织映射的最小奇异值决定。

Conclusion: 理论结果解释了基于DS的分类方法的实证表现，并倡导进一步利用DS理论分析AI系统——这与常见的“用AI建模动力学系统”的研究范式形成互补与对照。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that classifying large language models (LLMs)' responses can be distinguished by modeling token embeddings as trajectories of a black-box dynamical system (DS) and comparing prediction residuals of two DSs. Despite the empirical success of this dynamical approach, a theoretical understanding of why it works, how well it scales as a function of the token sequence, and when it transfers across embedding models remains lacking. We address these questions by formalizing the classification task as a binary hypothesis test between two stochastic linear DSs. We show that the total variation distance between the stationary marginal distributions of the two DSs can be arbitrarily small even when the dynamics differ substantially, which provides a fundamental accuracy floor for any classifier that ignores token dynamics. We then show that the misclassification probability of DS-based classification decays exponentially in the sequence length $L$, with the decay governed by a dynamical discriminability quantity $δ^2$ that captures the spectral distance between the two DSs. We also characterize cross-embedding generalization by introducing an approximate intertwining condition between embedding models and establishing a lower bound on the transferable discriminability in terms of the intertwining map's smallest singular value. Together, these results explain the empirical performance of DS-based classification and motivate further investigation into using DS theory to analyze AI systems, in contrast to the more common approach of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [201] [LARA: Lightweight Adapters in the Residual Stream for Composable Adaptation and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28669)
*Pascal Ekin,Hyosun Choi,Wei Jie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LARA 是一种轻量级适应方法，在冻结模型的残差流中施加低秩修正，而非修改权重。它在代码微调和偏好优化（DPO）上以同等参数量匹配 LoRA 的性能，并原生支持通过缩放因子 γ 在基座模型与适应行为之间平滑插值，同时允许在单一冻结模型上同时驻留多个独立训练的行为模块，按 token 自动路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调方法（如 LoRA）通过修改权重来实现适应，存在两个局限：一是无法在基座模型与适应行为之间进行平滑的 graded control；二是多条行为需要分别加载不同模型，难以在同一设备上高效托管多个行为。作者希望设计一种在残差流层面操作的方法，既保持参数效率，又天然支持行为插值和多行为共驻。

Method: LARA 在冻结基座模型的若干层的残差流上操作：读取隐藏状态，通过低秩分解生成一个修正向量，再将其加回残差流，所有权重矩阵保持不变。适应后的模型等价于「冻结基座 + 低秩残差模块」。推理时引入缩放因子 γ，控制残差修正的强度，实现基座行为到适应行为的平滑过渡。多个独立训练的 LARA 模块可同时驻留内存，通过 per-token 路由机制自动选择适用的行为。

Result: 在代码微调和 DPO 偏好优化任务上，LARA 在同等参数预算下匹配 LoRA 的性能。通过 γ 缩放，LARA 能平滑插值基座与适应行为。在 1.5B 冻结模型上同时加载 7 种行为（6 个微调 + 1 个偏好优化），总开销仅约 33 MB，相比为每种行为维护一个完整模型大幅节省存储。

Conclusion: LARA 证明在残差流而非权重空间中进行低秩适应是可行且高效的，既保持了参数效率，又额外获得了行为平滑插值、多行为共驻与 per-token 路由等能力，特别适合在单一设备上托管大量定制化模型行为的场景。

Abstract: We present LARA (Lightweight Additive Residual Adaptation), a method for efficient adaptation that operates in the residual stream of a frozen model rather than in its weights. Where LoRA adds an update of low rank to weight matrices, LARA reads the hidden state at a small set of layers and adds a correction of low rank back to the residual stream, leaving all base weights untouched. On a code fine-tuning task and on preference optimization (DPO), LARA matches LoRA at equal parameter counts. Because adaptation is a frozen base plus a residual, LARA exposes a scale γ, applied at inference, that interpolates smoothly between base and adapted behavior, a form of graded control that adaptation in weight space does not offer. Finally, because each behavior is a small residual module over a shared frozen base, many behaviors can be held resident at once and routed automatically per token. We place seven behaviors, six fine-tuned and one optimized for preference, on one frozen 1.5B model for roughly 33 MB of overhead, against one full model for each behavior. Because the base is untouched, behaviors are trained separately and selected per token rather than loaded on demand, which suits hosting many behaviors, and adding new ones, on a single model on a device.

</details>


### [202] [Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-\texorpdfstring{$K$}{K} Routing: Two-Sided Dependence Control for Frozen Mixture-of-Experts at Fixed Per-Token Routing Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28670)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了分层Copula-Gumbel-Top-K（CGA）方法，通过在MoE模型中引入可控的token间路由依赖关系，在保持每个token的边际路由分布不变的约束下，利用正相关（组内）和负相关（组间）两个"旋钮"来调节专家负载的方差与一致性，并通过一个轻量控制器进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MoE模型中，标准的路由机制假设各token独立，但这忽略了token间可能存在的关联结构。作者试图在严格保持每个token的边际路由分布（即路由律）不变的前提下，探索并控制token间路由选择的联合分布，以在不修改基础模型的情况下调节专家集一致性和负载分散度。

Method: 提出分层Copula-Gumbel-Top-K（CGA）方法：在相关token组内，利用可交换的高斯Copula对每个专家坐标的Gumbel扰动进行正相关，增强组内专家集一致性；在不相交的组对之间，通过可调的对立构造引入可控的负相关。证明这些操作在条件于预路由logits时，保持每个token的有序Top-K采样、混合权重和包含概率的分布与独立路由相同，从而保护了条件期望专家流量。通过一个基于得分函数估计器的轻量控制器在冻结特征上驱动这两个依赖"旋钮"。

Result: 理论证明了两类操作均保持边际路由分布不变。刻画了权衡关系：正相关只能放大专家负载的方差（相对于独立路由），而非负的组间对立只能减小方差（相对于同等组内耦合强度的平坦耦合）。初步小规模实验验证了机制与训练路径的可行性，但尚未在任务级微调上证明收益。

Conclusion: CGA方法在不修改基础MoE模型的前提下，通过在不变性约束面上引入两个互补的依赖"旋钮"，实现了对专家集一致性和负载分散度的解耦控制。该框架为MoE路由的依赖结构建模提供了新视角，但其实际任务级收益仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: A stochastic Gumbel-Top-$K$ router defines, for every token of a mixture-of-experts (MoE) model, a \emph{routing law}: a distribution over ordered expert lists and mixture weights. We ask which \emph{joint} distributions over the routing choices of different tokens are reachable while every individual token's complete routing law is held exactly fixed. We give a two-sided construction, \emph{Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-$K$} (\CGA{}). Within a group of related tokens, an exchangeable Gaussian copula positively correlates the Gumbel perturbations at each expert coordinate, which can increase within-group expert-set coherence. Across disjoint pairs of groups, a tunable antithetic construction introduces a selectable amount of negative dependence. We prove that both operations leave each token's ordered Top-$K$ sample, mixture weights, and inclusion probabilities identical in distribution to independent routing \emph{at a routing layer conditioned on its pre-routing logits}; conditional expected expert traffic is preserved as a consequence. We characterize the resulting trade-off: positive within-group coupling can only inflate the variance of realized expert loads relative to independent routing, while nonnegative cross-group opposition can only reduce it relative to flat coupling at the same within-group strength. Coherence and load dispersion are thus controlled by two complementary dependence dials on the invariance constraint surface. Because the base model is untouched, the dials can be driven by a small controller over frozen features, trainable with a score-function estimator: the frozen network is evaluated only in the forward direction, and gradients are confined to the controller. An initial small-scale pilot validates the mechanism and the training route, but does not establish task-level fine-tuning gains.

</details>


### [203] [LAWFUL: Law-Aligned Witness for Faithful Use of Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28672)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAWFUL框架，用于评估神经网络是否真正学习并内部使用了物理定律（如多普勒频率定律），识别并弥合了可解释性研究中的四个关键缺口，重点关注连续变量上的因果一致性与领域有效性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释性研究无法充分回答：当神经网络准确预测物理系统时，是否真正学习了结构化的物理定律知识，以及其内部计算是否在定律的有效域内始终使用该表征。作者识别出四个关键缺口：缺乏对连续反事实的覆盖感知因果一致性度量、缺乏对所识别电路的领域有效性检验、缺乏对定律不变量与禁止行为的验证、以及缺乏对导出物理量在电路中流动的量化分析。

Method: 提出LAWFUL（LAW Faithfulness from Uncontaminated Learning）基础框架。该框架：1）引入覆盖感知的因果一致性度量，在连续反事实空间上评估电路是否忠实于物理定律；2）设计领域有效性测试，验证所识别电路在定律适用范围内的一致性；3）为不变量验证和物理量流动量化奠定基础。在Mocap2Radar Transformer上进行实证验证。

Result: 在Mocap2Radar Transformer上成功验证：尽管训练数据中不显式包含频率f(t)或速度v(t)，网络确实学习并在内部使用了多普勒频率定律 f(t)=2v(t)/λ。LAWFUL框架有效闭合了前两个可解释性缺口，并为后两个缺口奠定了基础。

Conclusion: LAWFUL框架为评估神经网络是否真正习得并忠实使用物理定律提供了系统性方法论，填补了连续变量物理定律可解释性研究的关键空白。该工作不仅验证了Transformer可隐式学习物理定律，也为构建更可信的物理感知AI系统开辟了道路。

Abstract: When a neural network predicts a physical system accurately, has it learned the governing law as formal, structured knowledge, and if so, does the network's internal computation actually use that representation throughout the law's domain of validity? We identify four interpretability gaps that limit answering these questions for {\em physics laws over continuous variables}: the absence of a coverage-aware causal-consistency measure over continuous counterfactuals; of a domain-of-validity test for the identified circuit; of a verification of the law's invariants and forbidden behaviors; and of a quantification of how a derived physical quantity flows through the circuit. We develop a foundational framework, LAWFUL, that closes the first two and lays groundwork for the remaining two, and illustrate it on the Mocap2Radar transformer, validating whether it learns and internally uses the Doppler frequency law $f(t) = \frac{2 v(t)}λ$ from motion-capture and radar data in which neither $f(t)$ nor $v(t)$ appears.

</details>


### [204] [MPP-GNN: Subject-Adaptive Community Detection for fMRI-Based Alzheimer's Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28681)
*Yang Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Jonathan Warrell,Avram Holmes,Xuan Zhang,Mark Gerstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种元概率池化图神经网络（MPP-GNN），通过双层优化实现自适应的大脑功能模块发现，并将其作为先验引导边细化和表征学习，在阿尔茨海默病分类任务上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的脑功能连接分析方法通常预设所有受试者具有相同数量的功能模块，忽略了个体间差异；且发现的模块很少被用于直接指导连接模式的学习。

Method: 提出MPP-GNN，将任务建模为耦合的双层优化问题：层次化地进行自适应图划分以发现个体特异性模块，然后将发现的模块作为显式先验来指导边细化和表征学习。

Result: 在两个公开AD分类数据集上验证，AUC均超越现有基线方法；此外，发现的模块与Yeo脑图谱定义的标准功能网络组织高度一致，并揭示了AD的网络级去分化模式。

Conclusion: MPP-GNN通过自适应模块发现和模块引导的图学习，有效捕捉了个体间功能连接差异，在AD分类和脑网络机制解释方面均表现优异。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a widely used technique for studying the brain. Recent methods that utilize graph neural networks (GNNs) for analysis of brain functional connectivity have shown great potential for the classification of brain disorders, such as Alzheimer's disease (AD). However, these methods often assume a preset number of functional modules across all subjects, which overlooks inter-subject variability. In addition, the discovered modules are rarely used to directly guide the learned connectivity patterns. Here, to address these issues, we propose a Meta Probabilistic Pooling GNN (MPP-GNN). We frame the model's task as a coupled, bilevel optimization that performs adaptive graph partitioning hierarchically to discover subject-specific modules and then uses the discovered brain modules as an explicit prior to guide edge refinement and representation learning. We validate MPP-GNN on two public datasets for AD classification, achieving the highest AUC in comparison to established baselines for both datasets. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that MPP-GNN shows significant alignment with the canonical functional-network organization defined by the Yeo brain atlas and reveals a network-level dedifferentiation pattern for AD.

</details>


### [205] [Technological Advances in Detecting and Managing Cognitive Impairment in Older Adults: Trends, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28687)
*Mohammad Asif,Azizuddin Khan,Mohd Azam,Anurag Rajkumar Bombarde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了利用神经生理信号（EEG）、神经影像、血液生物标志物和数字标记物，结合AI/ML/DL技术进行老年人认知障碍早期检测与管理的最新进展，提出了跨学科分类法、整合性早期检测框架，并指出该领域前景在于可信、多模态、经纵向验证的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口老龄化使从轻度认知障碍（MCI）到痴呆的认知衰退成为重大健康挑战，而常规评估往往遗漏其最早迹象，亟需系统梳理技术进展以推动早期检测与干预。

Method: 本文采用批判性综述方法，综合了神经生理信号（EEG的alpha/theta变化、P300潜伏期）、结构与分子神经影像（MRI、淀粉样蛋白/tau PET）、血液生物标志物（如p-tau217）、数字标记物（可穿戴、远程、语音、VR）等多模态数据，以及CNN、LSTM/BiLSTM、Transformer、自监督EEG基础模型等AI/ML/DL方法，并提出了跨学科分类法、方法学严谨性视角（强调受试者及站点独立验证）和分层筛查-干预的整合性早期检测框架。

Result: EEG标记物与深度学习模型报告了高准确率，但多基于小规模、单站点数据集，难以通过严格外部验证；血浆p-tau217已具备临床实用性，首个血液检测于2025年获准用于辅助阿尔茨海默病诊断；抗淀粉样蛋白疗法（lecanemab、donanemab）虽获批准但获益有限且存争议；多领域生活方式预防已趋成熟；可穿戴、远程、语音及VR工具实现了连续生态化监测；多模态融合提升了敏感性和特异性。

Conclusion: 该领域仍面临标准化、可解释性、数据隐私和公平部署等障碍，近期前景在于构建可信、多模态、经纵向验证的系统，将早期检测与可操作、个性化照护相连接。

Abstract: As populations age, cognitive decline from mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to dementia is a defining health challenge of the coming decades, yet routine assessment often misses its earliest signs. This article critically synthesizes recent technological advances for detecting and managing cognitive impairment in older adults, spanning neurophysiological signals (chiefly electroencephalography, EEG), structural and molecular neuroimaging (MRI and amyloid/tau PET), blood-based biomarkers, and digital markers, integrated through artificial intelligence (AI), machine learning (ML), and deep learning (DL). Beyond summarizing, it contributes a cross-disciplinary taxonomy, a methodological-rigor lens foregrounding subject- and site-independent validation, an integrative early-detection framework linking tiered screening to intervention, and comparison tables of detection methods, interventions, and risk and protective factors. EEG markers (alpha/theta changes, P300 latency) and deep models (CNNs, LSTM/BiLSTM, transformers, self-supervised EEG foundation models) report strong accuracy, yet many rest on small, single-site datasets unlikely to survive rigorous external validation. Elsewhere, gains are tangible: plasma p-tau217 has reached clinical utility, with the first blood test cleared to aid Alzheimer's diagnosis in 2025; anti-amyloid therapies (lecanemab, donanemab) are approved despite modest, contested benefits; and multidomain lifestyle prevention has matured. Wearable, remote, speech, and virtual-reality tools enable continuous, ecologically valid monitoring, and multimodal fusion improves sensitivity and specificity. Barriers remain: standardization, explainability, data privacy, and equitable, externally validated deployment. The field's near-term promise lies in trustworthy, multimodal, longitudinally validated systems linking early detection to actionable, personalized care.

</details>


### [206] [SEDR-Seq2P: A Lightweight Dilated Residual Sequence-to-Point Network for Multi-Task Industrial NILM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28693)
*Hatem Haddad,Feres Jerbi,Issam Smaali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对工业NILM中噪声和并发机器运行导致的泛化难题，本文提出了一种轻量级Seq2Point扩展模型SEDR-Seq2P，通过膨胀残差块和压缩激励注意力机制，在准确率-延迟权衡上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景下的非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）面临两大挑战：测量噪声和大量机器并发运行，这使得基于居民数据训练的模型泛化能力不足。现有模型如GRU和WaveNet虽然精度较高，但推理延迟过大，难以满足工业场景对实时性和可扩展部署的需求。

Method: 采用一对多、多任务负荷分解设置，在IMDELD数据集上统一评估五种基线模型（Seq2Seq、Seq2SubSeq、Seq2Point、GRU、WaveNet）。提出SEDR-Seq2P模型，在Seq2Point基础上引入膨胀残差块（dilated residual blocks）和压缩-激励注意力（squeeze-and-excitation attention）机制，兼顾精度与效率。

Result: 相比Seq2Point基线，SEDR-Seq2P的MAE降低约7%，决定系数提升约1%，匹配率提高约0.8%。相比WaveNet，推理延迟降低约58%，在精度-延迟权衡上表现更优，适合工业可扩展部署。

Conclusion: SEDR-Seq2P以轻量化设计实现了工业NILM场景下精度与推理效率的良好平衡，为大规模工业部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial NILM remains challenging because measurement noise and widespread concurrent machine operation reduce the generalization of models tuned on residential data. This work adopts a one-to-many, multi-task disaggregation setting, in which a single network estimates multiple industrial machine loads from aggregate power. Under a unified evaluation protocol on IMDELD, we benchmark Seq2Seq, Seq2SubSeq, Seq2Point, GRU, and WaveNet using energy-estimation metrics and the accuracy-delay criterion. While Seq2Point offers a stronger accuracy-delay balance than Seq2Seq/Seq2SubSeq, GRU and WaveNet achieve higher accuracy at markedly higher computational cost. To close this gap, we propose SEDR-Seq2P, a lightweight Seq2Point extension with dilated residual blocks and squeeze-and-excitation attention. Relative to the Seq2Point baseline, SEDR-Seq2P reduces MAE by approximately 7%, improves the coefficient of determination by approximately 1%, and increases the match rate by approximately 0.8%. In addition, compared to WaveNet, SEDR-Seq2P reduces inference latency by approximately 58%, yielding a favorable accuracy-delay trade-off for scalable industrial deployment.

</details>


### [207] [Predicting Steel Fatigue Life from Micrographs Using Physics-Informed Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28695)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CV（计算机视觉）框架，通过光学显微图像直接预测轻质合金钢的疲劳寿命（log Nf），无需物理测试。该框架结合OpenCV预处理、物理信息特征提取器和CNN回归模型，采用GNLL损失函数联合预测寿命及不确定性。在合成显微图像基准上，ResNet-50达到R²=0.93、RMSE=0.18对数周期，校准误差降低76%。推理速度<65ms/图，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统疲劳寿命评估需进行数十至数百小时的机械测试，无法满足快速质量控制的需求，因此需要一种基于显微图像的快速、非破坏性替代方法。

Method: 提出CV框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）七阶段OpenCV预处理流程，用于去除显微图像伪影；（2）28维物理信息特征提取器，量化裂纹形态、晶粒结构、孔隙率和织构等冶金特征；（3）CNN回归模型（对比SE-CNN、ResNet-50、VGG-16），采用高斯负对数似然（GNLL）损失函数，联合预测疲劳寿命（log Nf）和样本特异性不确定性（σ̂）。使用Grad-CAM进行可解释性分析。

Result: 在合成显微图像基准上，ResNet-50表现最佳：R²=0.93，RMSE=0.18对数周期，macro-F1=0.91。GNLL损失相比MSE基线将期望校准误差（ECE）从0.089降至0.021，降低76%。Grad-CAM热力图证实网络关注具有冶金学意义的微观结构特征。单张图像推理时间低于65毫秒。

Conclusion: 该框架在合成条件下验证了方法学的有效性，证明了从光学显微图像直接预测疲劳寿命的可行性。当前局限在于验证完全依赖合成数据，下一步关键工作是开展真实现场样本的域迁移研究。

Abstract: Here is the plain text version optimized for arXiv's submission form. Custom macros (like \CV and \SI) have been converted to standard text/math so they render correctly on the webpage: Evaluating the fatigue life of structural steels conventionally requires mechanical testing lasting tens to hundreds of hours, making it impractical for rapid quality control. We present CV, a computer vision framework that estimates the fatigue life ($\log N_f$) of lightweight alloy steels directly from optical micrographs without physical testing.The pipeline features a seven-stage OpenCV preprocessing routine to remove artifacts, a 28-dimensional physics-informed feature extractor (quantifying crack morphology, grain structure, porosity, and texture), and a CNN regression model trained with a Gaussian negative log-likelihood (GNLL) loss to jointly predict $\log N_f$ and sample-specific uncertainty $\hatσ$.Evaluating three architectures (SE-CNN, ResNet-50, VGG-16) on a synthetic micrograph benchmark, ResNet-50 achieves $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE = 0.18 log-cycles, and macro-F1 = 0.91. The GNLL objective reduces Expected Calibration Error by 76% compared to a mean-squared-error baseline (ECE: $0.089 \rightarrow 0.021$). Grad-CAM maps confirm the network attends to metallurgically meaningful microstructural features.Running in under 65 ms per image, the pipeline and synthetic dataset generator are open-sourced. Because validation relies entirely on synthetic micrographs, these results demonstrate methodological soundness under simulated conditions; a domain-transfer study on real field samples is the immediate next step.

</details>


### [208] [Mitigating Class-Tail Undercoverage in Medical Vision-Language Models under Clinical Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28696)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALCoDe 是一种后验可靠性层，面向冻结的医学 VLM，通过交叉验证识别类别覆盖率不足风险，并结合类别条件尾阈值与局部保形阈值，在保持预设覆盖率的同时，显著改善最差类别覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学 VLM 在临床分布偏移后，虽然整体边际覆盖率尚可，但单类别覆盖率严重不足，且受影响类别随采集协议和骨干网络几何结构变化，源域患病率无法可靠揭示失败模式。现有局部和尾部自适应保形方法均未能建模此类类别级覆盖率失效。

Method: CALCoDe 包含三个核心组件：(1) 交叉验证预测识别存在覆盖率不足风险的类别；(2) 在不相交的校准集上估计类别条件尾部阈值；(3) 将每个受保护阈值与局部保形阈值通过单边最大值组合，形成最终预测集。此外，独立校准的支持审计对内部支持不足的样本进行延迟处理。在受保护类别内，被接受样本满足可交换性假设下，提供有限样本覆盖率保证。

Result: 在 HAM10000→ISIC 2019 和 HAM10000→PAD-UFES-20 两种皮肤病学偏移及四种冻结 VLM 骨干网络（BiomedCLIP、OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32、PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32、MedSigLIP-448）共八种设置中，CALCoDe 是唯一边际覆盖率和最差类别接受覆盖率均达到 0.95 的方法。在 HAM10000→ISIC 2019 上，其平均最差类别接受覆盖率为 0.970，而 sTACP 为 0.926，LCP-VLM 为 0.864。

Conclusion: CALCoDe 有效解决了医学 VLM 在分布偏移下类别级覆盖率不足的问题，通过类别尾部自适应机制与局部保形阈值的融合，在多个骨干网络和偏移场景中一致性地达到预设覆盖率，且不牺牲整体边际覆盖性能。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) can retain high observed marginal coverage after clinical shift while substantially under-covering an individual disease class. The affected class varies with acquisition protocol and backbone geometry, so source prevalence does not reliably reveal the failure. Existing localized and tail-aware conformal methods respectively adapt to test neighborhoods and source-frequency tails, leaving held-out class-wise coverage failure unmodeled. We introduce Class-Tail Adaptive Localized Conformal Deferral (CALCoDe), a post-hoc reliability layer for frozen medical VLMs. Cross-fitted validation predictions identify classes at risk of undercoverage, and a disjoint calibration split estimates their class-conditional tail thresholds. CALCoDe combines each protected threshold with a localized conformal threshold using a one-sided maximum. The resulting set contains every label admitted by the localized rule, with additional protection confined to validation-identified classes. An independently calibrated support audit defers cases with insufficient inlier support. Under exchangeability among accepted examples within each protected class, CALCoDe provides finite-sample coverage at the prespecified guard level and contains the corresponding localized conformal sets; coverage on shifted external cohorts is evaluated empirically. Among standard conformal baselines and recent VLM-specific conformal methods evaluated across two dermatology shifts (HAM10000 to ISIC 2019 and HAM10000 to PAD-UFES-20) and four frozen VLM backbones (BiomedCLIP, OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32, PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32, and MedSigLIP-448), CALCoDe is the only approach whose observed marginal and worst-class accepted coverage both reach 0.95 in all eight settings. On HAM10000 to ISIC 2019, its average worst-class accepted coverage is 0.970, compared with 0.926 for sTACP and 0.864 for LCP-VLM.

</details>


### [209] [Flow Matching with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28698)
*Fairoz Nower Khan,Nabuat Zaman Nahim,Peizhong Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出缺失数据流匹配（Missing-Data Flow Matching），将缺失坐标视为潜变量，在流匹配损失上对其可能取值求平均。作者证明了该校正精确而非近似，并在完全随机缺失条件下，不完全数据目标等价于完全数据目标。有限样本分析揭示了反直觉的设计规律：缺失性转移而非增加估计方差、每样本一次补全即可匹配完全数据方差、固定预算下一次补全最优，学习到的补全模型引入单一不可约偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配假设训练数据完全观测，但现实应用普遍存在缺失数据。现有方法多采用先插补后建模的两阶段策略，缺乏对缺失数据下流匹配的理论理解，亟需一个原则性的统一框架来处理缺失数据下的生成建模。

Method: 将缺失坐标建模为潜变量，对条件期望下的流匹配损失求平均。理论层面：证明在MCAR且使用真实补全分布时，不完全数据目标与完全数据目标等价；有限样本分析给出方差转移性质及补全次数最优性结论；偏差通过期望条件Wasserstein距离上界控制。实验层面：在合成数据和真实表格数据上验证理论预测，并对比经典与深度插补基线。

Result: (1) 缺失性转移而非增加估计方差；(2) 每训练样本仅需一次补全即可匹配完全数据方差；(3) 固定评估预算下，一次补全最优；(4) 学习补全模型的偏差由期望条件Wasserstein距离界定；(5) 确定性插补（而非冻结插补）导致生成分布坍缩；(6) 在真实表格数据上性能与强基线相当。

Conclusion: 缺失数据流匹配提供了一个处理不完全数据的严格理论框架。核心洞见在于缺失性仅转移方差而非增加方差，且最优策略竟是每样本一次随机补全。该方法将学习难度完全转移到补全模型上，为缺失数据下的生成建模开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Flow matching assumes fully observed training data, which many real-world applications rarely provide. We propose Missing-Data Flow Matching, which treats the missing coordinates of training samples as latent variables and averages the flow matching loss over the values they could take. We first prove the correction is exact rather than approximate. Under missing completely at random with true completions, the incomplete-data objective equals the complete-data objective, so missingness changes nothing about what flow matching learns and the entire difficulty relocates to the completion model. Our finite-sample analysis then answers design questions that the algorithm leaves open, and the answers are not the ones intuition suggests. Missingness transfers estimator variance rather than adding it, one completion per example already matches complete-data variance exactly, and under a fixed evaluation budget one completion is optimal. A learned completion model contributes a single irreducible bias, which we bound by its expected conditional Wasserstein distance to the true completion law. Experiments numerically validate the theoretical predictions, show that deterministic rather than frozen imputation is what collapses the generated distribution, and place our method alongside strong classical and deep imputation baselines on real tabular data.

</details>


### [210] [MMFGU: Multimodal Federated Graph Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28708)
*Haodong Lu,Zekai Chen,Weiwei Ji,Shihao Li,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Yinlin Zhu,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MMFGU 是一个多模态联邦图遗忘框架，通过目标特定表征解耦，实现对异构多模态遗忘请求的高效处理，在保护保留信息的同时，相比全量重训练实现 41.5 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习中，用户可能产生细粒度的遗忘请求（如删除特定图像/文本、撤销模态间关联等），现有联邦图遗忘方法仅支持实体/关系或客户端级别的移除，无法直接处理此类多模态异构请求，面临三大挑战：仅移除请求信息而不损害保留内容、防止目标通过剩余模态或图邻居被恢复、阻止其他客户端上的关联痕迹在聚合后重新进入全局模型。

Method: 提出 MMFGU 框架，核心为"目标特定表征解耦"。具体包括：将异构遗忘请求映射为统一的目标载体（target carriers）；解耦被请求遗忘的表征同时锚定保留语义；利用轻量级探针暴露并修复传播残差；通过紧凑原型和响应信号选择性清除受影响客户端上的痕迹。

Result: 实验表明，MMFGU 能有效移除被请求的信息，同时保持保留图的效用，并且相比全量重训练实现了 41.5 倍的加速。

Conclusion: MMFGU 成功解决了多模态联邦图场景下的细粒度遗忘问题，通过表征解耦和残差修复机制，在遗忘效果、模型效用保留和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal federated graph learning enables clients to collaboratively train graph models over structural, textual, and visual signals without sharing private local data. However, the presence of heterogeneous multimodal content also makes unlearning requests more frequent and fine-grained: users may delete accounts or interactions, remove a particular image or text while retaining the associated entity, or revoke the learned correspondence between retained modalities or graph attributes. Existing federated graph unlearning mainly handles entity/relation or client removal and cannot directly satisfy these multimodal requests. They introduce three challenges: removing only the requested information without damaging retained content, preventing the target from being recovered through remaining modalities or graph neighborhoods, and stopping related traces on other clients from re-entering the global model after aggregation. To address them, we propose \textsc{\textbf{MMFGU}}, a multimodal federated graph unlearning framework built around target-specific representation decoupling. \textsc{MMFGU} maps heterogeneous requests into unified target carriers, decouples requested representations while anchoring retained semantics, exposes and repairs propagated residuals with lightweight probes, and selectively purges affected clients through compact prototype and response signals. Experiments show that \textsc{MMFGU} effectively removes requested information, preserves retained graph utility, and achieves a $\boldsymbol{41.5\times}$ speedup over full retraining.

</details>


### [211] [Mirror Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28737)
*Yunpeng Liu,Matthew Niedoba,Oluwanifemi A. Adekanye,Jason Yoo,Yingchen He,Berend Zwartsenberg,Frank Wood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"镜像学习"框架，通过微调视频扩散模型进行视角变换，结合逆动力学模型，将第三方观察转化为可执行的伪第一人称专家数据（镜像数据），从而从被动观察中学习可操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆依赖密集对齐的第一人称数据，无法利用人类和动物日常依赖的丰富第三方观察信号。作者希望突破这一局限，实现从被动第三方观察中获取可操作策略。

Method: 提出镜像学习框架，由两个核心组件构成：(i) 利用微调的视频扩散模型实现视角变换，将学习者"置于"示范者的视角；(ii) 逆动力学模型，在学习者控制空间中推断动作轨迹。二者结合将第三方观察合成为镜像数据（伪第一人称专家数据）。

Result: 实验表明，仅使用镜像数据即可训练出有效策略；将镜像数据与第一人称行为克隆训练数据结合，可进一步提升下游策略性能。

Conclusion: 现代生成式世界模型隐式编码了足够的结构信息，能够支撑一种可扩展且安全的替代方案，减少对大量遥操作数据采集的依赖。

Abstract: We investigate imitation learning through the lens of third-person observation and propose a framework for mirror learning: acquiring actionable policies from passive observation. While behavior cloning (BC) excels under dense, well-aligned first-person data, it fundamentally fails to leverage the rich observational signals arising from third-person demonstrations that humans and animals routinely exploit. We introduce a method that composes (i) a learned perspective transformation that places learners in demonstrators' shoes using a fine-tuned video diffusion model and (ii) an inverse dynamics model that infers action trajectories in the learners' control space. This enables the synthesis of mirror data, pseudo first-person expert data generated from third-person observations of demonstrator behavior. Empirically, we show that mirror data alone can train effective policies, and that augmenting first-person BC training with mirror data further improves downstream policy performance. Our results suggest that modern generative world models implicitly encode sufficient structure to enable a scalable and safe alternative to teleoperation-heavy data collection.

</details>


### [212] [TAGTorch: A PyTorch Library for Geometry, Topology, and Symmetry-Aware Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28755)
*Brendan Kennedy,Tegan Emerson,Gregory Roek,Emilie Purvine,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TAGTorch，一个基于PyTorch的开源库，旨在统一拓扑、代数和几何启发的神经网络工具，包括数据预处理、架构、训练技术和模型分析，以解决该领域软件生态碎片化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管受拓扑、代数和几何启发的神经网络方法在过去十年中蓬勃发展，但支持这些方法的软件生态系统高度碎片化，许多重要方法仅以无人维护的研究原型形式存在，缺乏统一的工具库。

Method: 作者开发了TAGTorch（Topology, Algebra, and Geometry Torch），一个基于PyTorch的开源统一库，整合了数据预处理方法、模型架构、训练技术和模型分析工具，并提供统一的设计哲学与API。

Result: TAGTorch填补了当前软件生态系统中的多项空白，统一了拓扑、代数、几何启发的神经网络工具，提供了结构化的架构设计和能力描述。

Conclusion: TAGTorch为该领域提供了一个亟需的统一软件框架，未来将持续按优先级推进开发，进一步丰富和完善库的功能。

Abstract: Over the last decade, neural networks have been applied to an increasingly diverse range of applications, including data with rich geometric, topological, or symmetry-related structure. As a result, researchers have increasingly drawn inspiration from topology, algebra, and geometry. Despite this rich algorithmic development, the supporting software ecosystem remains fragmented. Many important methods exist only as research prototypes in unmaintained repositories. We address this by introducing Topology, Algebra, and Geometry Torch (TAGTorch), an open-source, PyTorch-based library that unifies tools inspired by topology, algebra, and geometry, including data-preprocessing methods, architectures, training techniques, and model analysis tools. We describe the design philosophy of TAGTorch and then discuss its current architecture and capabilities, highlighting areas where it can fill gaps in the current software ecosystem. We conclude with a discussion of our future development priorities for the library.

</details>


### [213] [TerraNova: A Foundation Model for the Anthropocene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29527)
*Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TerraNova是一个基础模型，在1024个物理与社会记录的原生几何形态上进行训练——512个网格化地球系统场和512个国家指标。它通过专用编码器、跨模态Transformer和超网络生成查询相关的预测分布，实现对物理地球与人类社会的耦合建模，避免了传统方法中跨边界平均所带来的信息损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于人类世 (Anthropocene) 面临的一个根本性难题：物理地球以无视政治边界的连续场（网格化数据）来测量，而人类社会数据则按行政单元（国家）报告。现有地球系统基础模型仅服务于第一种几何形态，耦合到第二种时需要进行有损的边界平均，丧失了空间精度。因此需要一个能在两种原生几何形态上统一学习和推断的表征。

Method: (1) 双几何编码：专用编码器分别处理512个网格化地球系统场和512个国家指标；(2) 多模态融合：专用编码器嵌入位置、国家、时间和任务信息，经跨模态Transformer融合为共享时空状态；(3) 超网络解码：超网络为每个查询生成一个专门的解码器，其证据头 (evidential head) 输出预测分布；(4) 双对比学习：人口加权对比（将国家表征与其领土内坐标对齐）以及与预训练地理空间嵌入的对比（引入图像语义）；(5) 冻结骨干网络可用于从稀疏观测重建密集场，并在消费级硬件上数分钟内适配未见过的变量。

Result: TerraNova的表征在性能上与专用地理空间编码器相当，同时覆盖了后者未涉及的维度（时间、海洋和不确定性），并支持国家层面的能力。冻结骨干网络可高效地从稀疏观测重建密集场，对新变量的适配仅需数分钟（消费级硬件）。

Conclusion: TerraNova首次实现了在物理地球与人类社会的原生几何形态上的统一基础建模，通过双对比学习和超网络解码架构弥合了网格场与国家单元之间的几何鸿沟，为人类世耦合系统建模提供了新的可能性，兼具竞争力、泛化性和实用部署效率。

Abstract: A defining problem of the Anthropocene is to model the physical Earth and human societies as one coupled system, yet no learned representation spans their observational breadth. We argue the obstacle is geometric: the physical Earth is measured as continuous fields that ignore political borders, whereas societies are reported for administrative units. Earth-system foundation models serve the first geometry; coupling it to the second has required lossy averaging over borders. We introduce TerraNova, a foundation model trained on 1,024 physical and societal records in their native geometries: 512 gridded Earth-system fields and 512 national indicators. Dedicated encoders represent location, country, time and task, cross-modal transformers fuse them into a shared spatiotemporal state, and a hypernetwork generates a per-query decoder whose evidential head returns a predictive distribution. Two contrastive objectives couple the representation: a population-weighted alignment between each country and coordinates in its territory, and one to pretrained geospatial embeddings carrying image-derived semantics. Read out through that decoder, the representation is competitive with purpose-built geospatial encoders while spanning axes they do not represent (time, oceans and uncertainty) and supporting country-level capabilities. The frozen backbone reconstructs dense fields from sparse observations and adapts to unseen variables in minutes on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [214] [Feature Interaction Modeling for Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28762)
*Quan Gu,Hongxia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将因子分解机（FM）的特征交互模块嵌入物理信息神经网络（PINNs）和神经算子学习框架中，以增强参数化偏微分方程解流形的模型表达能力。核心贡献包括FM-PINN（捕捉时空变量交互）和FM-Operator/FM-DeepONet（建模跨组特征交互），在冲击主导的非线性守恒律问题上取得显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受多元函数二阶泰勒展开刻画变量耦合关系的启发，现有PINNs和神经算子学习方法对参数化PDE中变量间（时空坐标、物理参数、初边值条件）的交叉依赖关系建模不足，尤其在高阶光滑PDE和非线性守恒律（激波、尖锐梯度）问题上表达能力受限。

Method: 提出三种方法：（1）FM-PINN：将FM的特征交互机制嵌入PINN，显式捕捉时空变量的二阶交互，提升对光滑高阶PDE的逼近精度；（2）FM-Operator与FM-DeepONet：将空间坐标、时间、物理参数、初边值条件划分为独立特征组，建模其交叉组交互，特别适用于含激波或不连续的问题。

Result: 数值实验表明：（1）FM-PINN在光滑高阶PDE上近似精度持续提升；（2）FM-Operator和FM-DeepONet在非线性守恒律及含尖锐梯度/间断的问题上效果显著，但在光滑算子学习基准上未表现出持续优势；（3）所提机制在激波主导方程上精度增益显著。

Conclusion: 将FM特征交互引入物理约束的神经网络与算子学习是一种有前景的方向，尤其适用于具有强交叉场依赖的参数化PDE建模，为激波等复杂物理现象的物理一致性建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: This work embeds feature interaction modules derived from factorization machines (FMs) into physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) and neural operator learning, to enhance model expressiveness for solution manifolds of parameterized partial differential equations (PDEs). Motivated by the second-order Taylor expansion of multivariate functions to characterize variable couplings, we first propose FM-PINN. It explicitly captures spatio-temporal variable interactions and improves the approximation accuracy for smooth high-order PDEs. We further group spatial coordinates, time, physical parameters, and initial and boundary conditions into independent feature sets and model their cross-group interactions. Based on this strategy, we develop FM-Operator and FM-DeepONet, which are particularly effective for nonlinear conservation laws and problems with sharp gradients or discontinuities, while offering no consistent advantage on smooth operator learning benchmarks. Numerical tests demonstrate that the proposed mechanism delivers substantial accuracy gains on challenging shock-dominated equations, indicating a promising direction for physics-consistent modeling of parameterized PDEs with strong cross-field dependencies.

</details>


### [215] [When Does On-Policy Interaction Help? Representational Tradeoffs in Value-Based Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29617)
*Luca Viano,Antoine Moulin,Audrey Huang,Volkan Cevher,Philip Amortila,Dylan J. Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了交互式模仿学习中专家交互与价值函数估计的协同作用，提出OVI算法，证明当学习者能表示专家价值函数时即可实现统计高效学习，无需完美复现专家策略，且交互是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BC等标准模仿学习方法存在复合误差与性能瓶颈，尤其在蒸馏等场景中学习者无法完美表示专家策略。虽然交互式查询和价值函数估计两种干预手段在实践中被广泛使用且效果显著，但二者各自改进的理论本质及其潜在交互机制尚不清楚。

Method: 作者提出OVI（On-policy Value-based Imitation），一种交互式在策略模仿学习算法。该算法在仅需学习者能表示专家价值函数（而非策略本身）的条件下，即可实现统计效率；配合线性最大化预言机时同时具备计算效率。此外，通过负向结果证明了交互的必要性：仅依赖专家价值可实现性，任何离线IL算法的样本复杂度必须随专家策略类的复杂度缩放。

Result: OVI在实验中显著优于离线策略方法（BC）、交互式策略方法（DAgger）和离线价值方法。当学习者网络的表达能力远弱于专家网络时，OVI的优势最为显著，验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 专家交互的核心价值在于降低了对学习者的表示需求——从实现专家策略放松至实现专家价值函数。OVI算法有效利用了这种交互与价值估计的协同作用，在理论和实践上均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL)---training an agent to replicate expert behavior from demonstrations---underpins applications from robotics to language model training.
