Standard approaches such as Behavior Cloning (BC) are known to suffer from compounding errors and performance plateaus, particularly when the learner cannot perfectly represent the expert's policy (as is typical, e.g., in distillation). Two interventions are widely understood empirically to improve performance: querying the expert interactively along the learner's own trajectories, and using value function estimation en route to generating a policy rather than directly fitting the expert's full action distribution.
  We investigate the nature of these improvements and their potentially surprising interplay. Our main finding is that expert interaction relaxes the representational demands on the learner: one only needs a model capable of realizing the expert's value function, bypassing the (often stricter) requirement of realizing the expert's policy itself. Concretely, we introduce OVI, an interactive on-policy IL algorithm that is statistically efficient whenever the learner can represent the expert's value function and computationally efficient given access to a linear maximization oracle. We complement this with a negative result showing that interaction is necessary. Namely, without stronger assumptions beyond expert-value realizability alone, any offline IL algorithm must scale with the complexity of the expert policy class. Our findings bear out empirically. OVI outperforms offline policy-based (BC), interactive policy-based (DAgger), and offline value-based IL methods, with the largest gains when the learner network is substantially less expressive than the expert's.

</details>


### [216] [Representations from Pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials as Coarse Coordinates for Material Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28776)
*Paul Hagemann,Katharina Ueltzen,Simon Müller,Janine George,Philipp Benner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预训练机器学习原子间势（如MACE）的原子平均特征进行无机晶体结构生成评估，引入粗-细传输距离（CFTD）来同时衡量生成结构的质量和新颖性，并展示粗粒度MACE特征可作为生成模型的引导信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无机晶体结构生成模型及其评估方法大多依赖简单的晶体结构表示，缺乏能够同时有效评估生成结构质量和新颖性的统一框架。作者旨在利用预训练MLIP提取的丰富特征来解决这一不足。

Method: 1）提出基于粗粒度MACE特征和细粒度特征的粗-细传输距离（CFTD），在单一分布评估框架中同时捕捉质量和新颖性；2）利用CFTD与连续SUN指标进行对比；3）将粗粒度MACE特征作为条件信号引导材料生成模型。

Result: CFTD能够有效捕捉晶体结构质量，同时检测记忆化现象；与连续SUN指标相比展现了其通用性；粗粒度MACE特征可成功用于指导材料生成模型。

Conclusion: 原子平均的预训练MLIP特征（尤其是MACE的粗粒度特征）在晶体结构生成评估和引导方面具有强大能力，CFTD提供了一个统一的质量-新颖性评估框架。

Abstract: Generative machine learning is increasingly used for inorganic crystal structure generation. Most models and the corresponding evaluation approaches rely on simple forms of crystal structure representation. In this paper, we showcase the power of atom-averaged features from pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials (MLIPs), such as MACE, for such tasks. We first introduce a distance measure that assesses the output of material generative models by capturing both quality and novelty in a single distribution-based evaluation framework. In particular, we introduce the Coarse-Fine Transport Distance (CFTD) using two different featurizers, where the quality component is based on coarse MACE features. We showcase CFTD's versatility in capturing crystal-structure quality while also detecting memorization, and compare it with the recently introduced continuous SUN metrics. We further show that coarse MACE features can be used as guidance for a material generative model.

</details>


### [217] [Distilling Knowledge from Large Language Models into Lightweight Reinforcement Learning Agents for Autonomous Cyber Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28826)
*Konur Tholl,François Rivest,Mariam El Mezouar,Adrian Taylor,Ranwa Al Mallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型（LLM）改进自主网络防御决策，通过提示工程使80亿参数的网络安全LLM在CybORG环境中超越基线RL智能体，并提出在线策略蒸馏框架将LLM的防御策略迁移至仅64,910参数的轻量RL智能体，在保持防御能力的同时大幅压缩模型规模，为前沿网络安全模型的实际部署提供可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主网络操作（ACO）中强化学习（RL）智能体在训练阶段需要大量探索，导致行为不稳定和初始决策质量差，收敛到有效防御策略的过程缓慢。需要一种方法来改善这一缺陷，同时避免RL智能体的大规模部署开销。

Method: 1）采用提示工程（非微调）方式，利用80亿参数、在网络安全数据上预训练的LLM在修改版CybORG CAGE Challenge 2环境中执行防御决策；2）提出在线策略蒸馏框架，将LLM教师模型的防御策略迁移到仅含64,910参数的轻量RL学生智能体中；3）在4至12台主机的CybORG场景中评估可迁移性，并测试教师引导的RL稳定化策略。

Result: 1）LLM通过提示工程即可超越基线RL智能体的防御表现；2）策略蒸馏成功将模型规模压缩数个数量级（从80亿参数降至64,910参数），同时保持有效防御能力；3）教师引导的RL稳定化策略均未能持续超越优化后的教师策略，揭示了奖励驱动的RL优化与教师引导防御策略之间存在策略对齐的局限性。

Conclusion: 网络安全领域专用的LLM可作为自主网络防御的专家知识来源，而策略蒸馏则为将前沿大型模型的能力压缩至高效、可扩展的轻量部署智能体提供了实用路径，二者结合为ACO的实际落地奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous Cyber Operations (ACO) are increasingly important for defending enterprise networks as cyber threats continue to evolve in sophistication. ACO applications commonly employ Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents to learn defensive behaviors through interaction with environments. However, RL agents typically require extensive exploration during training, often resulting in unstable behavior and poor initial decision-making before converging toward effective defense strategies.
  In this work, we investigate the use of a Large Language Model (LLM) to improve autonomous defensive decision-making within an ACO environment. Through prompt engineering rather than fine-tuning, we demonstrate that an 8-billion parameter LLM pretrained on cybersecurity data can outperform a baseline RL agent in a modified CybORG CAGE Challenge 2 environment. We then propose an online policy distillation framework that transfers the LLM's defensive policy into a lightweight RL agent containing only 64,910 parameters, reducing model size by several orders of magnitude while maintaining effective defensive capabilities. This provides a pathway toward operationalizing frontier cybersecurity models within lightweight, deployable agents.
  To evaluate transferability, we construct CybORG scenarios ranging from 4 to 12 hosts and assess the approach across varying network configurations. We also evaluate teacher-guided RL stabilization strategies and observe that none consistently surpass the optimized teacher policy, suggesting policy-alignment limitations between reward-driven RL optimization and teacher-guided defense strategies.
  Our results demonstrate the potential of cybersecurity-focused LLMs as sources of expertise for autonomous cyber defense, while policy distillation provides a practical path toward operationalizing frontier cybersecurity models within efficient, scalable agents.

</details>


### [218] [Hypergradient-based Bilevel Reinforcement Learning with Improved Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28849)
*Naman Saxena,Mudit Gaur,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于超梯度的双层强化学习算法，利用Boltzmann策略在熵正则化折扣RL目标下的最优性，实现了无Hessian计算，并在温和条件下达到$O(ε^{-1})$迭代复杂度和$\tilde{O}(ε^{-2})$的最先进样本复杂度，同时移除了此前工作中对外层目标函数的Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层RL算法面临两难困境：基于超梯度的方法因需要计算Hessian而难以扩展，基于惩罚近似的方法则样本复杂度高。本文旨在设计一种兼具可扩展性和低样本复杂度的双层RL算法。

Method: 利用Boltzmann策略在熵正则化折扣RL目标下的最优性，提出一种无Hessian的超梯度双层RL算法，并给出严格的收敛性分析。

Result: 算法在温和正则条件下达到$O(ε^{-1})$迭代复杂度和$\tilde{O}(ε^{-2})$样本复杂度，且无需假设外层目标满足PL条件，优于此前最优样本复杂度工作。

Conclusion: 该算法成功弥合了超梯度方法与惩罚近似方法之间的鸿沟，在理论保证和实践可扩展性上均取得突破，为双层RL提供了新的高效求解范式。

Abstract: Bilevel reinforcement learning (RL) is an important framework within the literature of RL that can be used to formalize various categories of problems, such as meta-learning, hierarchical task decomposition, and reinforcement learning from human feedback (RL-HF). Most of the bilevel RL algorithms are either not scalable because of using hypergradient with Hessian, or they suffer from high sample complexity because of using penalty-based approximation methods. In this work, we propose a hypergradient-based bilevel RL algorithm using the optimality of the Boltzmann policy for the entropy regularized discounted RL objective function. Our proposed algorithm is Hessian-free and obtains an iteration complexity of $O(ε^{-1})$ and state-of-the-art sample complexity of $\tilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ under mild regularity conditions. Further, in our convergence analysis, we are able to remove the assumption of the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition on the outer-level objective function present in the prior state-of-the-art sample complexity work.

</details>


### [219] [An analysis of machine learning approaches for enhancing decision-making in complex discrete choice tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28854)
*Sheng Lun Christine Cao,Destenie Nock,Alex Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了四种机器学习模型在个体异质性下学习离散选择规则的能力，通过蒙特卡洛实验和真实能源政策数据案例，证明半参数和非参数模型普遍优于参数模型，且模型选择应受选择任务情境驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散选择建模通常依赖参数模型进行政策制定中的偏好获取，但机器学习方法在个体异质性下估计离散选择规则的能力尚缺乏系统评估，尤其在实际偏好获取面临挑战的情境下。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛实验评估四种模型（多项逻辑回归、广义加性模型、孪生神经网络、高斯过程）在五种选择规则（线性强效用、单调强效用、理想点、词典半序、多属性线性弹道累加器）下的表现，操纵属性数量、训练选择集数量和选择规则确定性三个变量，并以真实能源政策偏好数据作为案例验证。

Result: 半参数和非参数模型在所有选择规则和实验情境下普遍优于参数模型；训练集增加使性能提升6%-96%，确定性提升带来0%-55%的改善；案例研究中TNN表现最佳（BIC=13.351）。

Conclusion: 半参数和非参数模型在政策导向的离散选择建模中具有可行性但也存在局限，选择任务的具体情境应作为模型选择的关键依据。

Abstract: Discrete choice modeling is a common tool used for preference elicitation during policy-making, but this is typically done through parametric models. Machine learning can push the boundaries of discrete choice modeling for policy-based preference elicitation by adopting a data-driven approach or learning individual preferences. However, there is limited knowledge of how well machine learning methods can estimate individual discrete choice rules under individual heterogeneity, especially in the context of challenges often experienced during preference elicitation. This study evaluates four machine learning models (multinomial logistic regression, generalized additive model, twinned neural network, and Gaussian process) with respect to their capacity to learn and predict five choice rules that are important in the behavioral and social sciences (linear strong utility, monotonic strong utility, ideal point, lexicographic semiorder, and multiattribute linear ballistic accumulator). Monte Carlo experiments were performed to assess model performance when increasing a) the number of attributes in the choice alternatives, b) the number of training choice sets, and c) the choice rule's determinism. The simulation results demonstrated that semi-parametric and non-parametric models generally outperform parametric models across all choice rules and experimental contexts. Model performance also generally improves by 6% to 96% and 0% to 55%, respectively, with an increase in training choice sets and choice rule determinism. A case study using real energy policy preference data was also conducted, where TNN performed best with a BIC of 13.351. This work demonstrated the viability and limitations of semi-parametric and non-parametric models in the context of policy-centric discrete choice modeling and showed how the choice task context should drive model selection.

</details>


### [220] [Fast Rates for Swap-Agnostic Learning of Proper Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28856)
*Princewill Okoroafor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"交换不可知学习"（swap-agnostic learning），允许比较器在学习者预测的每个水平集上选择不同假设。作者通过Blackwell可逼近性与Bernstein方差校正，将交换不可知学习归约为二阶多重校准，为任意固定适当损失函数给出了离线与在线学习算法，其速率在平滑、有界及Lipschitz适当损失下均达到紧界，显著改进了已有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典不可知学习要求单一比较器，而实际场景中常需根据预测值进行后处理（如不同分数段采用不同决策）。交换不可知学习捕获了这种预测依赖性后处理，但直接处理需为每个可能预测值求解独立的不可知学习问题，计算上不可行。本文旨在找到一种联合控制这些预测水平比较的方法。

Method: 核心技术贡献是将交换不可知学习归约为一种二阶形式的多重校准（second-order multicalibration）。具体通过Blackwell可逼近性框架配合Bernstein风格的方差校正来实现。基于此归约，为有限假设类H设计了算法：(1)对固定光滑适当损失，获得离线超额风险界Õ((log|H|/m)^{2/3})和在线交换遗憾界Õ(T^{1/3}(log|H|)^{2/3})；(2)对[-1,1]有界适当损失，获得Õ(√(T log|H|))在线和Õ(√(log|H|/m))离线界；(3)对凸1-Lipschitz适当损失，速率改进为Õ(T^{1/3}(log|H|)^{2/3})在线和Õ((log|H|/m)^{2/3})离线。

Result: 所获速率在忽略对数因子下是紧的，且显著改进了Luo等人(2025)的交换全预测保证所隐含的Õ(T^{2/3}(log|H|)^{1/3})速率。同时给出了可同时对整个损失函数族实现交换不可知预测的算法。

Conclusion: 交换不可知学习可以通过二阶多重校准统一处理，无需为每个预测水平独立求解。该方法在适当损失族上取得了最优速率，为预测依赖后处理提供了坚实的理论和算法基础。

Abstract: Swap-agnostic learning strengthens classical agnostic learning by allowing the comparator to select a different hypothesis on each level set of the learner's predictions. This benchmark captures prediction-dependent postprocessing, but appears to require solving a separate agnostic-learning problem for every possible prediction value. We show that, for proper losses, these prediction-level comparisons can instead be controlled jointly. Our main result is an offline swap-agnostic learner for any fixed proper loss. For a finite hypothesis class $H$ and any fixed smooth proper loss, the excess risk from $m$ i.i.d. samples is $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$, with a corresponding online swap-regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$. We also give algorithms whose predictions are simultaneously swap-agnostic for entire families of losses. For all proper losses bounded in $[-1,1]$, we obtain online and offline rates of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T\log |H|})$ and $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\log |H|/m})$, respectively. For convex, $1$-Lipschitz proper losses, these rates improve to $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$ online and $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$ offline. These bounds are tight up to logarithmic factors and improve upon the $\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3}(\log |H|)^{1/3})$ rate implied by the swap-omniprediction guarantee of Luo et al. (2025). Our main technical contribution is a reduction from swap-agnostic learning to a second-order form of multicalibration, obtained via Blackwell approachability with a Bernstein-style variance correction.

</details>


### [221] [Adaptivity via a Parallel Architecture for Stochastic Gradient Methods Adaptivity via a Parallel Architecture for Stochastic Gradient Methods Adaptivity via a Parallel Architecture for Stochastic Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28902)
*Bin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种并行框架，通过组合多个静态梯度方法（GD），按几何序列搜索合适的迭代次数 T，以实现更好的自适应性。每个处理器在不同阶段执行不同 T 值的梯度下降，从而满足收敛条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的梯度下降方法中，步长调度依赖于预先指定的迭代次数 T，而选择合适的 T 对收敛性能至关重要。作者希望通过并行化的方式，让多个处理器同时探索不同的 T 值，从而自动找到满足收敛条件的合适参数，提升方法的自适应能力。

Method: 定义静态梯度方法 GD(x₀, T)，其步长 s = S(T) 由预设函数确定，执行 ⌊T⌋ 次迭代。在并行框架中，p 个处理器按无限阶段序列运行，阶段 i 中处理器 j 被分配 T_{j,i} = h(j, i)，其中 h 为预设的分配函数（基于几何序列）。各处理器独立执行 GD(x₀, T_{j,i})，实现对不同 T 值的并行搜索。

Result: 论文摘要未明确给出具体数值结果，但指出该框架能使梯度下降满足期望的收敛条件，通过并行搜索自动适配合适的 T 值。

Conclusion: 通过并行装配多个静态梯度方法并按几何序列搜索 T 值，可以有效实现自适应梯度下降，无需人工调参即可满足收敛要求。

Abstract: We develop a parallel framework that assembles static gradient methods to achieve better adaptivity. A static gradient method, denoted by $\mathrm{GD}(x_0,T)$, takes as input an initial point $x_0\in\mathbb{R}^n$ and $T\in \mathbb{R}^+$ specifying the number $\floor{T}$ of iterations. The step size is chosen as $s=S(T)$, where $S(\cdot)$ is a predetermined function of $T$. The method then performs the iterations $ x_{i+1}=x_i-\fracη{s}\cdot g_i,$ where $g_i$ is a stochastic gradient evaluated at $x_i$, and $η$ is a scaling factor. For an integer $p\ge1$, the $p$ processors in the proposed parallel framework search for an appropriate value of $T$ according to a geometric sequence so that the resulting gradient descent satisfies the desired convergence conditions. Each processor executes an infinite sequence of stages indexed by $i=1,2,\ldots$. At stage $i$, processor $j$ is assigned $ T_{j,i}=h(j,i),$ where $h:\mathbb{N}\times\mathbb{N} \rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{+}$ is a prescribed function. Processor $j$ $(j=0,1,\ldots,p-1)$ executes $\mathrm{GD}(x_0, T_{j,i})$ at stage $i$.

</details>


### [222] [Reflection or Re-Generation? Why LLM Revision Fails Where Human Revision Succeeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28908)
*Yefan Tao,Gerald Friedland,Madhusudhanan Chandrasekaran,Luyang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文引入Human-LLM Reflection Framework (HRF)，在受控双轮协议下比较人类与LLM的"反思"行为。通过信息论分析发现，LLM反思存在两种失败模式：客观任务中信息增益趋近于零（等同于重采样），主观任务中产生负信息增益（偏离正确答案）。失败根源在于修订步骤本身，而非输入质量。核心结论是：缺乏外部信息时，自我条件化修订无法降低目标不确定性，LLM反思应被理解为"条件化再生成"而非真正的纠错驱动修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被提示进行"反思"，但这种行为是否真正类似于人类的纠错性修订尚不明确。现有研究缺乏在统一控制条件下对人类与LLM反思的系统性比较，亟需揭示LLM反思的内在机制与局限。

Method: 提出Human-LLM Reflection Framework (HRF)——一个受控双轮协议，在自我反思、同行反思和跨代理反思三种设置下，对人和LLM的修订进行统一比较。核心分析方法为基于信息的逐轮交叉熵减少量（ΔI）分析。此外，还进行了诊断性分析：按首轮正确性条件化的修订表现，以及基于预言机错误信号的修订与随机重排基线的对比。

Result: 发现LLM反思的两种失败模式：(1) 在有限答案空间的客观任务上，反思的信息增益接近零（ΔI≈0），行为等同于中性重生成；(2) 在主观任务上，反思产生显著负信息增益（ΔI<0），使预测偏离目标。跨代理实验将失败定位到修订步骤而非输入质量：LLM甚至会劣化高质量人类回答。诊断分析表明，具体失败子步骤因任务和模型而异：自检错在客观选择题上存在但主观任务上较弱；在预言机错误信号下，部分模型恢复能力超基线，部分则低于基线。

Conclusion: 统一解释是结构性的：缺乏外部信息时，自我条件化修订无法降低关于目标的不确定性，因此LLM反思更应理解为"条件化再生成"而非真正的纠错驱动修订。这一发现对LLM推理增强策略的设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Reflection, the ability to revisit and revise prior reasoning, is central to how humans improve their answers. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly prompted to "reflect," yet whether this resembles human revision remains unclear. We introduce the Human-LLM Reflection Framework (HRF), a controlled two-pass protocol comparing human and LLM revision under identical conditions across self-, peer-, and cross-agent settings. Using an information-theoretic analysis based on per-iteration cross-entropy reduction, we find two failure modes of LLM reflection. On objective tasks with finite answer spaces, reflection yields near-zero information gain (Delta I approx 0), behaving as neutral re-generation indistinguishable from re-sampling. On subjective tasks, it yields significant negative gain (Delta I < 0), moving predictions away from the target. Human revision, by contrast, yields positive gain in both settings. Cross-agent experiments localize the failure to the revision step, not input quality: LLMs degrade even high-quality human responses. Diagnostic analyses (revision conditioned on first-pass correctness, and oracle-guided revision against a random-reshuffle baseline) show that which sub-step dominates varies by task and by model rather than reducing to a single mechanism: self-error detection is present on objective multiple-choice tasks but weak on subjective ones, and recovery under an oracle error signal exceeds the baseline for some models and falls below it for others. The unifying account is structural: without external information, self-conditioned revision cannot reduce uncertainty about the target, so LLM reflection is better understood as conditioned re-generation than as genuine error-driven revision.

</details>


### [223] [Gated Q-learning: Add Off-Policy Bias to Taste](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28916)
*Brett Daley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gated Q-learning框架，通过连续的状态-动作门控机制在Watkins的Q(λ)与Peng的Q(λ)之间平滑插值，解决了多步信用分配中离线策略偏差与资格迹截断之间的长期矛盾，理论证明该算子保持收缩映射，实验表明中间门控可实现更快初始学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Q-learning中多步信用分配面临根本性困境：Watkins的Q(λ)通过截断资格迹消除离线策略偏差，但牺牲了学习速度；Peng的Q(λ)忽略偏差以加速学习，却引入有害误差。现有离线策略估计器依赖重要性采样，在Q-learning的贪心目标策略下重要性比率会崩溃，无法解决这一矛盾。历时30年，该领域始终缺乏一个能在两极端之间灵活折中的方案。

Method: 提出Gated Q-learning算法框架：核心是一个连续的、状态-动作依赖的门控函数（gating function），该函数以探索感知（exploration-aware）的方式选择性地衰减资格迹，替代传统的重要性采样。门控程度可在0（Watkins风格，完全截断）到1（Peng风格，完全保留）之间连续调节。理论证明：期望算子保持收缩映射（contraction mapping），并推导出精确不动点（fixed point）。

Result: 理论层面，证明了门控机制下期望Bellman算子仍是收缩映射，保证了收敛性，并给出了不动点的闭式解。实验层面，中间门控值能够安全地实现更长的信用分配视野，初始学习速度优于两种极端方法（Watkins's Q(λ)和Peng's Q(λ)）。

Conclusion: Gated Q-learning终结了Q(λ)变体中长达30年的二元选择困境，为Q-learning智能体提供了一种简单的重要性采样替代方案，同时允许灵活定制有效的多步视野与离线策略偏差容忍度。

Abstract: Multistep credit assignment is critical for sample-efficient reinforcement learning, yet managing off-policy bias in Q-learning remains a fundamental challenge. For 30 years, practitioners have been limited to a binary choice: eliminate the bias at the cost of severely truncated eligibility traces (Watkins' Q($λ$)), or ignore the bias to learn faster while injecting detrimental errors into the value estimates (Peng's Q($λ$)). Modern off-policy estimators fail to resolve this tension, as importance-sampling ratios collapse under Q-learning's greedy target policy. We introduce Gated Q-learning, a novel algorithmic framework that ends this dilemma by smoothly interpolating between the two historical extremes. Rather than relying on importance sampling, our approach employs a continuous, state-action-dependent gating mechanism to selectively attenuate eligibility traces in an exploration-aware manner. We provide a rigorous theoretical foundation for this mechanism, proving that the expected operator remains a contraction mapping and deriving its exact fixed point. Empirical evaluations verify that intermediate gating safely enables longer credit-assignment horizons, yielding faster initial learning than either extreme. Gated Q-learning offers a simple alternative to importance sampling while enabling customization of the effective multistep horizon and the amount of off-policy bias in Q-learning agents.

</details>


### [224] [Learning Optimal Dynamic Matching via Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28925)
*Genta Okada,Shunya Noda,Junpei Komiyama,Akira Matsushita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于值的强化学习框架，用于有限演化加权图上的动态匹配问题。核心创新在于将状态-动作Q函数约简为残差图上的单一续值函数，并结合图神经网络与时序差分学习进行近似，最终通过前向贪心匹配启发式实现策略。在两类基准测试中，该方法均展现出优于传统即时/阈值贪心规则的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态匹配市场中存在"何时匹配"与"匹配谁"的双重决策困境：当前匹配虽产生即时收益，但会消耗参与者，可能错失未来更优匹配机会。现有方法难以有效权衡这一跨期取舍，需要一种能够根据图的连接状态和退出信息自适应调整匹配策略的方法。

Method: 作者建立了一个无限时间连续时间模型，包含随机到达、节点类型转移、边实现和外生退出。首先证明了"事件时间约简"定理：规划者仅需在每个外生事件发生后立即行动，无需在事件之间做决策。进而证明最优边Q函数可由决策后残差图上的单一续值函数完全刻画，将学习对象从状态-动作值降维为图值函数。实际操作中，用图神经网络近似该值函数，通过时序差分学习训练，并嵌入前向贪心匹配启发式进行动作选择。

Result: 在二元类型基准中，学习策略通过在高价值稀有到达出现时保留公共节点、仅在密集池中形成低价值匹配，显著优于即时匹配和阈值贪心规则。在肾脏配对捐献基准中：当退出不可预测时，其表现与即时贪心相当；当预警可靠时，恢复出耐心匹配逻辑；在中等预警概率下，优于即时贪心和耐心贪心中较好者。

Conclusion: 残差图值学习能够产生依赖状态的动态匹配策略，该策略可根据已实现的连接结构和退出信息自适应调整，在多种环境下均展现出优越的权衡能力。

Abstract: Dynamic matching markets require decisions about whom to match and when: matching now yields value but removes participants who may create better future opportunities. We develop a value-based reinforcement-learning framework for this problem on finite, evolving weighted graphs. We study an infinite-horizon continuous-time model with stochastic arrivals, node-type transitions, edge realizations, and exogenous exits. We prove an event-time reduction: without loss of optimality, the planner acts immediately after each exogenous event and then waits for the next one. We further show that the optimal edge-wise $Q$-function is characterized by a single continuation-value function on post-decision residual graphs, reducing the learned object from state-action values to graph values. Exact action selection still requires combinatorial matching optimization; we approximate the value with a graph neural network, train it by temporal-difference learning, and use it in a forward-greedy matching heuristic. In a binary-type benchmark, the learned policy substantially outperforms immediate and threshold-greedy rules by preserving common nodes for rare arrivals of valuable matches while forming lower-value matches only in thick pools. In a kidney paired donation benchmark, it performs similarly to immediate greedy when exits are unpredictable, recovers the logic of patient matching when warnings are reliable, and outperforms the better of Immediate Greedy and Patient Greedy across intermediate warning probabilities. These results show that residual-graph value learning yields state-dependent dynamic matching policies that adapt to realized connectivity and exit information.

</details>


### [225] [Latent Lie-Poisson Neural Networks (LLPNNs): Discovering the motion of Lie-Poisson systems through observable data and latent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28939)
*Vakhtang Putkaradze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在李-泊松神经网络（LLPNNs），一种直接从可观测数据（如位形与速度）学习李-泊松系统动力学的保结构框架，利用哈密顿解码器/伪拉格朗日编码器、来自李-泊松对称约化的通用诺特不变量以及基于Magnus方法的李群更新，实现对正则与退化哈密顿系统的长期预测，并在刚体、水下航行器与最优控制等实例上展示了优异的长时预测精度和噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多力学与控制系统（如刚体、水下航行器、流体、等离子体、最优控制）可经对称约化得到李-泊松系统。但学习这类系统面临根本挑战：其动力学演化在动量变量中进行，而动量通常不可观测；最优控制情形下，潜在变量还包含不可观测的协态，且哈密顿量可能退化从而不存在对应的拉格朗日量，使编解码器方法失效。因此亟需一种能从可观测数据直接学习李-泊松动力学且保持几何结构的方法。

Method: LLPNN框架包含三个几何要素：（1）在活跃变量上学习哈密顿解码器或伪拉格朗日编码器；（2）利用由李-泊松对称约化产生的通用诺特不变量构造潜在轨迹；（3）通过李-泊松流结合基于Magnus展开的李群更新重建可观测与潜在动力学。该方法保持几何结构，适用于正则和退化哈密顿系统。

Result: 在三个数值实例上验证：SO(3)上的广义刚体、SE(3)上的Kirchhoff水下航行器、以及SE(2)^N上的交互车辆最优控制问题。结果表明LLPNN具有优异的长时预测精度、对噪声的强鲁棒性，且仅需适度数据集和轻量级神经网络架构即可实现有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: LLPNN成功解决了从可观测数据学习李-泊松系统的保结构建模难题，统一处理正则与退化哈密顿情形，在预测精度、鲁棒性和数据效率方面表现卓越，为物理系统长期预测和最优控制中的潜在动力学学习提供了有效新方法。

Abstract: Structure-preserving neural networks are essential for the long-term prediction of Hamiltonian systems from data. Many important Hamiltonian systems in mechanics and control admit symmetry reduction to Lie--Poisson systems, including rigid bodies, underwater vehicles, fluids, plasmas, and optimal control problems. A fundamental challenge in learning such systems is that their dynamics evolve in momentum variables that are typically unobservable, while available data consist only of observable quantities such as configurations and velocities. In optimal control applications, the situation is further complicated because the latent variables contain unobservable co-states and the Hamiltonian may be degenerate, preventing the existence of a corresponding Lagrangian and rendering the encoder-decoder approaches inapplicable.
  We introduce Latent Lie--Poisson Neural Networks (LLPNNs), a structure-preserving framework for learning Lie--Poisson dynamics directly from observable data. The proposed approach exploits three geometric ingredients: (i) learning either a Hamiltonian decoder or a pseudo-Lagrangian encoder on the active variables, (ii) constructing latent trajectories through a universal Noether invariant arising from Lie--Poisson symmetry reduction, and (iii) reconstructing observable and latent dynamics through Lie--Poisson flows combined with Magnus-based Lie-group updates. The resulting method preserves the geometric structure and is applicable to both regular and degenerate Hamiltonian systems.
  We demonstrate the method on three examples: a generalized rigid body on SO(3), Kirchhoff's underwater vehicle on SE(3), and an optimal-control problem for interacting vehicles on $SE(2)^N$. Numerical experiments show excellent long-term predictive accuracy, strong robustness to noise, and competitive performance using only modest datasets and lightweight neural-network architectures.

</details>


### [226] [FairDiffuseVQVAE: Sampling-Time Fairness in Tabular Diffusion via Conditional Refinement of Vector-Quantized Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28945)
*Nitish Nagesh,Mahdi Bagheri,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairDiffuseVQVAE 提出一种两阶段架构，将表格数据的分布保真度与公平性解耦：第一阶段使用带行级判别器的 VQ-VAE 学习忠实表示，第二阶段通过条件扩散模型在推理时对敏感属性均匀采样，从采样分布层面实现人口均等，无需训练时引入公平性惩罚项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格扩散模型（如 TabDDPM、TabSyn）保真度高但无公平性机制；而公平感知生成器（DECAF、FairTGAN、FairTabDDPM）在训练时施加显式公平惩罚，公平性提升有限且严重牺牲样本质量或下游效用。核心动机是将保真度与公平性解耦，避免二者在损失函数中的直接冲突。

Method: 提出 FairDiffuseVQVAE 两阶段架构：(1) 第一阶段：向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE）配合行级判别器进行训练，不含任何公平性损失项，仅追求对原始数据的高保真重建；(2) 第二阶段：采用 DiffuseVAE 风格的连续扩散精炼器，以第一阶段重建结果和敏感属性为条件，通过无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）进行条件生成。公平性通过推理时对敏感属性的均匀采样自然实现人口均等（demographic parity），而非来自训练时的竞争性损失项。

Result: 在 Adult、Bank 和 COMPAS 三个数据集上，FairDiffuseVQVAE 取得了最高的平均人口均等比（DPR）0.702（较 FairTabDDPM 提升 47%）和均等几率比（EOR）0.686（提升 100%）。同时取得所有已发表方法中最低的平均成对相关性误差（0.034），为这些公平性增益明确付出了约 15 个 AUC 点的代价。

Conclusion: FairDiffuseVQVAE 证明了通过采样分布设计而非损失函数惩罚来实现表格数据生成公平性的可行性，在公平性指标上大幅超越现有方法，且保持了领先的分布保真度，为隐私保护数据共享和去偏数据增强提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data is increasingly used in privacy-preserving data sharing, data augmentation, and to mitigate downstream classifier bias. State-of-the-art tabular diffusion models such as TabDDPM and TabSyn achieve excellent distributional fidelity but offer no mechanism for fairness; conversely, fairness-aware tabular generators (DECAF, FairTGAN, FairTabDDPM) impose explicit fairness penalties at training time, yielding modest fairness gains at substantial cost to either sample quality or downstream utility. We introduce FairDiffuseVQVAE, a two-stage architecture that decouples fidelity from fairness: a vector-quantized autoencoder with a row-level discriminator (Stage~1, no fairness terms) is followed by a DiffuseVAE-style continuous diffusion refiner that conditions on both the Stage-1 reconstruction and the protected attribute via classifier-free guidance (Stage~2). Fairness emerges as a property of the sampling distribution -- uniform sampling of the protected attribute at inference time enforces demographic parity by construction, rather than from competing loss terms. On the Adult, Bank and COMPAS datasets, FairDiffuseVQVAE achieves the highest mean Demographic Parity Ratio ($0.702$, $+47\%$ over FairTabDDPM) and Equalized Odds Ratio ($0.686$, $+100\%$). It also attains the lowest mean pair-wise correlation error ($0.034$) of any published method, while explicitly trading $\sim$$15$ AUC points for these fairness gains.

</details>


### [227] [Shapley-Value-Based Feature Attribution for Data Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28946)
*Xinxue,Qu,Francis Bilson Darku,Hong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Shapley值的特征归因框架，用于在特征层面而非数据集层面解决数据隐私中的披露风险与数据效用权衡问题，该方法对数据脱敏方法、统计/机器学习方法及评估指标均具有不可知性，实验证明其能有效降低披露风险同时保持数据效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人数据的广泛使用虽带来诸多好处，但也引发了严重的隐私担忧。现有研究主要在数据集层面关注风险-效用权衡，缺乏在特征层面的精细化分析，因此需要一种新的框架来从特征层面公平地评估和平衡披露风险与数据效用。

Method: 提出一个基于Shapley值的公平特征归因框架，将Shapley值特征归因方法适配到数据隐私领域，通过捕捉"披露风险"和"数据效用"两个关键维度，在特征层面实现数据脱敏的权衡。该框架对数据脱敏方法、统计与机器学习方法、以及数据效用和披露风险评估指标均具有不可知性（即通用性）。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法能够在有效降低披露风险的同时，较好地保持数据效用。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据隐私保护提供了一种特征层面的、基于Shapley值的公平特征归因新范式，具备方法不可知性和指标不可知性的优势，在风险-效用权衡上取得了良好效果。

Abstract: Despite its many benefits, widespread access to individuals' personal data also causes severe privacy concerns for consumers, companies, and policymakers. This study proposes a novel framework that adapts the Shapley-value-based feature attribution approach to the problem domain of data privacy by capturing the two crucial dimensions of data privacy---disclosure risk and data utility. Our proposed framework takes a holistic view of data masking through a fair feature attribution approach based on Shapley values. Different from the existing literature that mostly focuses on the risk-utility tradeoff at the dataset level, the proposed framework addresses the tradeoff at the feature level. Furthermore, the proposed framework is agnostic to data masking methods, statistical and machine learning methods, and data utility and disclosure risk evaluation metrics. Experimental results show that our proposed method can effectively reduce disclosure risk while preserving data utility.

</details>


### [228] [Overcoming the Weakest-Link Effect in LLM-Driven Program Optimization via Heterogeneous Edit Recombination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28947)
*Jingwen Fu,Zhen Liu,Yuhan Liu,He Zhang,Nanning Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERO是一种基于LLM的零阶程序优化器，通过让LLM生成多样、不重叠的原子编辑并系统性地组合筛选，克服了传统"文本梯度"方法在复杂程序-评分映射下不可靠的问题，在多个领域实现了更优性能和更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM程序优化方法依赖"文本梯度"（一阶更新方向）来指导搜索，但当程序-评分映射变得复杂时，这类梯度估计越来越不可靠。作者认为显式梯度并非程序优化的必要条件，LLM的先验知识足以直接提出原子编辑。然而，零阶搜索面临"最弱链接效应"——批量编辑统一接受/拒绝时，单个有害编辑会破坏整体改进。

Method: 提出HERO（程序优化器）：(1) 提示LLM从当前程序出发生成多样、互不重叠的原子编辑；(2) 利用评估器分数系统性地筛选和组合这些原子编辑，形成连贯的程序改进。这是一种零阶优化策略，避免了显式梯度估计。

Result: 在算法问题、策略游戏、基于LLM的智能体系统设计以及机器人路径规划四个领域进行评估，HERO一致地发现更高分的程序，收敛速度显著快于现有LLM优化器，且消耗更少的token。

Conclusion: 显式梯度并非程序优化所必需；通过生成原子编辑并智能组合的零阶策略（HERO），可以有效规避"最弱链接效应"，在多个复杂领域中实现更高效、更可靠的程序优化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to solve complex problems by searching over program space, offering a general paradigm for scientific problems that can be naturally represented and solved as programs. Despite recent progress, identifying effective optimization directions for a candidate program remains challenging. By analogy with automatic differentiation, existing methods typically guide the search using a textual ``gradient'': a first-order update direction expressed as textual edits. Such gradients are inferred either from previously evaluated programs or from LLM-generated feedback on the implicit program-score mapping. However, these estimates become increasingly unreliable as the program--score mapping grows more complex, limiting their practical utility. We argue that explicit gradients are not essential for effective program optimization. Leveraging their prior knowledge, LLMs can propose plausible atomic edits directly from the current program, thereby enabling a zeroth-order optimization strategy. However, zeroth-order search suffers from a \textit{weakest-link effect}: when a bundle of edits is accepted or rejected as a whole, a single harmful edit can negate the benefits of all remaining edits. To address this issue, we introduce HERO, a program optimizer that prompts an LLM to generate diverse, non-overlapping atomic edits and then systematically selects and composes them into coherent program improvements using evaluator scores. We evaluate HERO across algorithmic problems, strategy games, the design of LLM-based agentic systems, and robotic path planning. Across these domains, HERO consistently discovers higher-scoring programs and converges substantially faster than prior LLM-based optimizers, while consuming fewer tokens.

</details>


### [229] [Mining Verdict Boundaries for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28954)
*Jiawei Ren,Guanqin Zhang,Zhenya Zhang,Yulei Sui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分支定界(BaB)神经网络验证中沿树路径逐个子问题顺序求解效率低的问题，本文利用路径单调性，提出通过同时分裂多个激活函数来高效定位判定边界的方法，包括指数搜索策略及其基于定量信息的增强版本，在常用基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BaB方法在搜索判定边界时，沿树路径按深度递增顺序求解每个子问题，每个访问节点都需进行昂贵的边界传播，效率低下。作者观察到路径上存在单调性，可利用这一性质同时处理多个ReLU分裂，从而跳过与边界无关的子问题。

Method: 提出两种搜索方法：(1) 基础方法：利用路径单调性进行指数搜索(Exponential Search)，同时分裂多个激活函数，跳过大量边界无关子问题；(2) 增强版本：利用子问题求解中获得的定量信息来估计判定边界位置，进一步优化搜索效率。两者均改变了传统逐节点处理的范式。

Result: 在常用基准数据集上进行了实验评估，并与近期的BaB方法进行了对比（具体数值结果需查阅原文）。

Conclusion: 利用路径单调性进行多激活函数同时分裂的策略，能够高效且精确地定位判定边界，相比传统逐个子问题求解的BaB方法显著提升了搜索效率。

Abstract: Branch and Bound (BaB) aims to achieve complete verification of neural networks by adaptively partitioning the problem and applying off-the-shelf verifiers to subproblems. Its problem-splitting history can be represented as a tree, where each subproblem corresponds to a child node. A key problem of BaB lies in searching for the verdict boundaries across all the paths that divide the verified and unverified subproblems. We observe that the existing BaB approach tackles this problem by solving each expensive subproblem sequentially along the tree path as its depth increases, requiring costly bounds propagation at every visited BaB tree node (i.e., subproblem), which is inefficient. To address this issue, we propose effective search approaches that leverage the monotonicity of each path to efficiently and precisely locate the verdict boundary by simultaneously splitting multiple activation functions (e.g., ReLU), rather than processing them one at a time as in the classical approach. Our approach performs an effective exponential search along each path, allowing us to skip many boundary-unrelated subproblems when identifying the verdict boundary. The enhanced version further improves this process by estimating the boundary's position using quantitative information obtained from subproblem solving. We perform experimental evaluation on commonly-used benchmarks to assess our proposed techniques, and compare them with recent BaB-based approaches.

</details>


### [230] [Efficient LLM Adversarial Training via Low-Rank Defense and Circuit-Guided Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28959)
*Weiyi He,Yuping Lin,Jiliang Tang,Yue Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从防御侧（ReFT token匹配优化）和攻击侧（基于电路提取的轻量代理模型）两个维度系统地提出了加速潜在对抗训练（LAT）的计算高效策略，平均减少48.1%的每步FLOPs，且仅需0.0118%可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练是抵御对抗攻击最有效的方法之一，但在大语言模型（LLMs）规模下计算成本过高。现有加速策略（如LAT）仍存在较高计算开销，亟需从防御和攻击两侧同时挖掘更高效的加速方案。

Method: （1）防御侧：在LAT框架中引入表征微调（ReFT），并揭示若ReFT施加的token与攻击token不匹配将导致性能下降的问题。（2）攻击侧：在每轮LAT迭代生成对抗攻击时，仅从LLM中提取相关电路（relevant circuits）构建轻量级代理模型，避免攻击生成阶段通过完整模型进行前向-反向传播。两方面的策略均提供了理论证明和数值实验验证。

Result: 相比于标准全量微调的LAT，所提方法平均将每步对抗训练的FLOPs降低48.1%，且仅需0.0118%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: 通过防御侧与攻击侧的联合优化，可以在大幅降低计算开销的同时保持对抗训练的有效性，为LLMs的高效对抗训练提供了新方向。

Abstract: Adversarial training is one of the most effective defenses against adversarial attacks, yet the computational cost remains prohibitive at modern scales, especially for large language models (LLMs). While existing mitigation strategies, e.g., latent adversarial training (LAT), have been developed, they still incur a high computational cost. In this work, we comprehensively investigate computation-efficient strategies to speed up LAT from two complementary perspectives: (1) Defense-side optimization: We explore the representation fine-tuning (ReFT) within LAT, and reveal a potential issue if there is a mismatch on which tokens to apply ReFT and the attack. (2) Attack-side optimization: When computing adversarial attacks in each LAT iteration, we extract only the relevant circuits from the LLM to construct a lightweight surrogate model, avoiding the computation in the forward-backward passes through the full model during the attack generation. For both perspectives, we provide theoretical justifications and numerical evidence to illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed strategies. Ultimately, compared to standard LAT with full fine-tuning, our method on average reduces per-step adversarial-training FLOPs by 48.1% while requiring only 0.0118% trainable parameters.

</details>


### [231] [Beyond Feature and Structure Alignment: Learning Transferable Propagation Knowledge for Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28980)
*Yi Wang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProGFM提出将边与特征维度之间的传播关系作为可迁移知识单元，通过传播关系原型库学习跨域可迁移的传播模式，实现未见图域中的自适应信息聚合，显著提升了图基础模型的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型主要存在两个局限：(1) 侧重通过特征对齐和结构对齐来缓解域差异，忽视了图数据中可迁移知识单元的挖掘；(2) 消息传递中采用固定的传播机制，忽略了不同边在不同特征维度上可能呈现异质性传播模式。因此需要探索更本质的可迁移知识单元和自适应传播机制。

Method: 提出Propagation-aware Graph Foundation Model (ProGFM)，核心创新在于将边与特征维度之间的传播关系视为可迁移知识单元，并构建传播关系原型库（propagation relationship prototype bank）来学习跨域可迁移的传播知识，从而在未见目标域中实现自适应信息聚合。

Result: 在多种跨域迁移场景下进行大量实验，结果表明ProGFM具备强大的跨域知识迁移能力，并在泛化性能上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ProGFM通过挖掘传播关系作为可迁移知识单元，有效解决了图基础模型中异构传播模式的建模问题，为图基础模型的跨域泛化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling knowledge transfer across diverse domains. Unlike traditional graph learning methods that are typically designed for in-domain settings, GFMs aim to learn transferable knowledge that can generalize to unseen graph domains. However, unlike language or visual data, graphs lack intrinsic and unified representation units, such as tokens in language and patches in vision, making it challenging to identify transferable knowledge units for building graph foundation models. Existing graph foundation models mainly focus on mitigating domain discrepancies through feature alignment and structure alignment, while overlooking the exploration of transferable knowledge units underlying graph data. Moreover, these methods generally rely on fixed propagation mechanisms during message passing, overlooking the heterogeneity in propagation patterns, as different edges may exhibit distinct propagation patterns for different feature dimensions. To address these limitations, we propose a Propagation-aware Graph Foundation Model (ProGFM), which regards the propagation relationships between edges and feature dimensions as transferable knowledge units. Through a propagation relationship prototype bank, ProGFM learns cross-domain transferable propagation knowledge, enabling adaptive information aggregation in unseen graph domains. Extensive experiments across various cross-domain transfer scenarios demonstrate that ProGFM possesses strong cross-domain knowledge transfer capability and exhibits superior generalization performance compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [232] [SILVA Networks as Structured Implicit Layers and Vector Attractors via Dynamic Interaction Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28989)
*Jose Luis Lima de Jesus Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SILVA Networks 提出了一种结构化隐式层架构，将刺激输入、局部交互、全局交互、阻尼和读出机制在单一不动点框架中解耦，实现了可训练、可消融、可诊断的内部交互动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式神经层将所有影响（直接输入、邻近结构、全局上下文）压缩到单一不动点更新中，导致难以区分不同信息来源的贡献，缺乏可解释性和可诊断性。

Method: 提出 SILVA（Structured Implicit Layers and Vector Attractors via Dynamic Interaction Fields），将不动点架构显式分解为五个独立组件：刺激（stimulus）、局部交互（local interaction）、全局交互（global interaction）、阻尼（damping）和读出（readout）。该模板通过定义节点、邻域和全局摘要的领域特定方式，实例化到图像、分子、引文网络和长程图基准等多种任务。

Result: 实验和消融研究表明：局部交互在图任务中承担主要负载；在测试容量下 MNIST 几乎未从循环结构中获益；全局交互在长程节点分类基准中表现出最显著的增益。各组件的作用呈现任务依赖性。

Conclusion: SILVA 提供了一种内部交互动力学可训练、可消融、可可视化和可诊断的隐式表征框架，为理解和设计隐式神经层提供了结构化视角。

Abstract: Many learning problems require representations that reconcile direct input, nearby structure, and broader context. In implicit neural layers, these influences are usually absorbed into a single fixed-point update, making it hard to identify what enters from the stimulus, what propagates locally, what comes from global context, and what is produced by solver dynamics. Here we introduce SILVA Networks, Structured Implicit Layers and Vector Attractors via Dynamic Interaction Fields. SILVA separates stimulus, local interaction, global interaction, damping, and readout inside one fixed-point architecture. The same template is instantiated for images, molecules, citation networks, and long-range graph benchmarks through domain-specific definitions of nodes, neighborhoods, and global summaries. Experiments and ablations show task-dependent roles for these terms: local interactions are load-bearing in the graph tasks, MNIST gains little from recurrence at the tested capacity, and the clearest global benefit appears in a long-range node-classification benchmark. SILVA therefore provides an implicit representation whose internal interaction dynamics can be trained, ablated, visualized, and diagnosed.

</details>


### [233] [Dynamics-aware identification of governing equations from sparse and noisy data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29036)
*Pongpisit Thanasutives,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于Koopman算子的上采样技术（DMD、EDMD、优化DMD）在稀疏含噪数据下用于提升SINDy/PDE-FIND导数估计质量的效果，发现多项式EDMD在ODE场景表现最优，PDE结果则因系统而异，整体上这类动力学感知预处理方法优于传统插值方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SINDy和PDE-FIND等稀疏辨识方法依赖导数估计，但稀疏、含噪的时间测量会使导数估计不可靠。需要一种动力学感知的预处理手段来插值和降噪，以改善导数估计质量。

Method: 采用基于Koopman算子的上采样技术（DMD、扩展DMD EDMD、优化DMD），在选定观测量上学习Koopman演化的有限维逼近，用于对观测时间窗口内的快照进行插值和降噪，然后再进行导数估计和稀疏回归。同时与线性插值和光滑样条插值等非动力学方法进行对比，并在ODE（Lorenz-63、Van der Pol）和PDE（Burgers、Fisher-KPP、线性对流扩散）系统上进行了稀疏含噪采样的实证基准测试。

Result: 多项式EDMD在ODE场景下表现最强，尤其在系数精度方面；PDE结果因系统而异：低秩DMD辅助重建改善了Burgers和对流扩散方程的发现，而Fisher-KPP数据上原始基线（无上采样）仍具竞争力；Koopman预处理方法整体优于线性和样条插值；DMD辅助上采样可稳定基于Pareto的非oracle支持度选择。

Conclusion: Koopman上采样应被视为一种动力学感知的预处理步骤，当其可观测表示和低秩结构与数据特性匹配时，可有效降低导数估计误差，从而提升稀疏动力学辨识的性能。

Abstract: Sparse identification of nonlinear dynamics (SINDy) and PDE functional identification (PDE-FIND) recover parsimonious ordinary and partial differential equations (ODEs and PDEs) from data. However, sparse and noisy temporal measurements can make derivative estimates unreliable. To address this problem, we evaluate Koopman-based upsampling techniques implemented with dynamic mode decomposition (DMD), extended DMD (EDMD), and optimized DMD. These methods learn finite-dimensional approximations of Koopman evolution on selected observables and are used to interpolate and denoise snapshots inside the observed time window before derivative estimation and sparse regression. The empirical benchmark comprises two ODE systems, Lorenz-63 and Van der Pol, and three periodic PDE systems, Burgers, Fisher-Kolmogorov-Petrovskii-Piskunov (Fisher-KPP), and linear advection-diffusion, over sparse and noisy sampling regimes. Polynomial EDMD gives the strongest ODE results, especially in coefficient accuracy. The PDE results are system-dependent: low-rank DMD-assisted reconstructions improve Burgers and advection-diffusion discovery, while the raw baseline (without upsampling) remains competitive for the Fisher-KPP data. A comparison against linear and smoothing-spline interpolation techniques shows that the selected Koopman-based preprocessors provide overall performance gains over these non-dynamical alternatives. We also demonstrate that DMD-assisted upsampling can stabilize Pareto-based non-oracle support-size selection. Overall, Koopman-based upsampling is best viewed as a dynamics-aware preprocessing step that can reduce derivative-estimation error when its observable representation and low-rank structure are appropriate for the data.

</details>


### [234] [DFSC: Error-Controlled Differentiable Mittag-Leffler Propagation for Fractional Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29038)
*Ning Hu,Haitao Duan,Shuqun Li,Chuyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DFSC 是一个基于 PyTorch 的分数阶科学机器学习框架，核心是 Mittag-Leffler 谱层（MLSL），它将已知的分数阶传播与数据驱动修正分离，并通过自适应算法保证可微评估的精度，支持多种算子路径和 GPU 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶科学机器学习需要可微分、可批处理、可加速且能与神经网络组合的数值算子。当主导的线性分数阶演化可通过 Mittag-Leffler 传播子已知时，反复用历史求解器重建或从数据中重新学习该响应是低效的。因此需要一种能将已知传播与未知残差分离的计算框架。

Method: 提出了 DFSC 框架，核心为 Mittag-Leffler 谱层（MLSL）。该层将已知分数阶传播与数据驱动修正分离，使神经网络仅学习未解析的动力学，同时联合优化分数阶阶数和残差网络参数。自适应算法通过递增特殊函数截断深度或 Lanczos 维度，直到连续可微评估满足指定容差。在负实交替级数区域返回首项截断误差的认证界，其余区域标注为经验估计。支持稠密、稀疏、无矩阵、自伴、广义及受控复数算子路径，以及可训练分数阶阶数、直接反问题和残差神经组合。

Result: 认证级数界覆盖全部 59 个合格参考案例，界/误差效能中位数为 1.246。复用已准备的批处理 Lanczos 基可在 CPU 上减少重复查询时间 4.61–7.11 倍，在 RTX 5070 上减少 13.07–16.22 倍（不含一次性准备时间）。27 案例逆矩阵在全局范围内发现满秩局部曲率，同时保持显式模型条件性。外部求解器和混合真实数据结果支持 DFSC 作为匹配分数阶结构的误差感知可选原语，而非通用替代品。

Conclusion: DFSC 为具有已知 Mittag-Leffler 传播子的分数阶系统提供了一种高效、误差可控的计算原语，通过将确定性传播与可学习的残差分离，在精度保证和计算效率之间取得了平衡，适合作为分数阶科学机器学习的专用组件。

Abstract: Fractional scientific machine learning requires numerical operators that can be differentiated, batched, accelerated, and composed with neural networks. When the dominant linear fractional evolution is known through a Mittag-Leffler propagator, repeatedly reconstructing that response with a history solver or relearning it from data is unnecessary. We present DFSC, a PyTorch environment organized around the Mittag-Leffler Spectral Layer (MLSL). The layer separates known fractional propagation from data-driven corrections, so neural modules learn only unresolved dynamics while fractional orders and residual-network parameters are optimized jointly. Its adaptive algorithm increases special-function truncation depth or Lanczos dimension until successive differentiable evaluations satisfy a requested tolerance. In the negative-real alternating-series regime, DFSC additionally returns a certified first-omitted-term bound; outside that regime it explicitly labels estimates as empirical.
  DFSC supports dense, sparse, matrix-free, self-adjoint, generalized, and controlled complex operator paths; trainable fractional orders; direct inverse problems; residual neural composition; and CPU/GPU execution. The certified series bound covers all 59 eligible reference cases, with median bound/error effectivity 1.246 for resolved errors. Reusing a prepared batched Lanczos basis gives identical fixed-path values and reduces repeated-query time by 4.61--7.11 times on CPU and 13.07--16.22 times on an RTX 5070, excluding one-time preparation. A 27-case inverse matrix finds full-rank local curvature throughout, while remaining explicitly model-conditional. External solver and mixed real-data results support DFSC as an error-aware optional primitive for matched fractional structure, rather than a general replacement for fractional solvers or neural models.

</details>


### [235] [Learning Lookahead Lemmas for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29051)
*Liam Davis,Haoze Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于前瞻（lookahead）过程的神经网络验证"内处理"（inprocessing）框架，通过推导不稳定ReLU相位的新引理并构建蕴含图，用于剪枝搜索空间和活化布尔割，在Marabou和α-β-CROWN两个验证器上最多提升了34%的不可满足性证明实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的神经网络验证器以分支定界为核心求解机制，但搜索空间巨大严重制约了验证效率。作者希望在不改变验证器主体架构的前提下，通过内处理方式在求解过程中动态生成额外约束来压缩搜索空间，从而提升验证性能。

Method: 论文提出了一种由前瞻过程驱动的内处理框架。前瞻（lookahead）通过试探性地假设某些不稳定ReLU的相位，分析这些假设之间的逻辑依赖关系，推导出新的引理（lemmas）。这些引理被收集到一个蕴含图（implication graph）中，该图随后用于两方面：1）剪枝搜索空间，提前剔除不可能的分支；2）活化（vivify）布尔割，对已有约束进行简化和强化。该方法以即插即用的方式集成到Marabou和α-β-CROWN两个主流验证器中。

Result: 在两个最先进的验证器Marabou和α-β-CROWN上进行了实例化验证，实验表明该框架在两个验证器中均带来了性能提升，能够证明多达34%以上原本无法判定的实例为不可满足（unsatisfiable）。

Conclusion: 基于前瞻的内处理框架能够有效提升神经网络验证器的求解能力，通过在搜索过程中动态生成并利用蕴含图来压缩搜索空间，是一种通用且有效的方法，可无缝集成到现有的分支定界验证器中。

Abstract: State-of-the-art neural network verifiers use the branch-and-bound procedure as their core solving mechanism. We introduce an inprocessing framework for neural network verification driven by the lookahead procedure. Under this framework, lookahead derives new lemmas over the phases of unstable ReLUs, which are collected into an implication graph that is used to prune the search space and vivify boolean cuts. We instantiate the framework in two state-of-the-art verifiers, Marabou and $α$-$β$-CROWN, and demonstrate that it improves performance in both, proving up to 34% more instances unsatisfiable.

</details>


### [236] [Who Wins Where? Conformal Model Comparison for Local Superiority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29053)
*Yi Zhou,Baishi Li,Xuan Yao,Ke-Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"保形局部模型比较"框架，将传统的全局模型比较扩展到局部区域，使用三分样本方法构建校准的局部最佳模型地图，在控制错误声明率的同时识别不同协变量区域中哪个模型更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型比较方法只给出全局单一胜者，掩盖了模型性能在协变量空间中的异质性——某些区域模型A更好，另一些区域模型B更好。需要一种统计上严谨的局部模型比较方法。

Method: 采用三分样本框架：(1) 用第一个split拟合竞争模型；(2) 用第二个split的样本外得分估计局部中心和尺度；(3) 用第三个split进行保形校准残差不确定性。在目标点，仅当单侧保形边界排除平局（即得分显著不为零）时声明局部胜者，得分的符号决定胜出模型。该方法基于局部化均值得分估计量，并引入平方损失偏差-方差分解分析模型结构对局部胜负的影响。

Result: 理论上：证明了未来比较分数单侧错误声明的有限样本边际控制；建立了局部均值得分估计量在远离平局边界处的逐点一致性；揭示了全局比较与局部优势流行度可能严重不一致。实验上：合成和真实数据实验表明该方法能恢复异质性胜者区域，在不确定性下保持克制（abstain），并比全局选择获得更高的条件收益。

Conclusion: 保形局部模型比较提供了一种统计有效的框架，能够在控制错误声明的前提下识别模型性能的空间异质性，避免全局比较的误导性结论，在需要局部模型选择的应用场景中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Standard model comparison is global, aggregating losses across the covariate space to declare a single winner. This can obscure heterogeneous performance, where different models are preferable in different regions. We introduce conformalized local model comparison, a split-sample framework for constructing calibrated local best-model maps. Given a model comparison score, such as the difference between two squared losses, the method uses three disjoint splits to fit competing models, estimate local centers and scales from out-of-sample scores, and conformally calibrate residual uncertainty. At a target point, the procedure declares a local winner only when a one-sided conformal bound excludes a tie, with the score's sign determining the favored model. We prove finite-sample marginal control for one-sided erroneous declarations on the realized future comparison score, establish pointwise consistency of the localized mean-score estimator away from tie boundaries, show that aggregate comparison can disagree sharply with the prevalence of local superiority, and derive a squared-loss bias--variance decomposition that clarifies how model structure affects local wins. Synthetic and real-data experiments show that the method recovers heterogeneous winner regions, abstains under uncertainty, and yields higher conditional gain than global selection.

</details>


### [237] [Autonomous Repair for Multi-Agent Systems via Monte-Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29055)
*Hanxiao Lu,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARS是一种基于搜索的框架，将多智能体系统修复建模为蒙特卡洛树搜索过程，通过诊断引导的扩展和分类法增强的评估来导航修复空间，并引入StateMAS基准进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在输出错误时，用户需手动定位错误（故障归因）并提供反馈来修复，而现有的自动修复机制研究严重不足，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 提出MARS框架：将MAS修复形式化为MCTS过程；采用诊断引导的扩展（diagnosis-guided expansion）和分类法增强的评估（taxonomy-augmented evaluation）来高效探索修复空间；使用部分展开（partial rollout）而非完整展开来评估智能体轨迹，以降低token消耗。同时构建StateMAS基准，包含1310条可回放的多智能体故障轨迹，涵盖四种智能体架构和四种LLM骨干。

Result: 在StateMAS基准上，MARS在所有设置下均一致优于现有最优方法，实现了3.0%到12.1%的绝对性能提升，同时保持了可比的token消耗成本。消融实验证实分类法增强评估和诊断引导扩展对性能增益至关重要。

Conclusion: MARS框架有效填补了多智能体系统自动修复的研究空白，通过MCTS与诊断引导、分类法增强的结合，在显著提升修复性能的同时控制token成本，为MAS故障恢复提供了可行的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly deployed to solve complex tasks. In case of incorrect or unsatisfactory outputs, users have to manually locate agent mistakes by inspecting agent trajectories (i.e., {\em failure attribution}) and provide feedback to refine the outputs (i.e., {\em repair}). Despite some recent work in MAS failure attribution, automated mechanisms to recover from such mistakes remain largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose MARS, a search-based framework that formulates MAS repair as a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) process and navigates the vast space of potential repairs via diagnosis-guided expansion with taxonomy-augmented evaluation. Unlike standard MCTS, which evaluates a complete simulation via full rollout, MARS evaluates the agent trajectory using partial rollout to reduce token consumption. Furthermore, we introduce StateMAS, a large-scale MAS repair benchmark with 1,310 replayable multi-agent failure trajectories spanning four types of agent architectures and four LLM backbones. Experiments on StateMAS demonstrate that MARS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving an absolute improvement from 3.0\% to 12.1\% across all settings, while maintaining a comparable token consumption cost. The ablation study further confirms that taxonomy-augmented evaluation and diagnosis-guided expansion are critical to achieving these performance gains.

</details>


### [238] [Benchmarking Frontier Large Language Models Against Official Crash Database Coding Using Police Crash Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29064)
*Sudhir Bharati,Rajendra K C Khatri,Sudip Bharati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对标六种前沿大语言模型（LLM），测试其从警方事故叙事文本中自动编码事故属性的能力，并与阿肯色州官方结构化数据库进行比对。结果显示，GPT-5.5 High 综合表现最优，但简单基线（多数类、关键词规则）在原始一致率和F1等指标上与之相当甚至更优；模型间差异小于属性间差异，提示部署须按属性评估并辅以人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 警方事故叙事中包含丰富的补充信息，但人工审阅成本高昂，目前尚不清楚大语言模型在多大程度上能复现官方事故编码，因此需要系统性地基准测试LLM的编码表现。

Method: 链接阿肯色州2015–2025年5,587份致命事故叙事与5,889条结构化记录，获得4,194宗匹配事故。六种LLM使用相同的零样本提示对事故方式、非机动车参与者关系、交叉口类型、施工区关系、路面状况和光照条件六个属性进行编码。评估指标包括一致率、宏平均F1、Cohen's kappa、覆盖率、选择性一致率，并与多数类基线、Unknown基线和关键词规则基线对比；采用重复测量分析和广义估计方程模型比较模型与属性间的差异。

Result: GPT-5.5 High 一致率最高，但多数类基线的原始一致率更高，关键词规则基线的宏平均F1和Cohen's kappa与最佳LLM相当。非机动车关系和事故方式的一致率最高，光照条件、路面状况和施工区关系最低。事故属性间的差异大于模型间的差异。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于LLM的事故编码提供了基准，表明部署时应按具体属性、结合透明基线和人工审查进行评估，而非依赖单一模型的整体表现。

Abstract: Police crash narratives contain information that may supplement structured crash databases, but manual review is labor-intensive and it remains unclear how well large language models (LLMs) reproduce official crash coding. This study benchmarked six frontier LLMs by comparing narrative-derived crash attribute codes with corresponding fields in the Arkansas fatal-crash database. The analysis linked 5,587 fatal-crash narratives with 5,889 structured crash records from Arkansas (2015-2025), yielding 4,194 matched crashes. Six LLMs were evaluated using an identical zero-shot prompt to code crash manner, non-motorist relation, intersection type, work-zone relation, roadway surface condition, and light condition. Performance was evaluated using agreement, macro-averaged F1 score, Cohen's kappa, coverage, selective agreement, and comparisons with always-majority, always-Unknown, and keyword-rule baselines. Repeated-measures analyses and a generalized estimating equations model assessed differences among models and attributes. GPT-5.5 High achieved the highest agreement among the evaluated LLMs, but the always-majority baseline produced higher raw agreement and the keyword-rule baseline achieved macro-averaged F1 score and Cohen's kappa comparable to the best-performing LLM. Agreement was highest for non-motorist relation and crash manner and lowest for light condition, roadway surface condition, and work-zone relation. Differences across crash attributes exceeded differences across models. These results provide a benchmark for evaluating LLM-based crash coding and show that deployment should be evaluated on an attribute-specific basis using transparent baselines and human review.

</details>


### [239] [Federated Foundation Models Fine-Tuning with Heterogeneous Compressed Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29071)
*Shengkun Zhu,Jinshan Zeng,Zhihua Allen-Zhao,Mayi Xu,Quanqing Xu,Wei Ren,Qiang Yang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedSLM 提出一种基于SVD分解的联邦微调框架，通过低秩子空间嵌套、分组内适配器同步、跨组结构对齐融合以及弱到强知识激发，在客户端仅需约50% GPU内存的条件下，实现了异构压缩客户端下的高效联邦基础模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中存在根本性的资源不对称问题：拥有最有价值领域数据的机构无法承载数十亿参数的模型。现有异构联邦方法（参数高效微调、模型剪枝、知识蒸馏）各自牺牲了全模型内存缩减、架构自包含性或表示保真度中的某一关键属性，核心矛盾未解。

Method: FedSLM 采用三阶段设计：(1) 基于SVD分解生成自包含的客户端低秩模型，其低秩子空间构成嵌套流形，结构上兼容聚合；(2) 两阶段协议——先在压缩组内同步轻量适配器，再通过结构对齐跨组融合全秩重建；(3) 弱到强激发步骤，借助辅助置信度损失将聚合知识迁移至全规模服务器，并通过显式的偏差-方差权衡缓解压缩伪影。

Result: 在自然语言和视觉-语言基准上，FedSLM 在IID和非IID数据划分下均优于现有联邦基线，同时客户端模型仅需约全模型50%的GPU内存。

Conclusion: FedSLM 通过参数中心化的异构压缩联邦框架，有效解决了资源不对称带来的核心矛盾，在保持表示保真度的同时大幅降低客户端内存需求，理论和实验均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Federated learning of foundation models faces a fundamental resource-asymmetry challenge: the institutions holding the most valuable domain-specific data cannot host billion-parameter models. Existing heterogeneous federated approaches attempt to bridge this gap through parameter-efficient tuning, model pruning, or knowledge distillation, yet each trades away a critical property, whether full-model memory reduction, architectural self-containedness, or representational fidelity, leaving the core tension unresolved. We propose FedSLM, a parameter-centric framework for federated fine-tuning with heterogeneous compressed clients. FedSLM uses SVD-based decomposition to produce self-contained client models, whose low-rank subspaces form nested manifolds that are structurally compatible for aggregation. It then applies a two-stage protocol that synchronizes lightweight adapters within compression groups and fuses full-rank reconstructions across groups via structural alignment. Finally, a weak-to-strong elicitation step with auxiliary confidence loss transfers the aggregated knowledge to the full-scale server, while an explicit bias--variance trade-off mitigates compression artifacts. We provide theoretical guarantees for adapter-level aggregation, subspace-alignment bounds for cross-group fusion, and a characterization of how the confidence loss mitigates weak-supervision noise. Experiments on natural language and vision--language benchmarks show that FedSLM outperforms existing federated baselines under both IID and non-IID partitions, while client models operate at roughly 50% of the GPU memory required by the full model.

</details>


### [240] [DASH-OPD: Discrepancy-Aware Switching with Hysteresis for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29078)
*Yuchen Xia,Qianguo Sun,Chao Song,Junlong Wu,Yiyan Qi,Yunjian Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DASH-OPD 是一种新的智能体在线蒸馏方法，通过计算学生与教师模型在动作token上的对数概率比，积累多轮漂移/恢复证据，实现带滞回特性的自适应双向执行者切换，在ALFWorld上超越了所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在在线蒸馏(OPD)中，学生模型在自己的rollout上训练以减少曝光偏差，但在多轮智能体场景中，学生早期的错误会导致轨迹偏离教师熟悉的领域（distributional drift）。现有课程学习方法仅按训练进度调节教师支持力度，无法判断何时真正需要教师介入。

Method: 提出DASH-OPD方法：每轮计算学生与教师模型在动作token上的平均对数概率比作为两者间的差异度(discrepancy)。学生轮次上的学生-教师比形成漂移信号(drift signals)，教师轮次上的教师-学生比形成恢复信号(recovery signals)。这些信号经归一化后在多轮上累积为漂移证据和恢复证据。当累积证据超过对应切换阈值时触发执行者切换。多轮累积机制赋予了切换滞回特性(hysteresis)，避免瞬态波动导致的高频切换。

Result: 在ALFWorld基准上，DASH-OPD在训练效率和部署效率方面均优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: DASH-OPD通过差异感知的滞回切换机制有效解决了多轮OPD中的分布漂移与恢复问题，实现了高效且稳定的学生模型训练。论文尚处于work in progress阶段，代码和模型将后续发布。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains student models on their own rollouts to reduce exposure bias. However, in multi-turn agent scenarios, early student errors can lead a trajectory away from the teacher's familiar domain. Existing curriculum learning methods regulate how much teacher support is used according to training progress, but cannot determine when it is needed. In light of this, we propose DASH-OPD, Discrepancy-Aware Switching with Hysteresis for OPD, a new agentic OPD method that can switch executors adaptively and bidirectionally. On each turn, DASH-OPD calculates a mean log-probability ratio between the two executors over action tokens as their discrepancy. Student-to-teacher ratios on student turns form drift signals, while teacher-to-student ratios on teacher turns form recovery signals. These signals are normalized and accumulated over multiple turns into drift and recovery evidence. DASH-OPD switches executors when the evidence exceeds its corresponding switching threshold. This multi turn accumulation makes the switching hysteretic, preventing high-frequency switches caused by transient fluctuations. On ALFWorld, DASH-OPD outperforms all the baselines and demonstrates superior training and deployment efficiency. This paper is a work in progress. Code, training logs, and model checkpoints will be released later.

</details>


### [241] [What Is Missing in Surgical Risk Stratification and Outcome Prediction: A Scoping Review of End-to-End Machine Learning Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29090)
*Yizhi Dong,Yuhe Ke,Hairil Rizal Abdullah,Yucheng Xing,Kevan Kai Bing Teo,Ling Huang,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对190项研究的范围综述，系统梳理了利用电子健康记录数据进行术后风险分层的机器学习全流程，揭示了数据预处理、模型选择、评估标准和可解释性等方面的关键方法学缺陷，为更规范化的围术期ML工具开发提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术后不良事件（死亡率和并发症）是全球重大负担，许多可通过早期识别高风险患者加以预防。尽管EHR数据日益丰富，ML有望建模复杂临床模式，但现有研究设计差异悬殊、方法学实践碎片化，亟需系统梳理和评估当前ML流程中的不足。

Method: 采用范围综述（scoping review）方法，纳入190项基于EHR数据的外科风险分层与结局预测ML研究，系统分析其完整工作流程，涵盖数据预处理（缺失值处理、特征选择、类别不平衡处理）、算法选择、模型评估和可解释性等环节。

Result: （1）多数研究依赖单中心私有数据集，数据模态有限，开源外科数据集稀缺，制约可重复性与泛化能力；（2）关键预处理步骤（缺失数据处理、特征选择、类别不平衡）报告不完整；（3）传统ML模型和简单神经网络占主导，深度学习和多模态方法罕见；（4）基准数据集和标准化评估协议基本缺失，妨碍跨研究比较；（5）仅约三分之一的研究纳入可解释性方法。

Conclusion: 当前术后风险预测ML研究存在显著方法学缺陷，限制了临床稳健工具的构建。需在数据共享、预处理规范化、评估标准统一及可解释性整合等方面着力改进，以推动更严谨、可重复且具临床意义的围术期ML开发。

Abstract: Postoperative adverse events, including mortality and morbidity, remain a major global burden, many of which are preventable through early identification of high-risk patients and targeted perioperative care. Accurate risk stratification is therefore essential. With the growing availability of large-scale electronic health records (EHRs), machine learning (ML) provides a data-driven approach to model complex clinical patterns. However, existing studies vary widely in design, and methodological practices remain fragmented. This scoping review characterizes ML pipelines for surgical risk stratification and outcome prediction using EHR data. We reviewed 190 studies covering the ML workflow, including data preprocessing, algorithm selection, model evaluation, and explainability. Most studies relied on single-center private datasets with limited data modalities, while the scarcity of open-access surgical datasets constrained reproducibility and generalizability. Reporting of key preprocessing steps, including missing data handling, feature selection, and class imbalance, was often incomplete. Conventional ML models and simple neural networks predominated, whereas deep learning and multimodal approaches remained uncommon. Benchmark datasets and standardized evaluation protocols were largely absent, hindering cross-study comparisons. Only about one-third of studies incorporated explainability methods. This review identifies methodological gaps limiting clinically robust postoperative ML tools and provides a structured reference to support more rigorous, reproducible, and clinically meaningful ML development for perioperative care.

</details>


### [242] [PiDDM: Physics-Informed Differentiable Degradation Modeling for Lithium-Ion Battery State-of-Health Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29095)
*Zeping Chen,Ruda Jian,Sachin Sigdel,Guoping Xiong,Jian-Xun Wang,Tengfei Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息可微退化建模框架（PiDDM），将SEI生长和锂库存损失相关的阿伦尼乌斯退化动力学嵌入神经网络训练目标，在55块电池、6种工况的公开数据集上取得了最优预测精度，且在外推阶段避免了基线模型产生的非物理容量再生现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的锂离子电池SOH预测模型存在两大缺陷：一是跨循环工况的泛化能力差，二是在长期外推时会产生物理上不合理的行为（如容量再生）。亟需将退化物理知识融入模型以提升预测精度和物理一致性。

Method: 提出PiDDM框架，核心是将基于阿伦尼乌斯动力学的SEI生长和锂库存损失（LLI）经验退化模型作为物理约束，直接整合到神经网络的训练目标函数中，使模型在多样工况下学习物理一致的容量衰退规律。在包含55块电池、6种充放电协议的公开数据集上进行验证，并与多层感知机（MLP）及基线物理信息神经网络进行对比。

Result: PiDDM在所有评估模型中取得了最低的平均预测误差，相比MLP和基线物理信息神经网络显著降低了均方误差。在外推实验中（前90%循环训练，后10%测试），PiDDM成功捕获了寿命末期的加速退化行为，且未出现基线模型中的非物理容量再生现象。

Conclusion: 将退化物理学融入神经网络训练可以有效提升锂离子电池SOH预测的精度和物理一致性，PiDDM为实际电池健康监测提供了一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of lithium-ion battery state of health (SOH) is essential for reliable energy storage operation. However, purely data-driven models may generalize poorly across cycling protocols and produce physically implausible behavior during long-term extrapolation. We developed a physics-informed differentiable degradation modeling framework (PiDDM) for battery SOH prediction. PiDDM incorporates empirical Arrhenius degradation kinetics associated with solid electrolyte interphase growth and loss of lithium inventory into the training objective, encouraging physically consistent capacity fade under diverse operating conditions. The framework was evaluated using a public dataset of 55 batteries cycled under six operating protocols. PiDDM achieved the lowest average prediction error among the evaluated models and substantially reduced mean squared error relative to a multilayer perceptron and a baseline physics-informed neural network. For extrapolation, the models were trained on the first 90% of each battery's cycle life and evaluated on the unseen final 10%. PiDDM captured accelerated end-of-life degradation while avoiding the nonphysical capacity regeneration produced by the baseline models. These results show that incorporating degradation physics into neural network training improves predictive accuracy and physical consistency, providing a promising approach for practical battery health monitoring.

</details>


### [243] [StraightDP: Geometry-Aware Differential Privacy for Rectified-Flow Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29100)
*Xujun Che,Depeng Xu,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StraightDP 利用整流流的几何特性，在强隐私预算下将少量预算用于释放类别条件矩（噪声端主导），其余预算通过DP-SGD聚焦数据端细粒度结构，显著缓解了文本条件生成模型差分隐私训练中的效用悬崖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私训练文本条件生成模型在强隐私保护下面临严重的效用悬崖（utility cliff）。作者通过整流流的几何结构重新审视该问题：在噪声端，贝叶斯最优速度主要由少数类别条件矩决定；在数据端，样本特异性结构愈加重要。现有方法未有效利用这种异质性。

Method: 提出 StraightDP 框架：（1）将少量隐私预算用于一次性释放白化后的类别条件矩，可蒸馏入权重或在采样时注入；（2）剩余预算通过预声明的 DP-SGD 聚焦数据端训练；（3）对多模态主干的逐 token 流范数施加约束，在不改变预训练损失的前提下提升极端噪声像素空间下的下游精度；（4）将释放的矩可迁移至冻结的 SD3-medium，在采样时注入。

Result: 在 MNIST 上 ε=1 时：仅释放矩即可达 0.76 下游准确率和 FID 237（均匀 DP-SGD 仅 0.21 准确率）；完整管线在公共潜在空间中达 0.81 准确率、FID 56。逐 token 流范数约束使隐私越强时精度提升越显著。在 SD3-medium 上，采样时注入矩以极小预算超越 DP-LoRA 训练。

Conclusion: StraightDP 通过利用整流流在噪声端与数据端的几何异质性，将隐私预算分配与生成过程的不同阶段对齐，有效突破了强隐私下生成模型的效用瓶颈，且矩的迁移性使其可广泛适用于不同架构。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) training of text-conditioned generative models suffers a utility cliff at strong privacy. We revisit this problem through the geometry of rectified flows: along the straight interpolation between noise and data, the Bayes-optimal velocity is governed to leading order at the noise end by a few class-conditional moments, and increasingly sample-specific structure matters toward the data end. StraightDP exploits this heterogeneity end to end. A small budget share releases whitened class-conditional moments once, to be distilled into the weights or injected at sampling time. The rest is spent by pre-declared DP-SGD toward the data end, beyond the moments' reach. At $\varepsilon=1$ on MNIST, the released moments alone already attain $0.76$ downstream accuracy with prototype-like samples and an FID of $237$, and uniform DP-SGD attains $0.21$. The pipeline built on the release reaches $0.81$ accuracy at FID $56$ in a public latent space. Constraining per-token stream norms of the multimodal backbone leaves the pretraining loss unchanged yet improves downstream accuracy in the extreme-noise pixel-space regime, and its accuracy effect becomes monotonically more favorable as privacy strengthens. The released moments also port to frozen SD3-medium, where sampling-time injection beats DP-LoRA training at a fraction of the budget.

</details>


### [244] [Curriculum Matters: Data-Efficient Relational PFN Pretraining with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29120)
*Mohammad Sadeq Abolhasani,Viswanath Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了关系型先验数据拟合网络（PFN）的预训练策略，发现课程设计和合成数据多样性比特定的关系生成器或原始数据规模更重要。通过使用PluRel作为单一合成数据源，单表渐进式课程仅用约13,300张合成表即达到0.703 ROC-AUC，关系型课程仅用约5,500个数据库恢复RDB-PFN 88%的性能，且单表模型无需关系适应即可在关系基准上达到0.631。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究关系型PFN预训练范式中三个核心问题：(1) 不同结构的合成生成器（PluRel）能否替代RDB-PFN的先验；(2) 合成数据的呈现顺序对下游性能的影响；(3) PFN在引入关系数据前能从单表预训练中获得多少关系推理能力。

Method: 以PluRel为唯一合成数据源，设计单表渐进式课程（从7列逐步扩展至17列），与全量一次性训练对比；设计关系型课程从头训练；将单表课程模型直接在关系基准上评估，无需关系适应。

Result: (i) 单表渐进课程用约13,300张合成表在23任务表格基准上达0.703 ROC-AUC，全量训练仅0.541；(ii) 关系课程用约5,500个PluRel数据库在19任务RelBench/4DBInfer基准上达0.638 ROC-AUC，恢复RDB-PFN报告性能的88%，数据量减少约220倍；(iii) 单表课程模型直接在关系基准上评估达0.631，接近专用关系管线。

Conclusion: 课程设计和合成数据多样性对关系型PFN预训练的影响可能超过特定关系生成器或原始数据规模，为高效预训练策略提供了新方向。

Abstract: Relational Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) such as RDB-PFN approximate Bayesian inference over multi-table relational databases by pretraining on millions of synthetic tasks. We investigate three intertwined questions about this paradigm. First, can a structurally different synthetic generator PluRel substitute for RDB-PFN's prior? Second, how much does the order in which synthetic data is presented to the PFN affect downstream performance? Third, how much relational reasoning can a PFN acquire from single-table synthetic pretraining alone, before any relational data is introduced? Using PluRel as the sole synthetic data source across all experiments, we find: (i) a progressive single-table curriculum that gradually widens schema complexity from 7 to 17 columns reaches 0.703 average ROC-AUC on the 23-task tabular benchmark using only approximately 13,300 synthetic tables (approximately 45x fewer single-table datasets than RDB-PFN's reported warm-up recipe), while the same data trained all-at-once collapses to 0.541 ROC-AUC; (ii) a relational curriculum trained from scratch on only approximately 5,500 PluRel databases reaches 0.638 average ROC-AUC on the 19-task RelBench/4DBInfer benchmark, recovering 88% of RDB-PFN's reported performance with approximately 220x less relational synthetic data; and (iii) the single-table curriculum model, evaluated directly on the relational benchmark without any relational adaptation, achieves 0.631, nearly matching the dedicated relational pipeline. Together, these findings suggest that curriculum design and synthetic data diversity may matter more for relational PFN pretraining than the specific relational generator or raw synthetic scale alone.

</details>


### [245] [PluRel-to-RDB-PFN: Schema-Guided Synthetic Relational Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29129)
*Mohammad Sadeq Abolhasani,Viswanath Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用外部合成数据库生成器PluRel，仅用~33K任务（比原方案少55倍）且无需单表预热，在适当课程设计下，RDB-PFN恢复87.6%的原始性能，证明早期接触真实世界模式尤为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系基础模型（RFM）的预训练将数据生成与模型训练流程紧密耦合，缺乏灵活性。本文探索通用合成关系数据库生成器PluRel能否作为独立的外部数据源，为关系上下文学习器RDB-PFN提供有效的预训练信号。

Method: 构建将PluRel生成数据库转换为RDB-PFN训练格式的流水线；设计三种课程策略——SCHEMA-GUIDED FIRST（先真实模式后全合成）、FULLY SYNTHETIC（全程多样合成模式）、SCHEMA-GUIDED LAST（先全合成后真实模式）；仅使用约5,500个关系数据库（约33K任务），在19个真实基准任务上评估不同上下文长度（64-shot和1024-shot）下的ROC-AUC。

Result: 最佳课程（SCHEMA-GUIDED FIRST）在1024-shot下平均ROC-AUC达0.6346，恢复原RDB-PFN性能（0.7245）的87.6%；在64-shot下差距缩小至93.8%（0.6116 vs. 0.6517）。早期接触真实模式远优于后期模式适应。

Conclusion: 外部合成生成器配合适当课程设计可为RFM提供有效预训练信号，且课程中真实世界模式的引入时机至关重要——前置真实模式显著优于后置。

Abstract: Relational Foundation Models (RFMs) require large-scale synthetic relational databases for pretraining, but existing approaches tightly couple data generation with the model training pipeline. We study whether PluRel, a general-purpose synthetic relational database generator, can serve as an external data source for RDB-PFN, a relational in-context learner originally pretrained with a 600K-task single-table warm-up followed by an approximately 1.8M-task adaptation stage.
  We build a conversion pipeline that maps PluRel-generated databases, including externally constructed binary prediction tasks, into the RDB-PFN training format and evaluate three curriculum strategies: SCHEMA-GUIDED FIRST (real-world schema then fully synthetic), FULLY SYNTHETIC (diverse synthetic schemas throughout), and SCHEMA-GUIDED LAST (fully synthetic then real-world schema). Using only approximately 5,500 relational databases (approximately 33K tasks), roughly 55x fewer tasks than the original protocol, and no single-table warm-up, our best curriculum (SCHEMA-GUIDED FIRST) achieves 0.6346 average ROC-AUC across 19 real benchmark tasks at 1024-shot context, recovering 87.6% of the published RDB-PFN performance (0.7245). At 64-shot context, the gap narrows to 93.8% (0.6116 vs. 0.6517). Our results demonstrate that external synthetic generators can provide useful pretraining signals for RFMs when combined with appropriate curriculum design and that exposure to a real-world schema early in training is substantially more effective than late-stage schema adaptation.

</details>


### [246] [HERO: History-Enriched Rollout Training for Long-Horizon Autoregressive Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29135)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Shuxu Chen,Haifan Meng,Yi Lu,Zhihan Lyu,Fan Mo,Wei Dong,Yang Yang,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERO 提出一种历史增强的 rollout 训练策略，通过引入基于模型优化历史的相对监督信号（而非仅依赖绝对轨迹误差），构造 margin-based 目标函数来重加权梯度，在九个 PDE 基准上显著提升了自回归预测的长期精度、稳定 rollout 长度及分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经常微分算子训练中，自回归 rollout 会将每一步预测误差反馈为输入，导致局部误差累积；当前 rollout 训练策略仅以绝对轨迹误差为监督，无法告知模型是否克服了优化早期出现的长期预测失败行为，缺乏对失败模式的针对性矫正。

Method: HERO 方法核心包括：(1) 维护一个周期性刷新的滞后算子，从滞后算子、当前模型和扰动输入生成多个候选 rollout；(2) 按 rollout 误差、谱差异、能量漂移和误差增长率等指标对候选 rollout 排序，选取最强失败轨迹作为参考基线；(3) 将该参考基线纳入 margin-based 目标函数，作为固定比较基准，产生有界的、样本依赖的地面真值 rollout 梯度重加权（而非独立梯度方向），并对此进行了理论分析。

Result: 在 9 个 PDE 基准上，使用谱方法和注意力机制骨干网络进行实验，HERO 在长期预测精度、稳定 rollout 长度和分布外鲁棒性方面均取得一致提升，且不增加推理成本。

Conclusion: 历史增强的相对监督信号对稳定长时域自回归预测是有效的，为神经常微分算子的 rollout 训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Neural operators provide fast surrogates for time-dependent partial differential equations (PDEs) by applying a learned evolution operator recursively to its own predictions, but this autoregressive rollout feeds every prediction error back as input, so local errors accumulate. Existing rollout-training strategies reduce the mismatch between training inputs and self-generated states, yet their supervision still measures only the absolute discrepancy from the ground-truth trajectory. Such supervision is therefore uninformative about whether the operator has overcome the long-horizon failure behaviors it exhibited earlier during optimization. We propose history-enriched rollout training (HERO), which augments conventional absolute trajectory supervision with relative supervision derived from the model's optimization history. HERO ranks detached candidate rollouts from a periodically refreshed lagged operator, the current model, and a perturbed input by rollout error, spectral discrepancy, energy drift, and error growth, and selects the strongest failure trajectory as reference. This reference enters a margin-based objective as a fixed comparison baseline, inducing a bounded, sample-dependent reweighting of the ground-truth rollout gradient rather than an independent gradient direction, which we further analyze theoretically. Experiments on nine PDE benchmarks with spectral and attention-based backbones show that HERO consistently improves long-horizon accuracy, stable rollout length, and out-of-distribution robustness at no inference-time cost. These results indicate that history-enriched relative supervision is effective for stabilizing long-horizon autoregressive prediction.

</details>


### [247] [Implicit Machine Learning Force Fields Accelerate Molecular Dynamics Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29158)
*Johannes Maeß,Leon Werner,J. Thorben Frank,Winfried Ripken,Martin Michajlow,Joshua Futterer,Klaus-Robert Müller,Stefan Chmiela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式机器学习力场(I-MLFF)，用自洽不动点方程替代显式神经网络层堆叠，利用分子模拟中连续时间步的中间表示复用实现热启动，在保持深层网络表达能力的同时将计算和内存开销降至浅层单层网络水平，在多种图神经网络架构上实现了2-5倍的效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MLFF将力预测与轨迹积分分开考虑，每个时间步都需从头计算深层网络，导致计算和内存开销巨大。作者希望打破这种分离设计，利用分子动力学模拟的时间连续性，通过跨时间步复用中间表示来提升效率，同时保持全原子分辨率和原始积分步长。

Method: 提出I-MLFF，将深层神经网络的显式层堆叠替换为自洽不动点方程。核心思想是在分子动力学模拟中，相邻时间步的原子构型高度相关，因此可将上一时间步收敛后的中间表示作为当前步的初始猜测，实现不动点迭代的"热启动"，大幅减少迭代次数。该方法在三种主流图神经网络架构上进行了验证：不变性架构、等变笛卡尔张量架构和SO(3)-等变球张量架构。

Result: 在三类主要GNN架构上均实现2-5倍的计算和内存占用缩减，同时保持全原子分辨率和原始积分步长，未引入任何空间或时间粗粒化。

Conclusion: I-MLFF推进了量子力学精度分子模拟的规模化前沿，使得在固定GPU内存和计算预算下能够模拟更长的轨迹和更大的原子体系，为生物分子和材料系统研究开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: We introduce implicit machine learning force fields (I-MLFFs), which replace explicit stacks of neural network layers with self-consistent fixed-point equations. In molecular simulations, this formulation enables intermediate representations to be reused across successive timesteps, thereby warm-starting force evaluation. The resulting models effectively combine the computational footprint of a shallow, single-layer MLFF with the representational capacity and accuracy of a deep neural network. Our approach unlocks architecture-agnostic efficiency gains that are inaccessible when force prediction and trajectory integration are considered separately. We demonstrate this across three major classes of graph neural networks: invariant, equivariant Cartesian tensor, and SO(3)-equivariant spherical-tensor architectures. Each yields a two- to five-fold reduction in compute and memory footprint. Crucially, these gains are achieved while retaining full atomistic resolution and the original integration timestep, avoiding spatial or temporal coarse graining. Our contribution therefore advances the scaling frontier of quantum-mechanically faithful molecular simulation, enabling longer trajectories and larger atomistic systems within fixed GPU memory and compute budgets, and thereby opening access to new insights across biomolecular and material systems.

</details>


### [248] [MBDiff: Multi-view Behavior-aware Diffusion Model for Probabilistic Utility Data Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29177)
*Rongchao Xu,Lin Jiang,Dahai Yu,Ximiao Li,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MBDiff 是一种多视角行为感知扩散模型，用于公共事业数据的概率缺失值填补。它从全局、局部和实例级三个视角提取用户行为特征，并通过行为感知的条件扩散模型实现高效、准确的缺失值填补，在电力和水务数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共事业数据（电、水、气）因传感器故障和传输问题常存在大量缺失值，严重影响账单准确性、需求预测和供应管理。现有方法大多基于聚合数据集训练，忽略了丰富的用户行为信息，而用户行为信息对提升填补精度具有潜在价值。然而，从长期、多样化且不完整的公用数据中学习全面的用户行为，以及利用行为信息指导填补（因相关性间接而困难），是两大核心挑战。

Method: 提出 MBDiff 模型，包含两个关键组件：(i) 多视角用户行为提取模块，从全局视图、局部视图和实例级视图三个角度学习全面的用户行为表征；(ii) 行为感知条件扩散模型，由参考选择模块和条件注意力去噪网络组成，以计算高效的方式实现概率填补。

Result: 与美国佛罗里达州最大的市政公用事业供应商合作，在实际数据集上评估 MBDiff。实验结果表明，MBDiff 显著优于现有最先进基线方法：在电力数据集上块缺失填补提升 7.04%，在水务数据集上提升 29.1%。

Conclusion: MBDiff 通过多视角用户行为提取与行为感知条件扩散模型，有效解决了公共事业数据缺失值填补问题，尤其在用户行为信息利用方面取得了突破，填补精度大幅领先现有方法，具有重要的实际应用价值。
